[[sep-vplan-area-6]]
==== 6. eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

[[sep_efuse_image_test]]
===== 6.1 `sep_efuse_image_test`

*Phase*: 1

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu`, real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_image_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip

*Objective*: Proves the write-one-to-set programming path into one-time-programmable memory: bits that are burned read back as one directly from the array before any re-sense, and after a cold re-sense the shadow equals the original image plus exactly the bits that were burned.

*Test Coverage*: OTP write-one-to-set program, array readback, and post-resense shadow. Functional coverage: `sep_lc_feat_ctrl_profile_cg.cp_presense_feat[invalid_profile_zero]` and `sep_boot_milestone_cg.cp_sense_surface[status1_port1]`. The owner window opens right after the reset release and closes after CHK-SENSE-SHADOW. The `sep_fuse_sense_done` field of the status read that the second bin names is logged (`OBS-SENSE-STATUS-DONE`) and not graded. A legal outcome is held as pending and samples only on the rise of a pass label that the leaf raises after the owning check passed (CHK-PRE-SENSE-FAIL-CLOSED for the first bin, CHK-SENSE-SHADOW for the second). The window closes on the first FAIL, so a failing run holds no sample.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Stage an image, release reset, open the owner window, and AXI-read `FEAT_CTRL` while `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` is still low.
. Wait for sense-done and read `SEP_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS` and every shadow word; close the owner window after the grade. AXI-read `FEAT_CTRL` equals the image golden and is not 0.
. Burn a set of bits through the write-one-to-set path. The draw excludes bits 0 and 1 of the last `CHIPLET_UID` word, which step 5 programs.
. Read the burned bits directly out of the array before re-sensing.
. Read the last `CHIPLET_UID` word directly, then program its bit 0 and then its bit 1.
. Cold reset, re-sense, and compare the shadow against image-plus-burned-bits.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-PRE-SENSE-FAIL-CLOSED
|AXI `FEAT_CTRL` is 0 while `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` is still low. The read must complete before sense-done, or the closed side was not seen.
|CHK-SENSE-SHADOW
|After sense-done every shadow word equals the image golden, the scoreboard judged exactly as many words as the leaf read, and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` reads 1 after the status read. The `sep_fuse_sense_done` field of `SEP_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS` and its reserved bits have no stated value after sense-done, so they are logged as `OBS-SENSE-STATUS-DONE` and `OBS-SENSE-STATUS-RSVD` and not graded. A wrong shadow word or a port that stays low fails. Evidence: `CHK-SENSE-SHADOW PASS: sep_fuse_sense_done_o=1 shadow judged=<n>/<n> reads`.
|CHK-PRE-SENSE-OPEN
|After sense-done the same `FEAT_CTRL` equals the image golden and is not 0, so the guard actually opened.
|CHK-OTP-DIRECT
|The first and last burned bits read back as one directly from the array, before any re-sense, and their whole words match the golden. The remaining burned bits are covered only after the re-sense, by CHK-W1S-PERSIST.
|CHK-W1S-NOCLOBBER
|Before step 5 a direct read of the word equals the golden whole word with both bits 0 (the positive probe: no random burn drew them). After the second bit of step 5 is programmed into the word that holds the first, a direct read of that word shows both bits as one and equals the golden whole word, so the second program reaches its own bit, leaves the first set, and sets no other bit. The PASS line logs the word and its golden. The overwrite failure mode is not representable on this bench (see *Model honesty*).
|CHK-W1S-PERSIST
|After cold re-sense the shadow equals the original image plus exactly the burned bits — all twelve, the ten random ones and the two from step 5. The `sep_fuse_sense_done` field and the reserved bits 31:1 of `SEP_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS` have no stated value after the re-sense (`regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`) and are logged as `OBS-SENSE-STATUS-DONE` and `OBS-SENSE-STATUS-RSVD`, not graded.
|CHK-NONVAC
|The comparison covered the whole image, not a sample.
|OBS-SENSE-STATUS-DONE
|A logged line after each status read (the one of CHK-SENSE-SHADOW and the one of CHK-W1S-PERSIST), not a graded check: the `sep_fuse_sense_done` field and the whole status word. Evidence: `OBS-SENSE-STATUS-DONE: <CHK-SENSE-SHADOW\|CHK-W1S-PERSIST> sep_fuse_sense_done field=0x<f> SEP_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS=0x<v>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Evidence*: CHK-NONVAC is the scope of the compares, not a record of its own: `sep_efuse_shadow_check_seq` walks every word of every field `SepEfuseImage.check_fields()` returns, and the shared scoreboard asserts a non-zero value-comparison count, so a sampled or empty compare fails.

*Model honesty*: the OTP array behind the fuse-command interface is a
simulation-only behavioural bank, not an OTP macro. Two properties this entry
reads as proven are properties of that model rather than of hardware:
write-one-to-set additivity is OR-only by construction in the generated model, so
the "bank that overwrites instead of ORs" failure mode cannot be
represented; and persistence across cold reset comes from the
model having no reset on its storage. What the checkers do prove against real
logic is that each program reached its addressed bit through the real programming
path, and that the sense FSM re-reads the array after reset and the shadow tracks
it.

*Not claimed*: the `final_sec_disable` half of
`fuse_sense_done_i || final_sec_disable` — exposing the shadow before
sense-done — is not exercised here. Only the `fuse_sense_done_i` half of that
guard is proven.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_km_axil_cpu_mux_coexist_test]]
===== 6.2 `sep_efuse_km_axil_cpu_mux_coexist_test`

*Phase*: 1

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `cpu`, real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_km_axil_cpu_mux_coexist_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip, CPU FW, `tb/sep_outbound_mbx.sv` (console + verdict mailbox; OKAY / zero read data for any address)

*Objective*: Proves that the eFuse AXI-Lite mux serves the CPU and the Key Manager at the same time: the chiplet identifier reads its known value on every CPU iteration, the Key Manager's ownership tags stay intact, and its register payload keeps advancing.

*Test Coverage*: CPU and Key Manager contend on the eFuse AXI-Lite mux.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Real-sense a fuse image and wait for sense-done.
. Release the Key Manager from software reset.
. Handshake over the mailbox — receive the ready token, send the go token.
. Loop exactly 512 times: read the chiplet identifier, sample the Key Manager's register payload and ownership tags, publish a counter to cold scratch.
. Stop on the done token.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-HANDSHAKE
|Both processors are alive and synchronised over the mailbox.
|CHK-UID-STABLE
|The chiplet identifier reads the same known value on every iteration; no bad reads.
|CHK-TAG-INTACT
|The Key Manager's ownership tags keep their expected values throughout.
|CHK-PROGRESS
|The Key Manager's register payload advances and never moves backwards, so the contention window was real rather than an idle loop.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Evidence*: one aggregate record, `CHK-COEXIST` (`cocotb/tests/km/sep_efuse_km_axil_cpu_mux_coexist_test.py`), covers CHK-HANDSHAKE (the READY marker), CHK-PROGRESS (payload changes under contention), CHK-UID-STABLE (`bad_uid == 0`) and CHK-TAG-INTACT (`bad_tag == 0`).

*Anti-vacuity*: this entry is double-gated and the two gates are independent. The
Key Manager firmware counts its own errors and converts them into the pass/fail
magic the boot scoreboard asserts on; separately the host reads the six values
the firmware published to cold scratch and asserts each one in its own statement,
so a fake pass would have to survive both verdicts. Progress is held to a floor
rather than merely non-zero: the host requires the Key Manager's payload to have
advanced at least 256 times across the 512 host iterations, so a multiplexer that
starved it to a handful of writes fails.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test]]
===== 6.3 `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test`

*Phase*: 1

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu`, real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip

*Objective*: Walks the device through four lifecycle states — one preloaded, three programmed for real into one-time-programmable memory — and proves each reaches the lifecycle controller and produces the expected feature-control decode.

*Test Coverage*: Four lifecycle states via OTP program and resense; feature-control decode; `dft_secure` Case 3 vs the TEST_EN strap. Functional coverage: `cp_stm_event_x_strap` (`sense_done_strap0`, `sense_done_strap1`) and `cp_transition[test_dev_to_prod_prog]`. The owner window opens before the first sense and closes after the last state check of the chain. Each sense is followed by one state check before the next sense. The bins sample only on the rise of a pass label that the leaf raises after every check of that state has passed. The window closes on the first FAIL.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Start in the initial test state; check the lifecycle decode against the golden model.
. Program the production state through the ungated write-one-to-set bit, handling injected physical failures by clearing status and retrying; cold reset and re-sense.
. Pin the two disable overrides to distinct non-zero vectors so each decoded feature-control word is a different non-trivial value; check the state reached the controller and that feature control matches the golden model.
. Repeat for the two token-gated return-material-authorisation states.
. After the initial TEST_DEV check with TEST_EN=0, raise the strap, re-sense, and prove `secure_tm_o` follows the strap while `FEAT_CTRL` stays on the same golden. Score `dbg_disable.dft_secure` on both legs against Case 3 (not the strap). The strap is then lowered again; the rest of the walk runs at `secure_tm=0`.
. Inject a broken LC_STATE pair at the LCC decoder input; prove fail-closed FEAT_CTRL=0; release the inject and log the state two clocks later.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-LC-INPUT
|The state sourced from fuses reaches the lifecycle controller, for each of the four states — with no forced or deposited value anywhere on the path. The state read back is also checked for legal encoding and a legal forward transition, using the code the DUT returned rather than the code the test programmed.
|CHK-GOLDEN
|The feature-control output matches the golden decode at each of the four states, at the pinned override vectors, with demote at its reset value. The four-state walk runs at `secure_tm=0`; TEST_DEV is additionally re-checked at `secure_tm=1`, and both legs use the same golden because SECURE_TM does not qualify feature control. Demote is not written here — the demoted branches are covered by `sep_lcc_uvm_inbound_filter_gating_test`. See <<lcc-golden-from-spec>>.
|CHK-OTP-RETRY
|An injected physical program failure is observed, cleared and retried to success.
|CHK-NONVAC
|The pinned overrides break all-ones symmetry: the four expected feature-control words are mutually distinct, so a decode that ignored its inputs would differ from the golden at three of the four states.
|CHK-SECURE-TM
|The TEST_EN strap is driven frontdoor. After the initial TEST_DEV sense with the strap at 0, `secure_tm_o` is 0 and `FEAT_CTRL` matches the TEST_DEV golden. After the strap is raised and the image is re-sensed, `secure_tm_o` is 1 and `FEAT_CTRL` still matches that same golden. SECURE_TM does not qualify feature control.
|CHK-DFT-SECURE
|`dbg_disable.dft_secure` matches Case 3 of the DTP ladder (`SIP_DBG & CHIPLET_DBG & SEP_DBG`, inverted) for the same `FEAT_CTRL` each stitch check just verified, at both strap polarities and at every later state of the walk. The TEST_EN strap is not a term: a formula of `!secure_tm` fails when Case 3 and the strap disagree (TEST_DEV with the pinned DIS vectors keeps Case 3 closed at `secure_tm=1`; `RMA_CHIPLET` opens Case 3 at `secure_tm=0`). Dump refusal on the secure chain is a DFT command-class rule, not this SIB.
|CHK-SECRET-BLANK
|KM-secret disconnect, proven as a transition on one image rather than as a single observation. With the strap at 0 each of `CHIPLET_UID`, `SIP_UID`, `SYS_UID` and `CLASS_KEY` is present in the sensed shadow; with it raised the same four fields read zero at the sensed-shadow output (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc:24-25`: they read as zero downstream while SECURE_TM is 1; `hw/sys/sep/doc/test_mode.adoc:61-62`: the eFuse controller masks the secret shadow outputs to zero). Each staged value is asserted non-zero first, so a DUT that never sensed a field cannot satisfy that field's disconnected half, and a mask that is not zero fails the second half.
|CHK-SECURE-TM-PROG-BLOCK
|While `secure_tm` is asserted, LC_STATE updates from SEP are blocked (`hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc`, "secure_tm and the Secure Scan Chain"). Two program attempts of LC_STATE bit 96 do not succeed, and after the strap drops and the bank is sensed again the shadow equals the image without that bit. Two attempts are needed because the one-shot program-fail injection can fail only the first. No spec sentence states the `PROGRAM_CTRL` status of a blocked program, so `PROGRAM_DONE` and `PROGRAM_ERR` are logged (`OBS-STM-PROG-BLOCK-STATUS`) and not graded.
|CHK-SIGINT
|A force gated by the tb_top `lc_sigint_inject_i` port breaks the LC_STATE pair at the LCC decoder input. Observation is the DUT `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` probe plus an AXI `FEAT_CTRL` readback of 0 (fail-closed, `SEC_DIS` observed 0). Nothing is graded after the inject falls: no document states a release of `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`.
|CHK-SECURE-TM-OFF / CHK-SECURE-TM-ON
|The two legs of the strap walk, taken as a transition on one image: with the TEST_EN strap at 0, `secure_tm_o` is 0; after the strap is raised and the image is re-sensed, `secure_tm_o` is 1. `FEAT_CTRL` equals the same TEST_DEV golden on both legs. At every state of the walk (TEST_DEV three times, PROD, RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET), `secure_tm_o` equals the strap driven before that sense, and equals 0 after the strap is dropped, with SEC_DIS 0. Each state logs a PASS line with `secure_tm_o`, the strap, SEC_DIS and `FEAT_CTRL`.
|OBS-LC-SIGINT-RELEASE
|A logged line, not a graded check: two clocks after the inject falls, `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` (`lcc_sigint_err_probe_o`), the `FEAT_CTRL` readback and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. No document states the release.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

[[lcc-golden-from-spec]]
[NOTE]
====
The lifecycle golden is derived from the specification, not from the RTL.

`cocotb/env/sep_lcc_golden.py` is transcribed from `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (the
per-LC-state feature-control profile). Under that decode a PROD demotion relaxes its
debug group to `~(SIP_DIS | SYS_DIS)`, Function always takes both vectors, and
`DEMOTE_1` / `DEMOTE_2` act independently on `[23:0]` and `[47:24]`. The import-time
KAT set includes a `DEMOTE_2`-only vector, a both-demoted vector, and a PROD vector
with a `DBG_1` bit disabled.

`sep_efuse_image.py` derives the fuse-field table from the generated map and self-checks
that the result tiles all 256 words. The stitch sequence, image test, address-map probe,
and `fw/drivers/sep_efuse.h` use the same derived table.
====

'''''

[[sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test]]
===== 6.4 `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test`

*Phase*: 1

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `cpu`, real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip, CPU FW, `tb/sep_outbound_mbx.sv` (console + verdict mailbox; OKAY / zero read data for any address)

*Objective*: The debug-port counterpart to the two-processor coexistence test.

*Test Coverage*: JTAG AXI-Lite and the CPU coexist on eFuse.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Real-sense a production image; wait for sense-done; publish CPU-ready.
. CPU: seed resetless `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I[0]` with a distinct nonzero value and read it back before publishing CPU-ready. Then run a free-running loop scoring two reads per pass -- that token word must retain the CPU seed, and the eFuse interface STATUS must keep `efuse_sense_done` set -- while publishing a counter and an error count to cold scratch. The CPU issues no eFuse writes during the concurrent window, so only the read channel is contended.
. Host, concurrently: a write that seeds `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I[1]`, then debug-port register rounds of 3 AXI-Lite operations each (a token-1 read, a token-3 write and its readback), at least 16 and at most 512, until the CPU counter has advanced; all expected to succeed.
. Host: attempt a debug-port read of the protected shadow window; expect an error response. Log its read data (`OBS-JTAG-DENY-RDATA`).
. Host: repeat a register read after the denial; expect success.
. Read the CPU's counter to confirm it advanced during the burst.
. Host: read `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]` and `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH`; expect OKAY, and the token word reads back its seed. A sample of the token-block allow class in PROD. Then read `TOKEN_EOP` and log its response and read data (`OBS-JTAG-TOKEN-EOP-READ`).
. Host: read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`; expect an error response. Log its read data (`OBS-JTAG-DENY-RDATA`).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-JTAG-MMR
|Every debug-port register round (at least 16, until the CPU counter advances) completes successfully through the multiplexer.
|CHK-JTAG-DENY
|The protected shadow window is denied with an error response (SLVERR or DECERR), not OKAY and not a timeout. For SEP, `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` (JTAG Access Control Based on LC_STATE) states that accesses outside the MMR space "are blocked with an error response"; it names neither the response code nor the read data. The `0xbadcab1e` data sentence there belongs to the SMC policy, so `OBS-JTAG-DENY-RDATA` logs the read data and grades nothing.
|CHK-JTAG-ALLOW
|The MMR window still works after the denial. The shadow map and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` answer OKAY under TEST_DEV in `sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test`, so those two windows are gated per lifecycle state rather than denied on every image.
|CHK-CPU-MMR
|The CPU writes a distinct nonzero seed to resetless `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I[0]`, reads it back before the concurrent window, and requires that exact value throughout with zero errors. JTAG operates on different token words with different payloads, so a zeroed CPU path or cross-port leakage fails.
|CHK-COEXIST
|The CPU made real progress during the debug-port burst — the overlap was genuine.
|CHK-JTAG-TOKEN-BLOCK
|In PROD, JTAG reads of `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]` and `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` return OKAY, and `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]` reads back its seed. A sample of the `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` token-block allow class, not every `TOKEN_I` word and MATCH register. `CHK-JTAG-MMR` already walks RMA `TOKEN_I`. Every field of `TOKEN_EOP` is `sw = w` (`hw/ip/efuse/regs/efuse_mmr.rdl`) and no text states the response of a read of it, so `OBS-JTAG-TOKEN-EOP-READ` logs that read and grades nothing.
|CHK-JTAG-IFACE-DENY
|In PROD, a JTAG read of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` completes with an error response (SLVERR or DECERR), as for CHK-JTAG-DENY. `OBS-JTAG-DENY-RDATA` logs the read data. Shadow deny is CHK-JTAG-DENY; this is the program/read interface.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Evidence*: the firmware publishes CPU-ready to cold scratch only after real fuse sense completes and the CPU token seed written to the first eFuse MMR word reads back; otherwise it returns without publishing it, and the host fails the leaf at its CPU-ready wait. The firmware console is not in the leaf log, so these preconditions show as that wait; `CHK-CPU-MMR` also fails if the seed is lost.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test]]
===== 6.5 `sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test`

*Phase*: 1

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu`, real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (JTAG AXI-Lite master)

*Objective*: Prove the JTAG lifecycle gate denies the shadow map and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` by lifecycle state, not always. `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test` requires a refusal (SLVERR or DECERR) on both windows in PROD; this leaf requires OKAY on the same two windows in TEST_DEV, which is not a restricted state.

*Test Coverage*: JTAG reaches the shadow map and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` when `LC_STATE` is TEST_DEV.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Real-sense a TEST_DEV image. TEST_DEV is not PROD and not RMA_SIP, so the JTAG lifecycle gate does not steer those addresses at the error slave. The shared backdoor compare grades the sensed shadow against the image (CHK-SENSE-BACKDOOR).
. Read the shadow map base and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` on the JTAG AXI-Lite port. Both complete OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-SENSE-BACKDOOR
|After sense done, every sensed shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` equals the staged TEST_DEV image (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`: the fuse bank senses into the shadow registers), and the compare spans the whole image. A sense that loads another state, or no state, fails, so the OKAY reads below are made at TEST_DEV. The leaf names this shared record in `required_evidence`, so a missing record fails it. Evidence: `CHK-SENSE-BACKDOOR PASS words=<n> errors=0 secure_tm=0` (`cocotb/seq_lib/sep_efuse_backdoor_check.py`).
|CHK-JTAG-WINDOW-ALLOW
|In TEST_DEV, a JTAG read of the shadow map and a JTAG read of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` both complete OKAY. The PROD refusal (SLVERR or DECERR) for the same two windows is `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

'''''

[[sep_lcc_uvm_inbound_filter_gating_test]]
===== 6.6 `sep_lcc_uvm_inbound_filter_gating_test`

*Phase*: 1

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu`, real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_lcc_uvm_inbound_filter_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip

*Objective*: Proves that the lifecycle security state actually gates the external inbound port — the security-boundary property, not just the register that describes it.

*Test Coverage*: Lifecycle state gates the SMN-inbound port.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Real-sense a production image with pinned disable overrides.
. Read the feature-control register over the control bus and compare all 64 bits against golden; confirm the debug bit is clear.
. Attempt the same read from the external port; require a decode error.
. Write and read back the demote register over the control bus.
. Re-read feature-control; compare against golden; confirm that the debug bit is set.
. Repeat the external read; require success and the expected value.
. Still with the filter bypassed, write and read back a Scratch SRAM word from the external port, then read and write the first and last word of each unit the connectivity matrix does not connect to the System Interface initiator; require a decode error each time.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-PROD-FEAT
|In the production state the full feature-control value matches golden and the debug bit is clear.
|CHK-PROD-BLOCK
|The external read is refused with a specific decode error, not a timeout.
|CHK-DEMOTE-INDEP
|`DEMOTE_2` alone opens DBG_2 `[47:24]` and leaves DBG_1 `[23:0]` closed, so the two demote registers act per-group rather than sharing a priority. `sep_debug` is bit 0, inside DBG_1, so it must still read 0 — and the external port must still be blocked, which is a second, independent consequence of the same property. `DEMOTE_2` is driven first: the demote field is write-once-set and cannot be cleared, so this is the only order in which this DUT can show one group opening without the other.
|CHK-DEMOTE
|The demote register accepts a write and reads back.
|CHK-DBG-FEAT
|After demotion the full feature-control value matches the spec-derived golden decode and the debug bit is set. See <<lcc-golden-from-spec>>.
|CHK-DBG-ALLOW
|The external read then succeeds and returns the distinctive value, proving it reached the lifecycle controller.
|CHK-IDENTITY
|An external inbound access tracks the debug bit — refused when clear, served when set. This is a behavioural differential on the external port; the filter's internal bypass signal is not sampled.
|CHK-NONVAC
|Both the blocked and allowed cases were actually observed in this run.
|CHK-DBG-REACH
|With the filter bypassed, an external write and read-back of a Scratch SRAM word answer OKAY with the written value, and the control bus reads the same value. This is the live control for CHK-DBG-UNREACHABLE on the same port and in the same state.
|CHK-DBG-UNREACHABLE
|With the filter bypassed, an external read and an external write of the first and last word of the Boot ROM, Reset Ctrl, ICCM, DCCM, PIC, AP remap and STEE remap regions each answer DECERR (no timeout), and the refused write to `SW_RESET_N` leaves it unchanged on the control bus. The filter is not in the path, so the refusal is the fabric's. The SEP AXI4 connectivity figure of `hw/sys/sep/doc/fabric.adoc` gives the System Interface initiator no connection to CPU TCM, Reset Ctrl or System Periph, and the Boot ROM path serves CPU IFI and LSU only; ICCM, DCCM and the PIC are CPU resources. Unit extents come from the DV-owned table in `env/sep_axi_decode_map.py`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test]]
===== 6.7 `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip

*Objective*: Program × lock on every legal spare field in one invocation.

*Test Coverage*: Program then lock on every legal spare field in one invocation.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Walk `SPARE0` through `SPARE8` in order. For each spare, program one seed-selected bit while unlocked; OTP readback and resense shadow match the independent image golden.
. Program that spare's own `LOCKS_SPARE` bit, then reject a second bit (`PROGRAM_ERR`). OTP and resense golden keep the rejected bit 0.
. Spare `k+1` is programmed only after spare `k` is locked, so a lock bit wired past its own slot fails the next spare's unlocked-program cell.
. The seed selects only the bit offsets inside each spare. Slot coverage does not depend on reseed.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-UNLOCK-PROGRAM
|An unlocked spare bit programs and reads back in OTP and after resense. Logged once per spare.
|CHK-LOCK-REJECT
|A write-locked spare rejects a later program; OTP and the post-resense shadow match the independent golden (lock bit set, rejected bit 0). Logged once per spare.
|CHK-SPARE-LOCK-SLOTS
|All nine spares (`SPARE0..SPARE8`, lock slots 32-40 in `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`) ran both cells in one invocation. A lock of spare `k` must not block spare `k+1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test]]
===== 6.8 `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense, `+sep_efuse_prog_fail_count=2`

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip; the OTP bank model drops the first two program writes after reset with no APB error

*Randomization*: RANDCFG — the seed selects one spare field and two distinct bits in it. Every run walks the same four legs.

*Objective*: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL.efuse_program_read_back` (`efuse_interface_ctrl.rdl`) triggers a read back after a program. `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc` names the two commands `FUSE_COMMAND_PROGRAM` and `FUSE_COMMAND_PROGRAM_READ_BACK` (program with verification), and `program_status` is 0 for no error and 1 for an error. A dropped bank write returns no APB error (`hw/ip/efuse/dv/models/README.md`), so only the verified program can report it.

*Test Coverage*: the program command with and without read-back, against a dropped and a kept bank write.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Stage an image with every spare field at 0 at `LC_TEST_DEV`, and sense it.
. Program bit A without read-back (dropped write), then bit B with read-back (dropped write).
. Program bit A again without read-back and bit B again with read-back (both writes kept).
. After each program, read the OTP word through `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and compare it whole against the image golden with the bits that landed.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-NORB-UNVERIFIED
|Program without read-back, write dropped: `program_done` with `program_status` 0, and the OTP word still holds the bit clear. A controller that always verifies fails here.
|CHK-RB-DETECT
|Program with read-back, write dropped: `program_done` with `program_status` 1, and the OTP word still holds the bit clear. A controller that never verifies fails here.
|CHK-NORB-PROGRAM
|Control. Program bit A again without read-back, write kept: `program_status` 0 and the OTP word gains the bit.
|CHK-RB-PROGRAM
|Control. Program bit B again with read-back, write kept: `program_status` 0 and the OTP word gains the bit.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

'''''

[[sep_lcc_demote_feat_ctrl_matrix_test]]
===== 6.9 `sep_lcc_demote_feat_ctrl_matrix_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_lcc_demote_feat_ctrl_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip

*Objective*: Proves that every demote cell produces the `FEAT_CTRL` golden and the `dbg_disable` ladder and gates live external access, and that the demote lock blocks a later demote until `rst_ni`.

*Test Coverage*: Demote matrix vs the feature-control golden, including debug-disable bits. Functional coverage: `sep_dtp_gating_ladder_cg` (`x_path_case`, 20 bins; `x_prod_demote_case3`, 4 bins). Each cell grades `FEAT_CTRL`, CHK-LIVE-GATE, CHK-DBG-DISABLE and CHK-FUSE-DFT-DIS once, inside the owner window. The two coverpoints hold the legal cells of that `FEAT_CTRL` read as pending and sample them only on the rise of a pass label that the leaf raises after every check of the cell passed. The window closes after the cell, or on its first FAIL, which drops the pending cells. The resense, the OTP program and the demote writes run outside the window.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Walk TEST_DEV and PROD at DIS=0 through the four demote cells.
. W1S `sep_fuse_dbg` / `smc_fuse_dbg` and check PROD with both demotes set, so those bits close while Case 2 and Case 3 stay open.
. W1S a seed-pinned DIS pair and walk the four demote cells at PROD. Besides the fuse-dbg bits and `sep_debug`, the pair sets one seed-selected bit from DBG_1 `[23:1]` and one from DBG_2 `[47:24]` (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`), in both `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS`.
. Every cell's inbound probe is OKAY iff `sep_debug=1`, else DECERR.
. Every cell's `dbg_disable` vector matches the DTP gating ladder for that same `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Every cell's `sep_fuse_dft_disable` / `smc_fuse_dft_disable` ports match the compose of that same `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Lock one seed-selected `DEMOTE` at 0; a later demote write is ignored.
. `rst_ni` releases the lock; the same demote write then lands.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-FEAT-CTRL
|`FEAT_CTRL` equals the spec-derived golden for every walked cell. The whole 64-bit value is compared, and the FAIL line carries the cell tag and both values. `sep_debug` equals `FEAT_CTRL[0]`. Each cell runs this check once, inside the owner window.
|CHK-LIVE-GATE
|External access of the LC shadow is OKAY when `sep_debug=1` and DECERR when `sep_debug=0`. Not CSR storage alone.
|CHK-RAND-REP
|Thirteen demote cells ran in one invocation (eight DIS=0, one fuse-dbg compose, four pinned DIS).
|CHK-DEMOTE-LOCK
|With `lock` set and `demote` still 0, a later write of `demote` that also sets one `rsvd` marker bit (drawn with `SepSeededRng`) reads back `demote` 0, `lock` 1 and the marker bit 1, and `FEAT_CTRL` stays at the undemoted golden (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Programming and Locking; `sep_lifecycle_ctrl.rdl`, `DEMOTE_1`/`DEMOTE_2`: `rsvd` is write-one-to-set with no write enable). The marker proves that the ignored write reached the register. Its write response is logged (`OBS-LCC-WR-RESP`), not graded.
|CHK-DEMOTE-LOCK-RESET
|After `rst_ni` the lock is 0 and the same demote write lands.
|CHK-DBG-DISABLE
|All eleven `dbg_disable` bits, compared as one word in the order of the `dbg_disable` fields, match the DTP gating ladder for the same `FEAT_CTRL` the cell just verified. A FAIL names the wrong bits. `dft_secure` is Case 3 and `dfd` is Case 1. Polarity: `doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc`, `dbg_disable_o` (1 = path disabled). Paths: `hw/sys/dtp/doc/jtag.adoc`, section Debug Disable. Cases: `lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Debug and Test Port Path Gating.
|CHK-FUSE-DFT-DIS
|Class-relative. `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` is in its open class when Case 3 AND `sep_fuse_dbg` holds for the `FEAT_CTRL` of the cell, `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o` when Case 2 AND `smc_fuse_dbg` holds, and each is in its closed class otherwise (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, table DTP path feature gates). No document states the level of either class (`smu-sep.adoc` and `port_table.adoc` name the ports only), so the first cell of each class is its control: every cell equals the control of its class, and the open and closed controls of a port differ. TEST_DEV with DIS 0 is open and PROD undemoted with DIS 0 is closed, so every leaf reaches both classes; a leaf that does not fails. The compose cell keeps Case 2 and Case 3 open while bits 2 and 3 are closed, so a port that ignores those bits fails there. A port tied to one level fails at the first cell of its second class.
|OBS-DTP-PORT-LEVEL
|A logged line, not a graded check: the open and closed level of each fuse DFT port, once both are known.
|OBS-LCC-WR-RESP
|A logged line, not a graded check: the response of the ignored `demote` write after `lock`. No document states the response of a write to a locked demote register.
|CTRL-DEMOTE-RB
|Control, not a check: each `demote` write of a cell reads back `demote` 1 and `lock` 0 (`sep_lifecycle_ctrl.rdl`, `DEMOTE_1`/`DEMOTE_2`). At TEST_DEV with DIS 0 the demoted and undemoted goldens are equal, so this line shows that those cells ran with the demote set. A missing demote fails the leaf. Evidence: `CTRL-DEMOTE-RB: <cell tag> group=<1\|2> demote=1 lock=0`.
|CTRL-DEMOTE-CLEAR
|Control, not a check: before the first cell of each walk and after the resense inside the walk, `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` read `demote` 0 and `lock` 0 (`sep_lifecycle_ctrl.rdl`, reset 0; `rst_ni` clears them). At TEST_DEV with DIS 0 the undemoted and demoted goldens are equal, so this line shows that the `d00` and `d00b` cells ran undemoted. A set bit fails the leaf. Evidence: `CTRL-DEMOTE-CLEAR: <cell tag> demote_1=0x<v> demote_2=0x<v>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test]]
===== 6.10 `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip

*Objective*: Proves that a wrong RMA token leaves the lifecycle state unchanged and a legal one advances it, and that a comparator collapse or disagreement forces the error code, sets the sticky fault bit and raises PIC source 40 until reset.

*Test Coverage*: RMA token compare, match, mismatch, and lifecycle update.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Start in PROD; LC shadow and `FEAT_CTRL` match the independent golden.
. Wrong SIP then CHIPLET token: no match code, program `PROGRAM_ERR`, LC unchanged after resense.
. Legal SIP then CHIPLET token: match code. Then a hash of a wrong token on the same channel gives the mismatch code and a program of the `LC_STATE` bit returns `PROGRAM_ERR`. Then the legal token again and the program that lands: LC advances, shadow and `FEAT_CTRL` match the independent golden after resense.
. Common-mode invert of all three RMA_SIP comparators on a valid token: legal mismatch code, no sticky bit, IRQ low.
. Common-mode invert of all three RMA_SIP comparators on a wrong token: legal match code, no sticky bit, IRQ low. `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` documents this fail-open case; see <<known-limitations>>.
. Collapse instance 0, then two-instance disagree: match status `6'b111111`, SIP sticky bit, `sep_internal_interrupts[39]`. Valid retry plus write-0 leave the bit and IRQ asserted.
. At `RMA_SIP`, between the SIP and CHIPLET walks, program one zero bit of a spare field whose write lock is clear in the image (drawn from a separate seeded stream); read the bank bit at `otp_bank_probe_o` before and after and read `STATUS` (`OBS-PROG-RMA`). If `efuse_req_error` is set, clear it and read `STATUS` again (`CTRL-PROG-RMA-ERR-CLEAR`). A bit that landed stays in the golden image of the later cold-reset re-sense.
. A cold-reset re-sense clears `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and releases the interrupt.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-LC-FEAT
|LC shadow and LCC `FEAT_CTRL` match independent goldens after each resense.
|CHK-MISMATCH
|A wrong token does not update LC.
|CHK-MATCH
|A legal token updates LC (PROD → `RMA_SIP_1` → `RMA_CHIP_1`).
|CHK-RANDCFG
|SIP and CHIPLET both walked match and mismatch; tokens from the seed.
|CHK-TOKEN-REHASH-REVOKE
|After a match on a channel, a hash of a wrong token (the legal token with bit 0 flipped) on the same channel gives the mismatch code `0x2a`, and the program of the `LC_STATE` bit that the channel gates returns `PROGRAM_ERR`: a new start clears the validity of the earlier match (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc:52`). A DUT that keeps the program authorization after the wrong-token hash fails. Anchor: the match code of the second hash and the program response. Control: the legal token is presented again and the program that follows lands in the same leaf. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-REHASH-REVOKE PASS: <SIP\|CHIPLET> match, wrong-token hash code=0x2a, program saw_err=1`.
|CHK-COMMON-MODE
|All three comparators flipping a match to a legal mismatch does not set a sticky bit or raise `sep_internal_interrupts[39]`.
|CHK-COMMON-MODE-MATCH
|All three comparators flipping a mismatch to a legal match does not set a sticky bit or raise `sep_internal_interrupts[39]`.
|CHK-COLLAPSE
|Inject `3'b001` forces `6'b111111` and the SIP sticky bit. That encoding also makes instance 0 disagree with the others, so this check does not isolate the collapse detector.
|CHK-DISAGREE
|Two instances that disagree force `6'b111111` and keep the sticky bit.
|CHK-ERROR-CODE
|A redundancy fault writes `6'b111111` on every match-status bit.
|CHK-STICKY
|A valid-token retry and a write-0 leave `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and irq39 asserted (`sw=r` until reset).
|CHK-IRQ-39
|`sep_internal_interrupts[39]` (PIC source 40) asserts with the sticky bit.
|CHK-WHICH-TOKEN
|Each collapse sets only its own fault bit. Of the three fields (SIP bit 0, CHIPLET bit 8, SEC_DISABLE bit 16), FAULT holds exactly SIP after the SIP collapse, SIP and CHIPLET after the CHIPLET collapse, and all three after the SEC_DISABLE collapse. A decode in which one collapse sets another token's bit fails at that collapse.
|CHK-JTAG-FAULT
|After `RMA_CHIP_1`, a JTAG read of `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` matches the AXI read. PROD/RMA_SIP JTAG token-block allow is `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test`, not this check.
|OBS-PROG-RMA
|A logged line, not a graded check: at `RMA_SIP`, between the SIP and CHIPLET walks, one zero bit of a spare field with no write lock is programmed: the bit at `otp_bank_probe_o` before and after, the bank diff, `program_status` and `efuse_req_error`. With no candidate the line carries `candidates=0`. No document states whether `RMA_SIP` accepts a program of an unlocked field.
|CTRL-PROG-RMA-ERR-CLEAR
|When that program leaves `efuse_req_error` set, a write of 1 to `efuse_req_error_clear` clears it and a `STATUS` read returns it at 0 before the CHIPLET walk (`hw/ip/efuse/regs/efuse_interface_ctrl.rdl:24`: the error is cleared by writing 1 to `efuse_req_error_clear`), so a stale guard error cannot cause the CHIPLET mismatch refusal. The line is logged only when the program set the flag. Evidence: `CTRL-PROG-RMA-ERR-CLEAR: efuse_req_error before=1 after_w1c=0 resp_ok=1`.
|CHK-FAULT-RESET
|A cold-reset re-sense clears `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and releases `sep_internal_interrupts[39]`. The earlier sticky checks cannot tell a resettable latch from one that never clears. After the two zeros the detector is re-armed once, so the phase ends on a zero-to-one transition.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test]]
===== 6.11 `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip; `SepRmaTokenMatchSeq` drives the frontdoor token compare

*Objective*: The RMA-SIP token digest latch captures a frontdoor hash, holds it while idle, and stops capturing while the DFT test-enable is asserted -- so a scan-visible sticky register cannot be loaded with a real token digest during test.

*Test Coverage*: Digest latch capture, idle hold, and the test-enable freeze on both the digest and its valid bit.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Sense a `LC_PROD` image carrying the seeded RMA_SIP token.
. Present the correct token over the frontdoor. Watch `digest_latch_en_pre` and `vld_latch_en_pre` -- the pre-gate antecedents -- and require both to activate, then record the sticky digest and its valid bit.
. Hold for two idle cycles: both post-gate latch enables stay low and neither sticky field moves.
. Assert the DFT test-enable through the `token_digest_test_en_inject_i` hook and present a *mutated* token, so a capture would land a different digest than the one recorded.
. Throughout that presentation require the pre-gate antecedents to fire while both post-gate enables stay low and both sticky fields keep their recorded values.
. Release the test-enable.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-DIGEST-LATCH
|A frontdoor hash captures into the sticky digest and its valid bit; two idle cycles leave both unchanged; and while the DFT test-enable is asserted the two `prim_and2` gates hold `digest_latch_en` and `vld_latch_en` low, so a second frontdoor operation -- proven to have run by the pre-gate antecedents -- neither latches its own digest nor clears the valid bit.
|===

The freeze leg is falsifiable in both fields. Without the valid gate, `start_i` clears the sticky valid bit at the start of the second operation; without the digest gate, the mutated token latches a digest that differs from the recorded one. The pre-gate antecedents are the positive control: they show the operation genuinely ran, so the frozen outputs are not frozen because nothing happened.

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: the test-enable is asserted through a testbench force on `u_sha256_rma_sip_token.test_en_i`, which is DFT stimulus with no frontdoor path and is tied low at the SEP boundary. The force targets the enable only -- never the latch enables or the sticky outputs the checker reads.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test]]
===== 6.12 `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu`; omit `+skip_fuse_sense` so the shadow bank is live

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip

*Objective*: Proves the eFuse shadow-register write path on every `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` fuse-field row except `LC_STATE`: set-only rows OR-merge so a bit that is already set cannot be cleared by a write of zero, and SW-writable rows overwrite. Randomization is RANDCFG — every walked row is visited every seed, word index and bit patterns come from the run seed, and the write-policy contract itself is directed.

*Test Coverage*: Every walked fuse-field row follows its specification write policy. The lifecycle nibble stays with the LC W1S leaves.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Stage an OTP image at `LC_TEST_DEV` from the seeded configuration. Bring up and wait for fuse sense.
. Read every walked row through the shadow-check sequence and compare it to the staged image (CHK-SENSE-WALK).
. For each early set-only cell (`SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE`, `BL1_VERSION`, `BL2_VERSION`, `REQUIRED_ALGS`), at the seeded word index: read the sensed word (CHK-SENSE). If it carries a 1, write 0 and read again (CHK-CLEAR-REJECT). Write the seeded set bits and read the OR (CHK-SET-STICKS). Write 0 and read the OR again (CHK-OR-MERGE).
. For each SW-writable row: read 0, write the seeded pattern and read it back, then write 0 and read 0 (CHK-WRITABLE-CONTRAST).
. Last, run the same set-only cell on `LOCKS` word 0, `LOCKS` word 1 and `LOCKS_SPARE`.
. Require every cell to pass, and log the walked counts (CHK-RANDCFG).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-SENSE-WALK
|The shadow-check sequence reads `SEP_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS` and every word of each walked row through the frontdoor, and the shared scoreboard judges every one of those reads against the staged image and rejects none. A read that the scoreboard does not judge, or a word that differs from the image, fails. Evidence: `CHK-SENSE-WALK PASS: rows=<n> judged=<k>/<k> reads`.
|CHK-SENSE
|The staged value is read back. Staged set-only words carry a known 1. Assigned `LOCKS` words sense 0.
|CHK-CLEAR-REJECT
|A frontdoor write of 0 leaves a sensed 1. Assigned `LOCKS` words start at 0, so this cell is not claimed there; `LOCKS_SPARE` claims it on the unassigned half.
|CHK-SET-STICKS
|A write that sets a bit that reads 0 sticks; readback is the OR. Both `LOCKS` words and the assigned `LOCKS_SPARE` half take this cell from zero.
|CHK-OR-MERGE
|A later write of 0 cannot clear the OR. The PASS lines of this check and of CHK-CLEAR-REJECT print `write-0 resp=OKAY`: the write driver fails the leaf on any other response. A write of 0 to a set-only word changes no value by contract, so the readback alone cannot show that the write reached the word.
|CHK-WRITABLE-CONTRAST
|Every SW-writable row overwrites, then a write of 0 clears, so the OR is field-local.
|CHK-RANDCFG
|Forty-one fuse-field rows walked in one invocation: six early set-only cells (`SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE`, `BL1_VERSION`, `BL2_VERSION`, `REQUIRED_ALGS`), three late `LOCK` cells (`LOCKS` word 0, `LOCKS` word 1, `LOCKS_SPARE`), and the 34 SW-writable rows. `LOCK` is counted only when both RDL windows are written. `LC_STATE` is not this leaf. `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` stay on their function-group word so the walk does not OR `sep_debug`, `chiplet_dbg` or `sip_debug` into `FEAT_CTRL`. This test drives the CPU-LSU master, which has no inbound filter.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope*: Scope is the shadow-register write path. Every `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` fuse-field row except `LC_STATE` is walked. Set-only rows OR-merge. SW-writable `true` rows overwrite, then a write of 0 clears. `LOCK` is both RDL windows: `LOCKS` word 0, `LOCKS` word 1, and `LOCKS_SPARE`. Assigned lock slots stay 0 at sense and are written after every other row so they do not write-lock a field this invocation still writes. `LOCKS_SPARE` stages ones only in the unassigned half; the assigned half is set from zero in that same late cell. `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` stay on their function-group word. Program × lock is a different mechanism.

'''''

[[sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s]]
===== 6.13 `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_*` (seven leaves)

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu`, real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_prod_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_prod_demote_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_rma_sip_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_rma_chiplet_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_prod_end_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_prod_end_rma_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_transient_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip, LCC golden

*Objective*: Prove that a frontdoor shadow write which the lifecycle rules hold changes nothing, and log the state each other write returns. This is the second writer of the lifecycle state and the stitch walk does not reach it: that walk programs a one-time-programmable bit and re-senses, which never evaluates this logic. The contract is the specification's three rules — write-one-to-set, a don't-care low bit inside each return-material-authorisation state, and the two hardware-ordered token gates — from which the reachable destinations follow.

*Test Coverage*: Frontdoor `LC_STATE` write-one-to-set, token gates, and the seven leaves: six starting states, with PROD_END walked by two leaves, one per exit.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Sense the leaf's starting state from a real fuse image; require the sensed lifecycle value to be the one requested, and the security-disable override to be inactive so no feature-control check can pass by being forced to all-ones.
. Present the return-material-authorisation tokens the cell needs, and require each to reach a match code before relying on it — an unmatched token would let a gate check pass for the wrong reason. Each match prints `CTRL-TOKEN-MATCH seen: kind=<RMA_SIP|RMA_CHIPLET> code=<c>`; a token that does not match logs `CTRL-MISSING TOKEN-MATCH: kind=<k> code=<c>` and fails the leaf.
. Write the lifecycle word write-one-to-set. Two TT texts disagree on such a write: `sep_efuse_map.rdl` (register `LC_STATE`) gives `lc_state` as `sw = r`, so no write changes the state, and `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (Fuse Fields) gives `LC_STATE` as SW writable set-only, subject to the token and transition checks. A hold cell is a write whose chapter golden is the prior state; both texts give that value, so the cell requires the differentially encoded lifecycle byte `LC_STATE[7:0]` and the controller's decoded feature-control output to match the golden. An advance cell is a write whose chapter golden moves the state; it logs `OBS-LC-WR-ADVANCE cell=<c> prev=<state> wdata=<w> sip_match=<0|1> chiplet_match=<0|1> chapter=<golden> got=<returned> word=<word>` and requires that feature control equals the decode of the returned state (`CHK-LC-FEAT-DECODE`; 0 for an INVALID state). Later cells start from the returned state, so the returned state must be the chapter golden (`CTRL-LC-ADVANCE-STATE seen: cell=<c> prev=<state> expect=<golden> got=<returned>`; any other state logs `CTRL-MISSING LC-ADVANCE-STATE` and fails the leaf, because the hold cells that follow claim the state the golden names). A DUT that follows `sw = r` fails this control and the leaf, as it fails `CTRL-LC-WR-LANDED`; the control never lets a check pass. Each write also carries, in `LC_STATE[31:8]`, one bit that no earlier write or the sensed word set; the readback must show it (`CTRL-LC-WR-LANDED LOG`), so a dropped write fails the leaf. The seed-derived patterns cover `LC_STATE[23:8]` only, so `LC_STATE[31:24]` keeps such a bit for every write cell on every seed. `LC_STATE[31:8]` is logged as an open question (`OBS-LC-UPPER LOG tag=<cell> wrote=<w> prior=<p> read=<r> overwrite=<0|1> or_merge=<0|1>`): the RDL gives `lc_state` and `rsvd` as `sw = r` and `rsvd` as "Unused" (`sep_efuse_map.rdl`, register `LC_STATE`), and the fuse-field table gives `LC_STATE` as SW writable set-only (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields), so the texts disagree on the readback after a write. `CTRL-LC-WR-LANDED` relies on the written `[31:8]` bit reading back; a DUT that follows `sw = r` fails that control and the leaf, so the control never lets a check pass. A second seed-derived pattern that differs from the first in both directions is written once per leaf (a CHK-W1S-NO-CLEAR cell), so the log tells an overwrite from an OR-merge.
. Re-check the observed step against the transition rules in value form, using the code the device returned rather than the one the test wrote.
. On the demoted leaf, assert both demotion registers first and prove the demotion is visible in feature control, then run the authorized bit-1 write as an advance cell.
. On the production leaf, present a valid SIP token with the comparator collapsed and require the error code (`CTRL-GATE-SIP-FAULT LOG code=0x3f`); the bit must not set. Release the fault and run the same write as an advance cell.
. On the production-end leaf, leave the sensed state by one of two exits, selected by the seed: a write of the ungated bit 0, or the RMA path (an RMA_SIP token match, then a write of bit 1). The exit is an advance cell (chapter golden INVALID `0x9` or `0xA`). Require the returned state to be INVALID (`CTRL-LC-INVALID-START seen: seed=<n> exit=<bit0\|rma_sip> state=<s>`; a miss fails the leaf with `CTRL-MISSING LC-INVALID-START`). Then write the lifecycle bits the exit left clear, with both tokens matched; the state must hold.
. On the transient leaf, after sense require the `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` shadow bit 0 at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` to read 1 (`CTRL-TRANSIENT-EN-ARMED seen: seed=<n> staged=1 shadow=1`; a miss fails the leaf), then require the state to hold with the enable armed and no token, then present the token and take no bus write at all; the same rules must apply.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Leaves |Contract
|CHK-START-SENSED
|prod, rma_sip, rma_chiplet, prod_end, prod_end_rma
|Before the first write, a frontdoor read of `LC_STATE` returns the sensed starting state the leaf claims. The bring-up already compares the sensed nibble on the shadow probe; this cell confirms the same value over the bus the walk writes on.
|CHK-W1S-NO-CLEAR
|all seven (prod and transient also with the leaf's first pattern)
|A write of zero to the lifecycle word clears nothing. Write-one-to-set means a bit never returns to 0. On prod, prod_demote, rma_sip and rma_chiplet the write runs from a named state, so a controller that treated the write as an assignment fails there. On prod_end, prod_end_rma and transient the write runs from INVALID, where the terminal rule also holds the state; on those leaves the cell shows that the state holds, not that it holds for the write-one-to-set reason.
|CHK-GATE-SIP
|prod
|The system-in-package bit does not set without a matching token. The write is otherwise identical to the advance cell later in the same leaf, which presents the token; that cell's result is logged (`OBS-LC-WR-ADVANCE`), not graded. On `prod_end_rma`, `CTRL-LC-INVALID-START` requires that a bit-1 write with the SIP token matched moves the state, so the SIP gate is proven to open.
|CHK-GATE-SIP-FAULT
|prod
|A valid SIP token presented with the comparator collapsed must not set the bit. The status is the fault code `6'b111111`, which a gate written as "not a mismatch" would accept. The cell that follows presents the same token with the fault removed; its result is logged (`OBS-LC-WR-ADVANCE`), not graded.
|CHK-GATE-CHIP-ORD
|prod
|The chiplet bit does not set from the production state even with the chiplet token *already matched*, because the system-in-package state is not yet established. This isolates the ordering requirement from the token requirement — with the token gate open, only the ordering gate can hold. The allow leg of bit 2 is on the rma_sip leaf: its CHIP-ADVANCE cell writes bit 2 from the system-in-package state with the chiplet token matched, and `CTRL-LC-ADVANCE-STATE` requires the chapter golden RMA_CHIPLET, so a DUT whose bit-2 path never sets fails that leaf.
|CHK-GATE-CHIP-TOK
|rma_sip
|The complementary half: from the system-in-package state, the chiplet bit does not set without a chiplet token match. The system-in-package start state is the RMA-BIT0 advance cell's chapter golden, required by `CTRL-LC-ADVANCE-STATE`. The bit-2 write with a chiplet match that follows is an advance cell, logged (`OBS-LC-WR-ADVANCE`) and not graded as a check; `CHK-GATE-CHIP-ORD` and this check prove the hold only.
|CHK-DEMOTE-OPEN
|prod_demote
|Both demotion registers read back asserted AND the decoded feature-control word matches the golden *with demotion applied*. The advance cell that follows runs demoted only if this check passes.
|CHK-LC-FEAT-DECODE
|prod, prod_demote, rma_sip, rma_chiplet, prod_end, prod_end_rma
|After each advance cell (prod: bit 1 with a SIP match; prod_demote: the same write, demoted; rma_sip: bit 0, bit 2 with a chiplet match, then bit 3; rma_chiplet: bit 0; prod_end, prod_end_rma: the exit), feature control equals the `lifecycle_controller.adoc` decode of the `LC_STATE` value the DUT returned, with the leaf's demotion applied; an INVALID state decodes to 0. Under the pinned disable vectors no named state decodes to 0, so an INVALID result is told apart from a named one. The `LC_STATE` value itself is `OBS-LC-WR-ADVANCE`.
|CHK-INVALID-TERM
|prod_end, prod_end_rma
|From the INVALID state the exit returned (`CTRL-LC-INVALID-START`), the lifecycle bits the exit left clear are written with both tokens matched, and `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` re-read as the match code after the RMA_CHIPLET match. After the bit-0 exit (`0x9`) those are bits 1 and 2; after the bit-1 exit (`0xA`) they are bits 0 and 2, and bit 0 is ungated. The write changes nothing. An ordinary write-one-to-set implementation would set bits the gates leave open, so the held value proves the end-of-life state is terminal.
|CHK-TRANSIENT-NO-TOKEN
|transient
|With the transient enable armed and no token presented, the sensed terminal product state holds. The transient path applies the token gates; it does not move on the enable bit alone.
|CHK-TRANSIENT-INVALID
|transient
|The transient return-material-authorisation path applies the same write-one-to-set and token rules with no bus request at all, including when the result falls outside the named set.
|CHK-LC-STATE-PORT
|all seven
|In every cell, the `sep` output `lc_state_o` (tap `lc_state_probe_o`) equals `LC_STATE[7:0]` at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and `{~raw, raw}` of the cell's state: the golden state of a hold or readback cell, the state the DUT returned in an advance cell. `doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc:202`: "`lc_state_o` is the differentially encoded LC_STATE shadow."; `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc:217-218`: "The shadow encodes the logical state as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}` in bits [7:0]". Comparing the port with both the probe and the encoding means a probe fault cannot mask a port fault. A nibble swap, a missing complement or a stale port fails. Evidence: `CHK-LC-STATE-PORT PASS: tag=<cell> lc_state_o=0x<v> shadow=0x<w> expect=0x<v>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every checker named for the leaf under test PASSes; the Leaves column is that per-leaf set, and the leaf requires it as its evidence set (a missing check fails the leaf). UVM_ERROR == 0. The closing line `INFO-LEAF walked=<walk> exit=<bit0\|rma_sip\|na> start=<state> seed=<n> required=<list>` grades nothing.

[NOTE]
====
The expected next state comes from `lc_state_next` in `cocotb/env/sep_lcc_golden.py`,
which is derived from `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` and not transcribed
from the register logic. The chapter gives three rules and no destination table, and
the golden states them the same way. See <<lcc-golden-from-spec>>.

Randomization: the tokens and the upper-byte pattern come from the run seed
(`cocotb/env/sep_lc_transition.py`, which reuses `SepRmaTokenCfg` so this walk and
the token randomized-configuration test cannot drift). Each production-end leaf
pins its PROD_END exit with `+lc_prod_end_exit`; without the plusarg the seed
selects one. Every cell within a leaf is fixed and runs every seed, so each leaf
runs one seed per regression.
====

*Logged, not graded* (all seven leaves, module `sep_lcc_lc_state_transition_matrix_test`):

* `OBS-LC-WR-NIBBLE`, every write cell: bits [7:4] of the write data and of the readback word. The specification does not state the effect of bits [7:4] of an `LC_STATE` write.
* `OBS-LC-WR-ADVANCE`, every advance cell: the prior state, the write, the chapter golden and the state the DUT returned. `sep_efuse_map.rdl` gives `lc_state` as `sw = r` and `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` gives `LC_STATE` as SW writable set-only, so no text-backed value exists for a write that the chapter moves. `CTRL-LC-ADVANCE-STATE` uses the chapter golden only as the start state of the cells that follow.
* `OBS-LC-UPPER`, every cell: `LC_STATE[31:8]` written, prior and read (step 3).
* `OBS-LC-WR-REFUSED-RESP`, every hold cell whose write a token gate or the terminal rule refuses (`CHK-GATE-SIP`, `CHK-GATE-CHIP-ORD`, `CHK-GATE-SIP-FAULT`, `CHK-GATE-CHIP-TOK`, `CHK-INVALID-TERM`): the write response. No text states the response of a refused write, so the scoreboard does not grade it; the state and the readback of the cell stay graded. Evidence: `OBS-LC-WR-REFUSED-RESP tag=<cell> prev=<state> wdata=0x<v> resp=<r>`.

*Leaves*: `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_prod_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_prod_demote_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_rma_sip_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_rma_chiplet_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_prod_end_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_prod_end_rma_test`, `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_transient_test` — one module, one sensed starting state per leaf, selected by `+lc_start` and staged at t=0 by `cocotb/dv_sim_prestage.py`.

*FEAT_CTRL liveness across the seven leaves*: the decode of an INVALID state is 0. On `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_prod_end_test` the first FEAT_CTRL read is the sensed PROD_END profile (`0xF000_0000_0000_0000` with the pinned DIS vectors) and is the in-leaf positive control; the read after the exit is graded by `CHK-LC-FEAT-DECODE` against the decode of the returned state. `prod`, `rma_sip` and `rma_chiplet` also read a non-zero decoded profile at the same RDL-derived address. On the PROD_END leaf the hold check `CHK-INVALID-TERM` compares the whole `LC_STATE[7:0]` byte.

*Why one leaf per starting state*: the one-time-programmable image is deposited at time zero and a fuse holds its state across every reset, so a test cannot re-sense its way to a different starting state; from one image only a write-one-to-set superset is reachable. Each starting state is therefore its own leaf, with the sensed value read off the device and required to match what the leaf asked for.

'''''

[[sep_sec_dis_override_test]]
===== 6.14 `sep_sec_dis_override_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu`; omit `+skip_fuse_sense`; `+sep_edn_jtag_reset_waive`. The JTAG `sep_reset_n` override resets the crypto engines without isolation, so an engine can drop an ungranted EDN request with no endpoint cancel; `tb_top` turns off the crypto EDN arbiter and adapter request-hold assertions for this leaf.

*Source Module*: `sep_sec_dis_override_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip, JTAG master on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_i` and `axil_sep_otp_jtag_resp_o`, taps on `ext_debug_bus_o`, `secure_tm_o`, `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` and `test_en_strap_i` (observation taps)

*Randomization*: RAND-NONE — directed match / mismatch / restore.

*Objective*: A match is reachable because `tb_top` binds `SEP_SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN` to SHA-256 of the all-zero token. `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` says a match does not release the SEP reset, and that the IC_RESET `sep_reset_n` override is an independent step: the override alone releases the reset and leaves life-cycle control closed. `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` says the match opens feature control without waiting for fuse sensing. `hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc` records the override. This leaf drives the SEP override inputs and reads the reset probes and `FEAT_CTRL`. The bus access and the IC_RESET TDR write are `sep_sec_dis_reset_reach_test`. Until sensing completes the SEP core is held in reset (`hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc`), so its load path issues nothing then: the OTP JTAG port presents the tokens while sense is stopped (the match and the `CHK-DROP` mismatch) and makes every eFuse access of the retained window, and each frontdoor read of `FEAT_CTRL` or the shadow map while sense stays stopped runs inside one release by the IC_RESET override (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`: the override releases a part whose sense does not complete), which ends with both reset probes back at 0. A read of the `LC_STATE` shadow-map word while SEC_DIS is active and sensing is still open must return AXI OKAY. A match while sensing is still open stops the sense, so `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` stays 0. SEC_DIS is for a part whose sense is already broken, and the recovery flow is a reset after the token. `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` says the token state is retained across reset, so after that reset SEC_DIS is still active, sense stays stopped, the map stays open and the override still releases the reset. A mismatch token then clears SEC_DIS, and a second reset runs a full sense. In the retained window the JTAG port writes the shadow map. `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc:121` restricts JTAG only in PROD and RMA_SIP, and the held INVALID encoding is neither, so the port reaches the map. `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc:66` says SEC_DIS opens the shadow registers to software, and `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` is software writable (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc:325`; `hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_efuse_map/sep_efuse_map.rdl:846-851`: `sw = rw`, `onwrite = woset`, reset 0), so a write of a 0 bit sets that bit and leaves the rest after sense; no line states a shadow write before sense under SEC_DIS. The pre-sense `LC_STATE` word holds the INVALID encoding `4'b1111`, the RDL reset `0x0F` (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`; `sep_efuse_map.rdl`). After the reset with the match kept, that word reads its reset value while the sense before the reset had loaded the sensed state into it, so the readback proves the reset reached the shadow. A JTAG write of the PROD encoding to the INVALID `LC_STATE` word leaves the word at its reset word `0x0000000f` (`CHK-SECDIS-LC-WR`): `lc_state` is `sw = r`, `LC_STATE` bits can only be set, and INVALID is terminal. Every read of the retained window goes through the same JTAG port and must answer AXI OKAY (`CTRL-SECDIS-JTAG-REACH`): the table gives JTAG read access to both words in the held INVALID state, so a refused or dead port fails. The JTAG readback of `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` after the JTAG write is logged without a verdict (`OBS-SECDIS-SHADOW-WR`); the read before the write returns its RDL reset value and is a control (`CTRL-SECDIS-SHADOW-WR`). The JTAG write responses are logged and not graded, because the specification states no response for these cases. No specification puts eFuse state on `ext_debug_bus_o`, so the bits of the whole bus that change while the two JTAG writes run and in a quiet window before and after them are logged without a verdict. Whether the bus carries eFuse state, its lane position and its value are open specification questions (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). After a later full sense with the `TEST_EN` strap at 0, a fresh match gives an all-ones `FEAT_CTRL` and `secure_tm_o` at 0: `SECURE_TM` is not a term in the feature-control computation (`hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc:336`, `:341`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc:25`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/test_mode.adoc:12`), and the strap loads at fuse-sense completion (`test_mode.adoc:34`).

*Test Coverage*: Security-disable match, mismatch, restore, override-before-match, sense-gated reset hold, pre-sense feature control, the same override inputs after the match, the pre-sense shadow-map read, the sense stopped by the match, SEC_DIS kept across `rst_ni`, JTAG shadow writes in the retained window with the debug bus around them, a full sense after SEC_DIS clears, and the strap-0 override cell. Functional coverage: `sep_sec_dis_boot_cg` `cp_hold` (the JTAG match while the reset stays held), `cp_map_resp` (the `LC_STATE` read), `cp_release` and `cp_rehold` (the override releases), which sample without an owner window, and `sep_sec_dis_boot_cg.cp_alone[override_open]` and `sep_sec_dis_robustness_cg.x_secdis_stm_feat[secure_tm_0]`, which have an owner window each. A first owner window runs from before the first `rst_ni` release to the stopped-sense gate read: `sep_lc_port_gate_cg.cp_presense_port` and the before-sense and SEC_DIS cells of `x_cond_gate`. It closes before the first override release under SEC_DIS. Window two covers CHK-OVR-AFTER in the stopped-sense window. Window three opens before the strap-0 match and closes after CHK-OVERRIDE-TM0 (CHK-MATCH, CHK-OVERRIDE, CHK-OVERRIDE-TM0); CHK-DEACTIVATE and CHK-RESTORE follow with the window closed. Each event is held as pending and samples only on the rise of its bit of a pass label that the leaf raises after the check of that window passed. The stuck-sense match, the CHK-RETAIN checks and the re-entry into SEC_DIS after `rst_ni` run outside every window. A FAIL closes the window and drops the pending event.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. A match is reachable because `tb_top` binds `SEP_SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN` to SHA-256 of the all-zero token (`CHK-MATCH` is the bind proof).
. The comparator hashes the written `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` and compares it to the metal expected digest (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`).
. After `release_no_cpu_reset`, and before any token, drive `jtag_sep_reset_n_ovrd_i=1` and `jtag_sep_reset_n_val_i=1`. Both reset probes read 1 while `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` is still 0 and `sec_dis` is 0. `FEAT_CTRL` equals the withheld-map golden (invalid lifecycle, feature control closed). Clear both inputs. Both probes read 0.
. Write the all-zero token over JTAG before `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` (every JTAG access answers OKAY). Match must assert, and `sep_cpu_reset_n_o` and `dbg_sep_reset_n_o` must stay 0 while sense is still open (`ext_boot_seq_done_i` is already 1, so sense is the only held term). Inside an override release, `FEAT_CTRL` equals the all-ones override golden.
. Drive the override again. Both reset probes read 1 while sense is still 0 and `sec_dis` is 1. Clear both inputs. Both probes read 0.
. Inside an override release, read the `LC_STATE` shadow-map word while `sec_dis=1` and sense is still 0. The checker requires AXI OKAY.
. Watch 4000 cycles. Sense stays 0 and both reset probes stay 0.
. Pulse `rst_ni` with the token kept. `sec_dis` stays 1, both reset probes stay 0, and inside override releases `FEAT_CTRL` stays all ones and the `LC_STATE` read returns AXI OKAY with the reset value `0x0F` where the read before the pulse returned the sensed state, the override releases and re-holds the reset, and sense stays 0 for another 4000 cycles.
. In the window after that `rst_ni` pulse, before the override releases, with sense stopped and every shadow at its reset value, read `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and `LC_STATE` over JTAG, watch `ext_debug_bus_o` for a quiet window, write one 0 bit of `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and then the PROD encoding to `LC_STATE` over JTAG, and watch the bus over both writes and a settle time. Watch a second quiet window at least twice the write window. Read both words again over JTAG; each of the four reads answers OKAY (`CTRL-SECDIS-JTAG-REACH`). Log the write response, the `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` value before and after, and whether the bit landed (`OBS-SECDIS-SHADOW-WR`). Require the `LC_STATE` readback to equal its reset word `0x0000000f` (`CHK-SECDIS-LC-WR`), and log the `LC_STATE` write response (`OBS-SECDIS-LC-WR`).
. Token `1`, written over JTAG, mismatches and `sec_dis` drops to 0.
. Pulse `rst_ni` again. Sense finishes in fewer than 4000 cycles, the sensed shadow matches the staged image, and both reset probes read 1.
. Token `1` mismatches.
. On a later match, `sec_dis` asserts and `FEAT_CTRL` follows `feat_ctrl_expected(..., sec_dis=1)`. With `test_en_strap_i` at 0, `FEAT_CTRL` and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` equal the all-ones golden and `secure_tm_o` reads 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-OVR-ALONE
|Before any match, with `sep_fuse_sense_done_o=0` and `sec_dis=0`, `jtag_sep_reset_n_ovrd_i=1` and `jtag_sep_reset_n_val_i=1` make both reset probes read 1, and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the withheld-map golden (invalid lifecycle, closed). Clearing the override makes both probes read 0. `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`: the override alone releases the reset and leaves life-cycle control closed.
|CHK-SENSE-GATED-RESET
|A match, presented over JTAG with every JTAG access answering OKAY, with `sep_fuse_sense_done_o=0` and `ext_boot_seq_done_i=1` leaves `sep_cpu_reset_n_o` and `dbg_sep_reset_n_o` at 0. `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`: a match does not release the SEP reset.
|CHK-FEAT-PRESENSE
|With that match still held, on the same clock as CHK-SENSE-GATED-RESET (`sep_fuse_sense_done_o=0`, `ext_boot_seq_done_i=1`, `jtag_sep_reset_n_ovrd_i=0`), `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` reads all ones while `sep_cpu_reset_n_o` and `dbg_sep_reset_n_o` read 0. `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc:39-41`: a match does not release the SEP reset, which stays held until fuse sensing completes; `hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc:45-53`, `:172-174`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc:336-338`, `:396-397`: SEC_DIS forces all ones. Control: in an override release of the same window both reset probes read 1 and a frontdoor `FEAT_CTRL` read returns all ones (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`). Evidence: `CHK-FEAT-PRESENSE PASS: held: sec_dis=1 sense_done=0 ovrd=0 sep_cpu_reset_n_o=0 dbg_sep_reset_n_o=0 lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o=0xffffffffffffffff exp=0xffffffffffffffff`.
|CHK-LC-STATE-PORT
|On the first `clk_i` edge after the first `rst_ni` release, with `sep_fuse_sense_done_o=0`, `lc_state_o` (`lc_state_probe_o`) equals the `LC_STATE` reset value 0x0F, the INVALID encoding `{~4'b1111, 4'b1111}` (`hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc:62-63`, `:92-93`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc:74-76`, `:216-218`; `doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc:202`; `hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_efuse_map/sep_efuse_map.rdl:577`). An X or Z bit fails. Evidence: `CHK-LC-STATE-PORT PASS: first clk_i edge after the rst_ni release sense_done=0 got_port=0x0f exp=0x0f`.
|CHK-DBG-DISABLE
|With sense open, each `dbg_disable_<path>_o` equals its case golden: before the token (SEC_DIS 0, INVALID profile, feature control 0) every gated path reads 1, and after the stopped-sense match (SEC_DIS 1, feature control all ones) every path reads 0; the two OTP JTAG2AXI paths read 0 in both (`doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc:213-214`: active-high; `hw/sys/dtp/doc/jtag.adoc:127-171`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc:62-63`, `:336-338`, `:394-397`, `:679-728`). Evidence: `CHK-DBG-DISABLE PASS: window=<presense\|sec_dis> sense_done=0 feat=0x<v> dbg_disable=<11 bits> (fields ...)`.
|CHK-FUSE-DFT-CLASS
|No text states the level of `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` and `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o`, so each is graded against two controls of the same leaf read after the full sense: the fail-closed PROD profile (closed: Case 3 AND SEP_FUSE_DBG, Case 2 AND SMC_FUSE_DBG both 0) and the post-sense match (open). The two controls differ, the pre-sense read equals the closed control and the stopped-sense SEC_DIS read equals the open control (`hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc:49-52`, `:336-338`, `:394-397`, `:709-712`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc:112-113`). Evidence: `CHK-FUSE-DFT-CLASS PASS: <port>_o presense=<a> == closed_ctl=<a>, sec_dis=<b> == open_ctl=<b>, controls differ`.
|OBS-DTP-PORT-LEVEL
|A logged line, not a graded check: the open and closed level of each fuse DFT port, once both are known (`sep_open`, `sep_closed`, `smc_open`, `smc_closed`, and the pre-sense and SEC_DIS reads).
|CHK-OVR-AFTER
|With `sec_dis=1` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o=0`, `jtag_sep_reset_n_ovrd_i=1` and `jtag_sep_reset_n_val_i=1` make both reset probes read 1. Clearing the override makes both probes read 0 while sense is still 0. The IC_RESET TDR write is `sep_sec_dis_reset_reach_test`.
|CHK-MAP-OPEN
|With `sec_dis=1` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o=0`, a read of the `LC_STATE` shadow-map word returns AXI OKAY.
|CHK-SENSE-STUCK
|After that match, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` stays 0 for 4000 cycles, and both reset probes stay 0.
|CHK-RETAIN-FEAT
|After a `rst_ni` pulse with the token kept, `sec_dis=1` and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the all-ones golden while `sep_fuse_sense_done_o=0`. `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`: the token state is retained across reset.
|CHK-RETAIN-RESET
|In that window, `ext_boot_seq_done_i=1` and both reset probes stay 0.
|CHK-RETAIN-MAP-OPEN
|In that window, a read of the `LC_STATE` shadow-map word returns AXI OKAY.
|CHK-RETAIN-LC-RESET
|That read returns the RDL reset value of the `LC_STATE` word, `0x0F`, the INVALID hold before sense (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, Reset Persistence: "every shadow register keeps its reset value"; `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing). A DUT that ignores the `rst_ni` pulse under SEC_DIS keeps the sensed word and fails. Control: the `CHK-MAP-OPEN` read before the pulse differs from `0x0F` (the stopped sense had loaded the word), else the leaf fails. Evidence: `CHK-RETAIN-LC-RESET PASS: LC_STATE @0x<a> pre=0x<v> post=0x0000000f want=0x0000000f`.
|CHK-RETAIN-OVR
|In that window, the override inputs release both reset probes to 1, and clearing them returns both to 0.
|CHK-RETAIN-STUCK
|In that window, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` stays 0 for 4000 cycles, and both reset probes stay 0.
|CTRL-SECDIS-SHADOW-WR
|In the retained window, before the JTAG write, the JTAG read of the `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` shadow returns its RDL reset value (`hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_efuse_map/sep_efuse_map.rdl:846-851`), and `sec_dis` is 1 with sense stopped around the debug-bus watches and the writes. A window in which one of these fails raises `CTRL-SECDIS-SHADOW-WR FAIL`.
|OBS-SECDIS-SHADOW-WR
|Log only. After a JTAG write of one 0 bit of `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` in the retained window: the write response, the JTAG-read value before and after, and whether the bit landed. The lines `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc:66`, `:121` and `:325` state a shadow write after sense, and none states it before sense under SEC_DIS, so the outcome has no verdict. Evidence: `OBS-SECDIS-SHADOW-WR: sec_dis=1 sense_done=0 JTAG wr REQUIRED_SIGNERS @0x<a> bit=<b> resp=<r> pre=0x<v> post=0x<v> landed=<0\|1>`.
|CHK-SECDIS-LC-WR
|In the retained window, after a JTAG write of the PROD encoding to the `LC_STATE` word, which holds the INVALID encoding, the JTAG readback equals the RDL reset word `0x0000000f`.
