Every text leaves the word unchanged: `hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_efuse_map/sep_efuse_map.rdl:573-577` gives `lc_state` as `sw = r` with reset `0x0f`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc:87` "LC_STATE bits can only be set" and `:90` "Every other encoding is INVALID and permanent", and raw `4'b1111` has no bit left to set; `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc:220-224` "Invalid lifecycle encodings are terminal". A write that lands (`0x000000e1`, or an OR-merge `0x000000ef`) fails. The write data keeps bits [31:8] at the read value 0, so this cell does not test those bits. Evidence: `CHK-SECDIS-LC-WR PASS: sec_dis=1 sense_done=0 LC_STATE @0x<a> wdata=0x000000e1 pre=0x0000000f post=0x0000000f want=0x0000000f`.
|CTRL-SECDIS-JTAG-REACH
|In the retained window, each of the four JTAG reads (`REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and `LC_STATE`, before and after the writes) answers AXI OKAY. `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control) restricts JTAG only in PROD and RMA_SIP, and the held INVALID state is neither, so JTAG has the read access of the fuse field table: both words are SW readable, and `LC_STATE` is never read-locked. A filter that refuses the port, or a dead port, fails it, so the unchanged word of `CHK-SECDIS-LC-WR` comes from a port that reaches the map. Evidence: `CTRL-SECDIS-JTAG-REACH PASS: sec_dis=1 sense_done=0 JTAG rd REQUIRED_SIGNERS @0x<a> pre=0x<v> post=0x<v>, LC_STATE @0x<a> pre=0x0000000f post=0x0000000f, all resp=OKAY`.
|CTRL-SECDIS-JTAG-WR-REACH
|(Control of `CHK-SECDIS-LC-WR`.) The JTAG write of one 0 bit of `REQUIRED_SIGNERS`, sent on the same port in the same window just before the `LC_STATE` write, reads back with the bit set, and the `LC_STATE` write completes. So a JTAG shadow write reaches the shadow map in this state, and a JTAG write that never arrives (refused by the filter, or a dead write channel) fails the leaf instead of passing `CHK-SECDIS-LC-WR`. The write responses are not graded (`OBS-SECDIS-SHADOW-WR`, `OBS-SECDIS-LC-WR`). Evidence: `CTRL-SECDIS-JTAG-WR-REACH PASS: sec_dis=1 sense_done=0 JTAG wr REQUIRED_SIGNERS @0x<a> bit=<b> landed=1 resp=<r>; LC_STATE wr @0x<a> completed resp=<r>`.
|OBS-SECDIS-LC-WR
|Log only. The JTAG write response of that `LC_STATE` write. No text states the response for a write to the terminal INVALID word before sense, so it has no verdict. Evidence: `OBS-SECDIS-LC-WR: sec_dis=1 sense_done=0 JTAG wr LC_STATE @0x<a> wdata=0x<v> resp=<r>`.
|OBS-SECDIS-DBG-BUS
|Log only. The bits of `ext_debug_bus_o` that change over the window of the two JTAG writes and over a quiet window before and after them, each at least twice the write window. No specification puts eFuse state on the bus (`hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` names the port only), so the line has no verdict (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, eFuse lane of `ext_debug_bus_o`).
|CHK-DROP
|Token `1`, presented over JTAG with every JTAG access answering OKAY, returns `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH=0x2a`, and `sec_dis` drops to 0.
|CHK-SENSE-CONTROL
|After a second `rst_ni` pulse with `sec_dis=0`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises in fewer than 4000 cycles. This is the live control for both stuck windows. The sensed shadow then matches the staged image, and both reset probes read 1. Evidence: `CHK-SENSE-CONTROL PASS: healthy sense done <n> cycles after rst_ni release (< 4000-cycle stuck windows); shadow_vs_staged words=256 mismatches=0; sep_cpu_reset_n_o=1 dbg_sep_reset_n_o=1`.
|CHK-MISMATCH
|Written token=1 returns `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH=0x2a`, and the poll after the go reads Idle `0x00` at least once (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, Token Submission: a go clears digest validity). `CHK-DROP` left a Mismatch result that a retained digest gives again after the reset, so without the Idle read the code does not show that this go ran. Evidence: `CHK-MISMATCH PASS: SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH=0x2a after token=1, idle_reads=<n>` with n at least 1.
|CHK-NO-OVERRIDE
|After the mismatch, `sec_dis` stays 0; `FEAT_CTRL` equals the fail-closed PROD golden.
|CHK-MATCH
|Written all-zero token returns `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH=0x15`.
|CHK-OVERRIDE
|After the match, sec_dis=1 and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the override golden (all ones).
|CHK-OVERRIDE-TM0
|At the `CHK-OVERRIDE` read with `test_en_strap_i` at 0, `sec_dis` is 1, `FEAT_CTRL` equals `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` equals the all-ones golden, and `secure_tm_o` is 0. `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc:336`, `:341`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc:25`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/test_mode.adoc:12`, `:34`. A strap that reads 1 fails the control with `CHK-HOOK`.
|CHK-DEACTIVATE
|A later token=1 returns mismatch again.
|CHK-RESTORE
|After that mismatch, `sec_dis=0` and `FEAT_CTRL` is fail-closed PROD again.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. `UVM_ERROR` is 0.

'''''

[[sep_sec_dis_reset_reach_test]]
===== 6.15 `sep_sec_dis_reset_reach_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu`; omit `+skip_fuse_sense`; `+sep_edn_jtag_reset_waive`. The JTAG `sep_reset_n` override resets the crypto engines without isolation, so an engine can drop an ungranted EDN request with no endpoint cancel; `tb_top` turns off the crypto EDN arbiter and adapter request-hold assertions for this leaf.

*Source Module*: `sep_sec_dis_reset_reach_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip, `jtag_ic_reset_reg`

*Randomization*: RAND-NONE — the eFuse image is PROD with fixed `SIP_DIS` / `SYS_DIS`

*Objective*: `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` says the IC_RESET `sep_reset_n` override alone releases the SEP reset and leaves life-cycle control closed. `hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc` says JTAG drives `sep_reset_n` through the SEP slice of the IC_RESET TDR. This leaf shifts `jtag_ic_reset_reg`, sized to `jtag_sep_reset_ctrl_t` and packed the way `jtag_ptap` packs the SEP slice. The TAP instruction that selects the TDR is outside this DUT. The per-port override pins stay 0. `SW_RESET_N` is in the `sep_reset_n` domain (`hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep_reset_ctrl.sv`). No token is written.

*Test Coverage*: TDR reset value, control-then-enable programming, `SW_RESET_N` readback, closed feature control, TDR re-hold, sense completing the release, and `SW_RESET_N` readback after that release.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. After `release_no_cpu_reset`, both reset probes read 0 and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` is 0.
. Reset the TDR and select it. Both probes stay 0. The override pins stay 0.
. Capture-DR and shift in the reset value with `sep_reset_n` `reset_control` cleared. The shifted-out word is all ones. Update-DR leaves both probes at 0.
. Shift the all-ones word in with no Capture-DR and no Update-DR. Then Capture-DR and shift again with `sep_reset_n` `reset_enable` cleared and `reset_control` at 1. The shifted-out word is the word written in step 3. Both probes read 1 while sensing is still open. The override pins stay 0.
. Read `SW_RESET_N`. The access returns AXI OKAY and the register reset default.
. `FEAT_CTRL` equals the withheld-map golden and `sec_dis` stays 0.
. Shift the all-ones word again. Both probes read 0 while sensing is still open.
. Wait for sense-done. Both probes read 1 with the TDR override off.
. Read `SW_RESET_N` again. The access returns AXI OKAY and the register reset default.
. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. It equals the PROD golden of the pinned image.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-TDR-IDLE
|Selecting the TDR at its reset value, with `sep_fuse_sense_done_o=0`, leaves both reset probes at 0 on every clock of the release bound CHK-TDR-RELEASE uses. `jtag_sep_reset_n_ovrd_i` and `jtag_sep_reset_n_val_i` stay 0.
|CHK-TDR-CAPTURE
|The word shifted out of the TDR after reset is all ones: `reset_hold` and every `reset_enable` and `reset_control` are 1.
|CHK-TDR-CTRL
|An Update-DR that clears `sep_reset_n` `reset_control` with `reset_enable` still 1 leaves both probes at 0 on every clock of the same release bound (`hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc`, PTAP IC_RESET fields: `reset_control` is driven only while `reset_enable` is 0). Before the next Capture-DR the leaf shifts the all-ones word in with no capture and no update, so the shift register no longer holds the written word; that Capture-DR shifts out the written word, so the update landed: `reset_control` moves without `reset_enable`. A TDR whose Capture-DR does not load the updated word shifts out all ones and fails.
|CHK-TDR-RELEASE
|Clearing `reset_enable` for `sep_reset_n`, with `reset_control` at 1, makes both probes read 1 while `sep_fuse_sense_done_o=0`. The override pins stay 0.
|CHK-REACH
|That read of `SW_RESET_N` returns AXI OKAY and the register reset default, while sensing is still open and both probes are 1.
|CHK-FEAT-CLOSED
|With no token match, `sec_dis=0` and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the withheld-map golden. `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`: the override alone leaves life-cycle control closed. The scoreboard judges both halves of the read (OKAY response and value) before the `PASS` line, so a refused read with zero data fails.
|CHK-TDR-REHOLD
|Restoring `reset_enable` makes both probes read 0 while sensing is still open.
|CHK-SENSE-RELEASE
|After sense-done, with the TDR override off, both probes read 1.
|CHK-SENSE-REACH
|After that release, a read of `SW_RESET_N` returns AXI OKAY and the register reset default, with both probes at 1.
|CHK-FEAT-SENSED
|After sense-done, `FEAT_CTRL` equals the PROD golden of the pinned image (`SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` fixed), which differs from the withheld-map golden. It is the positive control for CHK-FEAT-CLOSED: the same register leaves the withheld value once sensing completes.
|OBS-SENSE-STATUS-PRE
|A logged line, not a graded check: in the same TDR release, with sensing still open, a 64-bit read of `SEP_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS` (`hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl/sep_cpu_ctrl.rdl:232`): response, data, sense done before and after the read. No text states the response or value of that read while sensing runs. Evidence: `OBS-SENSE-STATUS-PRE: SEP_FUSE_SENSE_STATUS @0x<a> resp=<r> timed_out=<0\|1> rdata=0x<v> sense_done_before=0 sense_done_after=<d> sep_cpu_reset_n_o=1 dbg_sep_reset_n_o=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. `UVM_ERROR` is 0.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test]]
===== 6.16 `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test`

*BFM / Model*: none; DUT probes and a one-cycle state force

*Objective*: The eFuse read and program interfaces fail closed. From an illegal state encoding, the read interface withdraws an accepted command rather than carrying it to the fuse bank and retires with an error. The program interface is injected while idle and must recover to a legal encoding without issuing a bank command; request suppression and retirement status are not claimed on that leg. Separately, `read_error_o` is set by a failed or timed-out read and cleared by the next legal request. The word is `cleared by`, not `cleared only by`: the leaf shows the flag is still set once the timeout budget and the enable have been dropped, so the clear is attributable to the request, but it does not enumerate everything that cannot clear it. An interface that resumed from a corrupted state, or that carried a stale error into the next read, could return or mislabel fuse data it never legitimately fetched.

*Test Coverage*: Both FSMs and both illegal encodings: request suppression and error retirement on the in-flight read interface, and legal-state recovery with no bank command on the idle program interface. Plus the read error lifecycle across success, timeout, re-request and guard refusal.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Sense an `LC_PROD` image and wait for fuse sense to complete.
. Positive controls: a frontdoor OTP read and a frontdoor OTP program must each present a command to the fuse bank, on the same signals the refusal checks grade. Fuse sense does not drive either interface, so it is not a control for them.
. Measure the read interface's in-flight window: start a real read and count the cycles its bank request stays asserted. The injection below is only evidence if a machine left alone would have kept the request high across the same edge.
. For each of `2'b00` and `2'b11`, and for each of the read and program machines: force the state register for one cycle. The read injection lands while a real read is in flight with its bank request asserted, so the request must drop because of the injection. The program injection lands in an idle window and grades only legal-state recovery with no bank command.
. While the injected encoding is held, require the read interface's in-flight command to be withdrawn.
. Release, and on the next edge require the read interface to issue no command, set `error_o` and `done_o`, clear `busy_o`, and not return the sensed control-word value. On the program interface, require a legal recovered encoding and no bank command. The recovered encoding is asserted on both interfaces.
. Repeat the live-read control after all injections, so a machine left wedged by the recovery is caught.
. Read error lifecycle, on the same interface. A completed read reports done with no error. A read given a four-cycle timeout budget -- too short for the SHIM to answer inside -- retires with the error set. A fresh legal request then clears it. Finally, with the `TEST_EN` strap latched and the image re-sensed, a read refused by the guard retires with the error set and zero data.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-READ-ALIVE
|A frontdoor OTP read drives the read machine out of idle, before and after the injections. Fuse sense does not use this machine, so only a real `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` read is a control.
|CHK-PROGRAM-ALIVE
|A frontdoor OTP program presents a command on the program interface, before and after the injections. Every `SPARE7` bit used as program stimulus is already set in the golden image. The interface issues its bank command either way -- that is the only thing these legs grade -- and the array cannot change, so an aborted operation cannot leave the OTP image disagreeing with the golden the end-of-test resense compares against. Both controls are load-bearing: every suppression check below holds on an interface that never issues a command.
|CHK-READ-HELD
|Control for CHK-READ-SUPPRESS. A real read holds its bank request asserted for the whole bank latency, measured on unforced outputs and required to be more than one cycle. Without it, "no command" could hold because the request had fallen on its own across the injection edge, which is true on any RTL.
|CHK-READ-SUPPRESS
|The read interface presents no command to the fuse bank while `read_state_q` holds an illegal encoding. The encoding is injected while a real read is IN FLIGHT with its request asserted, so the request drops as a consequence of the injection: a design that carried an accepted command through a corrupted state fails here. Graded on that interface's own request, because the two interfaces drive separate requests.
|CHK-READ-FAILCLOSED
|One edge after the illegal encoding the read interface issues no bank command, reports done and not busy with the error set, and does not return the sensed control-word value. The recovered state is a legal encoding (`2'b01` idle or `2'b10` wait). No document names the encodings; the legal set is the one the state-inject block in `tb/tb_top.sv` accepts.
|CHK-PROGRAM-FAILCLOSED
|The program interface reports the injected illegal encoding on `*_state_o` and recovers to a legal encoding (`2'b01` idle or `2'b10` wait, the legal set accepted by the state-inject block in `tb/tb_top.sv`), with no bank command issued. *Suppression is not graded on this leg.* The injection lands in an idle window -- a program command cannot be kicked mid-flight without burning a fuse -- and an idle interface has already retired its last command, so `error_o` and `done_o` are both 1 and `busy`/`req` both 0 before the injection. The data term compares the interface's fixed refusal sentinel against an image word, which are never equal. Those four terms are logged and none is asserted as a result. The recovered-state legal-encoding check is asserted. The in-flight read leg (`CHK-READ-FAILCLOSED`) is where the 1/1/0 verdict is earned, and its terms are asserted low before the injection.
|CHK-READ-ERR-CLEAR-SUCCESS
|A read that completes normally reports done, not busy, and no error.
|CHK-READ-ERR-TIMEOUT
|A read that outlives its configured timeout budget retires with done set, busy clear and the error set, rather than hanging or reporting success.
|CHK-READ-ERR-CLEAR-REQUEST
|A fresh legal request clears the error left by the timed-out read. Two controls make the attribution real: the preceding check establishes an error was actually set, and the flag is re-read after the timeout budget and the enable are dropped and required to be STILL set, because either of those writes could otherwise be the thing that cleared it.
|CHK-READ-ERR-BLOCKED
|A read refused by the guard while `secure_tm` is latched retires with the error set and returns zero, never fuse content.
|===

The waits in the error legs observe `read_done_o` on the interface rather than
polling the `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` status field. The legs grade interface outputs, so
the wait has to watch the same signals; a status poll would let a read that
never retired look like one that did.

Both illegal encodings are walked rather than one. `2'b00` and `2'b11` fail the legal-set test for different reasons, and a decoder written as a comparison against a single value would recover from that value only.

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Not claimed*: request suppression on the program
interface. Showing it needs the illegal encoding injected while a program is in
flight, and aborting a program the SHIM has already accepted parks the SHIM
write FSM in its APB wait state until the next fuse resense, so every later leg
would resense across a stalled transaction. The difference is in
`efuse_interface_shim` alone: its read path copies the bank address into a
register at acceptance and drives APB from that copy, and its write path instead
tracks the live request for the whole transfer. That is why aborting a read is
harmless. `efuse_interface_shim` is an example shim; its write-path behaviour
is not graded. The idle program injection grades legal-state recovery with no
bank command. It does not grade suppression: the request is already low, so
that term would pass with the RTL's suppression logic deleted.

*Scope limit*: the illegal encoding is reached by forcing the state register, which no frontdoor stimulus can do -- that unreachability is the property the design relies on, so the behaviour asserted for it cannot otherwise be provoked. The force targets the state register only, for one cycle, and never the error, data, busy or request outputs the checkers read; the recovery those checkers observe is the design's own.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_km_public_id_test]]
===== 6.17 `sep_efuse_km_public_id_test`

*Phase*: 2

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense, `+km_rom_hex=km_rom_otp_id.parhex`. Two legs in one leaf: strap 0 (the bring-up) and strap 1 (a cold reset with `secure_tm_req_i` = 1 from before the release and a second real sense).

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_km_public_id_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip, KM CPU image (`km_rom_otp_id.S`)

*Objective*: the public identity fuses reach the Key Manager as sensed, and in secure test mode the four secrets reach it as valid zero while the public identities stay available. `hw/ip/key_manager/doc/architecture.adoc` (secure test mode): this mode replaces the four Class 1 sources (`*_UID` and `CLASS_KEY`) with valid zero values before KM captures them, and a dual-rail value word is the bitwise inverse of its complement word, so valid zero is every `VAL` word 0 and every `CPL` word `0xFFFF_FFFF`. A KM read lock gives `VAL` = `CPL` = 0 instead. `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` routes `SEP_CHIPLET_ID`, `SEP_SIP_ID` and `SEP_SYS_ID` to the Key Manager, and `km_csr.rdl` exposes each as eight value and eight complement words that `OTP_READ_LOCK` zeroes. The eFuse read lock hides only software and JTAG reads (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`) and does not blank the hardwired KM port (`hw/ip/key_manager/doc/architecture.adoc`, public identity delivery), so both KM views must survive it. The Key Manager block bench drives its own patterns onto the KM OTP input, so only a SEP-level run observes what the SEP delivers.

*Test Coverage*: Three public IDs through fuse sense, the SEP dual-rail encoders and the KM capture, with one eFuse read lock and the KM-side lock. The four secret fields (fixed named vectors) through the same path at strap 0 and at strap 1. `sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test` grades the secret mask at the shadow output; this entry adds the KMCSR view of it. Functional coverage: `sep_km_otp_readout_cg` (`cp_otp_efuse_lock_port`, one bin per seed: the seed draws the read-locked identity). The graded window is open in the strap-0 leg, from CHK-KM-ID-PORT after the sense to CHK-KM-ID-PORT after the KM lock, and closes on the first FAIL; the strap-1 leg runs with the window closed.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw three 256-bit IDs from the seed with no zero, all-ones or shared word, and set the eFuse read lock (`LOCKS[59]`, `[61]` or `[63]`) of one seed-selected field. Stage the image at `LC_PROD` and sense it for real.
. Compare each field on the KM `otp_data_i` input with `{~id, id}` of the staged value.
. Read all eight shadow words of the read-locked field over AXI and require that none returns its staged word. Read the two unlocked fields and let the scoreboard judge them against the image.
. Release the KM. Its image stores 0 to KM SRAM word 0, copies the 48 KMCSR words `OTP_SEP_CHIPLET_ID_VAL_0` .. `OTP_SEP_SYS_ID_CPL_7` to KM SRAM, copies the 32 `VAL` words of `OTP_CHIPLET_UID`, `OTP_SIP_UID`, `OTP_SYS_UID` and `OTP_CLASS_KEY` and, per field, the AND over k of `VAL_k` XOR `CPL_k`, copies `OTP_LIFE_CYCLE` and `OTP_DEMOTION_STATE`, sets the three public-ID bits of `OTP_READ_LOCK`, reads the register back, stores the OR of the 48 public-ID words read again, and stores a done marker. The host waits for word 0 to differ from the marker and then for the marker. The layout fits the 98 words of `km_sram_probe_o`.
. Compare the first copy with the staged IDs and their complements, the read-locked field included. Require the readback to be exactly the three bits and the OR of the second read to be zero. Compare the secret `VAL` words with the staged vectors and require each rail word to be `0xFFFF_FFFF`.
. Compare the KM input again.
. Strap-1 leg. Pulse `rst_ni` with the strap at 1, wait for the sense and for `secure_tm_o` = 1 within 16 cycles (a bounded wait; a timeout fails CHK-KM-SECRET-TM1 as a failed precondition). Release the KM again. Require every secret `VAL` word to be 0 and every rail word to be `0xFFFF_FFFF`. Compare the 48 public-ID words with the staged IDs and their complements. Log the KM input and `OTP_LIFE_CYCLE` and `OTP_DEMOTION_STATE` of both legs (`OBS-KM-OTHER-TM1`).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-KM-ID-PORT
|Each public-ID field on the KM OTP input equals `{~id, id}` of the staged value, after sense and again after `OTP_READ_LOCK` is set.
|CHK-KM-ID-EFUSE-LOCK
|No shadow read of the eFuse-read-locked field returns its staged word, and the unlocked fields' shadow reads return theirs.
|CHK-KM-ID-CSR
|The 48 KMCSR words KM firmware read are the staged ID words (`VAL_k`) and their complements (`CPL_k`) for all three fields, the eFuse-read-locked field included.
|CHK-KM-ID-KM-LOCK
|`OTP_READ_LOCK` reads back exactly its three public-ID bits, and all 48 words then read zero (their OR is 0).
|CHK-KM-SECRET-TM0
|With `secure_tm_o` = 0 the 32 secret `VAL` words equal the staged vectors, and each field's AND over k of `VAL_k` XOR `CPL_k` is `0xFFFF_FFFF` (every `CPL` word is the complement of its `VAL` word). This is the control of CHK-KM-SECRET-TM1.
|CHK-KM-SECRET-TM1
|With `secure_tm_o` = 1 every secret `VAL` word reads 0 and each rail word is `0xFFFF_FFFF`, so every `CPL` word reads `0xFFFF_FFFF`: valid zero (`hw/ip/key_manager/doc/architecture.adoc`, secure test mode and the dual-rail encoding). A DUT that does not blank the secrets shows the staged words; a DUT that blanks both rails shows a rail word of 0.
|CHK-KM-PUBID-TM1
|With `secure_tm_o` = 1 the 48 KMCSR words KM firmware read are the staged ID words and their complements for all three fields, the eFuse-read-locked field included. `hw/ip/key_manager/doc/architecture.adoc` (secure test mode): public identities remain available; `hw/sys/sep/doc/test_mode.adoc` (Protection While Active) disconnects only `CHIPLET_UID`, `SIP_UID`, `SYS_UID` and `CLASS_KEY`. No staged ID word is 0, so a DUT that also masks a public ID to zero fails. Control: CHK-KM-ID-CSR at strap 0.
|===

Logged, not graded: `OBS-KM-OTHER-TM1` (the KM input at strap 1, and `OTP_LIFE_CYCLE` and `OTP_DEMOTION_STATE` at both straps).

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: `OTP_READ_LOCK_COLD` is never set and is not graded here. At strap 1 the KM input, `OTP_LIFE_CYCLE` and `OTP_DEMOTION_STATE` are logged only. The KM image grades nothing itself: it copies raw words and reduces the locked public-ID read and each secret field's rail pair to one word, and the host holds every golden.

'''''

[[sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle]]
==== 6. eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field, Phase 3

Phase 3 entries of this area. Each entry is a seeded random test that draws its
cells of a legal matrix from the run seed, and it walks cells in one invocation only where the walk is cheap. The entries carry the full contract of each test. Each expected value is built from the cited specification or
register description. No expected value comes from RTL, from another test or
from the behaviour of the DUT. Tests that share a stimulus and a run mode are
one test with one leg per matrix. An entry that goes beyond an open test names
that test in its Test Coverage and lists only the cells that it adds. A check
that needs a PROPOSED internal drive is blocked and is marked `Blocked: needs <item>`
(<<sep-vplan-credit-rules>>), and it is not counted as reached. An error inject
or a top-port drive outside the closed set of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` is an added
hook and blocks no check.

===== Rules common to every test of this bucket

* *Random source*. Every random value comes from a seeded, replayable random source, and `--stage sim --seed N` replays a leaf. The stream is not a CSPRNG, so no draw makes a token, a key, a UID or a digest. The one exception is the HMAC key of the HMAC-overlap legs of `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`: it is stimulus of the HMAC engine, is never a token or a fuse field, and never leaves the simulation. The bench holds fixed named vectors for the tokens (`TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2`, `TOKEN_OTHER`, and the all-zero `TOKEN_ZERO`) and for the 256-bit fuse fields, each of eight distinct words that are non-zero and not all ones, with no two fields sharing a vector. A draw picks only the structure: which vector, the order, the shape and the position. No per-seed value is quoted as evidence.
* *Observation paths*. "Frontdoor" is the CPU-side bench master of target `lsu_stub_all_live`. The inbound filter does not act on it, so no disable-vector draw can cut this path. "JTAG master" drives `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_i` and reads `axil_sep_otp_jtag_resp_o`. `FEAT_CTRL` is read as the 64-bit register `SEP_LIFECYCLE_CTRL.FEAT_CTRL` (`hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc`, register `FEAT_CTRL`) or at the existing probe `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, because `feat_ctrl_o` is not a port.
* *Approved probes and injects*. An approved item is in the closed set: it is listed in `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` or exists in `tb/tb_top.sv`. Boundary ports: `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`, `security_disable_o`, `secure_tm_o`, `secure_tm_req_i`, `ext_boot_seq_done_i`, `lcc_demote_state_1_o`, `lcc_demote_state_2_o`, `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` and `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o` (`hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc`). Read-only probes: the sensed-shadow output `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, the demotion lock bits, the PIC source 40 request line (`sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o[39]`), the SEP reset net, the read and program interface outputs (state, error, done, busy and the request valid before the guard) and the RMA_SIP digest latch (`token_digest_*`). Test-side access: the OTP image preload at t=0, the direct OTP word read through the frontdoor read interface (`sep_efuse_direct_read_seq`, which secure test mode blocks and which is not the anchor of a check on the read interface), the JTAG master and the frontdoor. Injects: `lc_sigint_inject_i` (the lifecycle decoder input), `token_cmp_fault_inject_i` with `token_cmp_fault_sel_i` (copy 0 of each channel only), `token_digest_test_en_inject_i` (the RMA_SIP latch only) and the bank-model program-failure plusargs.
* *Observation taps*. An observation tap is a read-only reference to a DUT net. It is not PROPOSED and blocks nothing, and a test that uses one outside `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` adds it to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The observation taps of the tests are the post-guard fuse command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o`, the OTP bank probe `otp_bank_probe_o` (a read-only view of the 256 bank words, for the snapshot, the bit diff and the per-clock sample), the tap of the 24 token word registers, the digest latch probes of RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS, the comparator rail taps `cmp_match_p_i` and `cmp_match_n_i` (the hook control of the comparator copy select, also sampled by the coverage sampler), the DUT port `jtag_id_i` (the hook control of its drive), the integrity-error flag probe `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o` at the decoder output of the JTAG lifecycle filter, the shim response taps `efuse_shim_resp_valid_o`, `efuse_shim_resp_status_o` and `efuse_shim_resp_data_o` at the `sep` input `efuse_shim_command_resp_i` (the sense response of CHK-SENSE-STATUS), and the bound SEC_DIS digest `sec_dis_digest_probe_o` (the anchor of CHK-BIND). The coverage sampler adds these observation taps, which no check uses: the SEC_DIS token status `tok_sdis_i`, the sticky comparator fault bits `tok_fault_i`, the hash start pulses `token_go_i`, the SEC_DIS expected digest `sec_disable_expected_i`, the status flags `rd_addr_err_i`, `pg_addr_err_i` and `req_err_i` of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, the unmasked stored shadow `stored_shadow_i`, and the shim response `shim_resp_i` (valid, status and data) at the `sep` input `efuse_shim_command_resp_i` (`SEP_FCOV.adoc`, eFuse sampled signals). The sampler also reads, under its own port names, nets that the checks read through the probes above: `shim_cmd_i` (the post-guard command), `tok_words_i` (the token words), `latch_*_i` (the digest latches) and `jtag_filt_sigint_i` (the JTAG filter integrity flag).
* *Added hooks*. An added hook is an error inject or a top-port drive outside the closed set of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` (<<sep-vplan-credit-rules>>). It is not PROPOSED and blocks no check. The added hooks of the tests are the top-port drive of `jtag_id_i[31:1]` and these error injects: the comparator copy select at the `token_cmp_fault` injection port, the test-enable inject of the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS digest latches, the injection `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i` at the decoder input of the JTAG lifecycle filter, the bank-model sense status inject `efuse_sense_status_inject_i` with `efuse_sense_status_word_i`, and the shim response hold `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i` on the `sep` input `efuse_shim_command_resp_i`. A test adds its error injects to the inject exceptions and its top-port drive to the port table of the HDL Top section of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` (<<sep-vplan-phase3-added-hooks>>). Each item is named in the entry that uses it.
* *Hook controls*. Where a hook that does not act could let a check pass, the check has a hook control in the same leaf: the port value at the DUT boundary, a probe that shows where the inject lands, or the effect of the inject in a control step. A leaf whose hook control fails fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` and never skips.
* *Blocked*. A check, leg, cell or statement that needs a PROPOSED item is blocked: it is marked `Blocked: needs <item>` and does not run. The leaf logs `OBS-BLOCKED <check> reason=<item>` and no `CHK PASS`, and no summary row, log line or coverage statement counts the check as reached.
* *Fuse command observation*. The bank loads once at t=0 and keeps every program over reset, and the bench has no hook that rewrites the bank array. An image rewritten between senses changes only the test golden. A burn shows in a direct OTP word read through the frontdoor, in the sensed shadow after the next cold reset, and in an image snapshot and bit diff at the bank probe `otp_bank_probe_o`. The request valids of the read and program interfaces sit before the guard and cannot show a guard refusal. A check that a refused request changes no bank bit uses a direct read of the word where the read interface is not under test, and the bank probe diff where it is. A statement that no program command reaches the post-guard port, and every statement about a read command at the bank port, read the post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. The lifecycle output `lc_state_o` has no tap, so no check reads it. The `LC_STATE` shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` carries `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}` in bits [7:0] (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`) and is the lifecycle state observation.
* *Golden*. Each test builds its golden from the cited specification or register description, and the log prints the source and row of the rule that produced each expected value. A value that the specification leaves open is logged and not graded.
* *Reserved bits*. A graded compare of a frontdoor or JTAG register read covers only the bits of the RDL fields of that register (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, `efuse_mmr.adoc`, `sep_efuse_map.adoc`, `sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc`). A bit whose RDL row is `Reserved` with no access and no reset value has no stated value: the compare masks it, and the PASS line logs it as `rsvd=0x<v>`. A register value in a check or a step of this area is the value of the field bits: `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` `0x00000001` is the value of its fields, and a match-register value such as `0x15` is the value of `token_match_status` (bits 5:0), in a poll, a read run and a graded compare. A field that the RDL describes with an access and a reset value has a stated value, also when its name says reserved.
* *Control windows*. Every "never" check has a control in the same leaf. The control drives the same signal where a healthy design drives it, and the leaf fails if the control does not show the signal. A leaf with zero PASS lines for a required check fails.
* *Log shape*. `CHK-<NAME> PASS|FAIL seed=<n> key=value ...`. The verdict token carries a colon in the log: a graded line reads `CHK-<NAME> PASS: seed=<n> key=value ...` or `CHK-<NAME> FAIL: seed=<n> ...`, and every evidence line of this area that an entry writes as `CHK-<NAME> PASS seed=<n> ...` has that form. A control logs `CTRL-<NAME> seen: seed=<n> ...`; a control that does not show its cause logs `CTRL-MISSING <NAME>: seed=<n> ...` and fails the leaf. A PASS line prints the expected and the observed value. PASS and FAIL come from `results.xml` evidence of an executed comparison. A clean simulator exit is not evidence. A line `OBS-<NAME> seed=<n> ...` or `OBS-<NAME> LOG seed=<n> ...` records a value. A line `CHK-<NAME> INFO seed=<n> ...` records a logged cell that the entry names, and a line `CHK-<NAME> N/A seed=<n> ...` records a check that does not apply to the leg or the state of the leaf. These lines have no PASS token, grade nothing and are not counted as evidence of a check.
* *Image rules*. The OTP image is staged through the preload. A leaf has one t=0 image and never restages it. Where the Randomization of an entry draws an image dimension (the lifecycle state code, the lock layout, the field values or the disable vectors), the pre-sim hook `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/dv_sim_prestage.py` builds that image from the leaf seed before the simulator starts, through the same plan function that the test calls at run time, and the leaf logs each drawn dimension as a `INFO-DRAW` line. An entry whose Randomization draws no image dimension (6.18, 6.23 and 6.30) stages a fixed image: the test writes it before the first reset release, and its `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` are one fixed filter-legal pair, not a seed draw. A cold reset senses the same bank again, with every program of the leaf kept. Every 256-bit field has eight distinct words that are non-zero and not all ones. Reserved and unused bits stay 0. Lock bits stay 0 unless the test draws them. `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` follow the vector filter unless the entry states an exception and its reason.
* *Vector filter*. A pair of disable vectors is legal for a leaf when, in each of DBG_1 (bits 0 to 23), DBG_2 (bits 24 to 47) and Function (bits 48 to 63), the OR of the two vectors has at least one 1 and at least one 0, and each group has at least one bit set in only one vector. Without this filter a demotion, an owner veto or an RMA bypass has the same result as no effect, and the check cannot fail.
* *Time*. The specification gives no cycle count for sense, hash or program. Steps are placed by a probe or by a status poll with a bound. No wait for a program or a read request ends on a busy bit, because nothing shows that busy has risen before the first poll. Such a wait lasts a window W, a test-parameter multiple of the sense time measured in the leaf, with no early exit, and the status is read after it. A window that proves "never" is bounded by a multiple of the sense time measured in a control sense of the same leaf.
* *Persistence*. OTP bits, token arrays, the retained digest and its valid bit survive cold reset (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: reset alone does not revoke a prior token authorization). Every epoch that matches a token ends with a mismatch go on each matched channel before its cold reset, unless the entry grades the retained match. A SEC_DIS match never carries into the next leaf.
* *Digest staging*. The hash input is the 32-byte big-endian value of `token[255:0]`, index 7 first, no byte swap, and the comparison is `SHA256(token[255:0]) == expected_digest[255:0]` (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, sections Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures, Token Submission). The test reads the SHA-256 output as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, first digest byte most significant, and stages each RMA digest as that value in the 256-bit `token_digest` field with shadow word k holding bits [32k+31:32k], the same word rule that the token arrays state (index 0 holds `token[31:0]`). The specification states no split of the digest into shadow words and bytes (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, Digest word and byte order), so this staging is a stimulus choice and no check grades it. No leaf takes a packing from the DUT. The first Match step of a leaf that stages a digest is the control of the staging: a leaf whose control step does not read Match fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and its later steps are not graded.
* *SEC_DIS token*. The expected SEC_DIS digest is a constant bound at the bench (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). The bench binds the digest of the all-zero token; the specification states no digest value, so `CHK-BIND` grades this bench binding, a precondition of the token tests. `TOKEN_ZERO` is therefore the SEC_DIS right token: a test presents it only to activate SEC_DIS, and every other SEC_DIS go carries `TOKEN_OTHER`. A leaf that makes a SEC_DIS go starts with `CHK-BIND` and logs `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h> bound=0x<h> src=handle`: the SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the bound digest. The anchor is the simulator handle of the bench tap `sec_dis_digest_probe_o`, which carries the digest that `tb_top` binds to the DUT (`docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`, observation probes). The test never reads the digest from the bench source text. A build that does not show the handle, or a handle that holds X or Z after one simulator time step, fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. SEC_DIS acts only while its revision enable has the A0 value 1 (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Silicon Revision Scope). No leaf sets that value, because the carrier of it is open (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The first SEC_DIS match of every leaf is the control of the assumption: `security_disable_o` must rise, or the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
* *Strap during a token go*. Every SEC_DIS token go runs with `secure_tm_req_i` at 0, except the leg-E go of `sep_secure_tm_strap_sampling_test`: CHK-STM-LATE needs the strap at 1 when the token arrives, so that a DUT that samples the strap at the token fails. That match is a precondition (`CTRL-STM-E-TOKEN`), not a graded cell; if `secure_tm_req_i` is the test enable that freezes the retained digest, the go does not match and the leaf fails at that precondition. The specification says that test enable freezes the retained digest latches and does not say which signal is test enable, so no test of this bucket grades a token match with the strap at 1 (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, Test enable and token freeze). The leg-E match held into `SECURE_TM` = 1 is graded by `sep_secure_tm_strap_sampling_test` (CHK-STM-SECDIS-FEAT), and a token go with `test_en_i` = 1 is logged by `sep_scan_mode_state_hold_test` (`OBS-SCAN-SECDIS-GO`).
* *Bit address*. A program or read bit address is 8 times the shadow byte offset of the field plus the bit index (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields; `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc`).
* *Testlist*. Each test is one `[[tests]]` entry of `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml` with `run_modes = ["no_cpu"]`, `target = "lsu_stub_all_live"` and its seed count: 1 when every leaf walks the whole cell set and the seed selects only orders and values, otherwise small (3 or 4). No test has a per-cell testlist variant, and no plusarg pins a cell. Regression seeds are random per leaf, and a leaf does not know its run index, so no seed is tied to a cell. A cell that needs its own image, its own strap or its own leaf is a seed draw (weighted toward the corners where the entry says so), and its coverage closes over a merge of regressions (`SEP_FCOV.adoc`, Seed-dependent groups). No statement says that one regression fills a drawn cell. A leaf walks several cells only where the walk is cheap: register-level cells with no extra boot and no extra cold reset. A cheap cell that is contract-critical (a legal boundary, one representative per leg, the error injection itself) runs on every seed. The tests are members of the groups `all` and `cpu_stub`. A test that drives the JTAG `sep_reset_n` override, on the bench pins or through the IC_RESET TDR, adds the assertion waiver plusarg `+sep_edn_jtag_reset_waive` (<<sep-vplan-phase3-edn-waiver>>). No test needs the full CPU model.

'''''
[[sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test]]
===== 6.18 `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 1 seed per regression, because every leaf reads every cell; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. The rows of `CHK-CSR-RESET` with a zero reset value count as evidence on VCS only, because a 2-state run reads 0 from an unreset flop.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor), with the response code of every access. The port `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the sensed-shadow probe `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. A register model written from the register descriptions `efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, `efuse_mmr.adoc` and `sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc`. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. Every probe is approved. The test needs no inject and no PROPOSED addition.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RANDCFG). The draws are: two 28-bit timeout counts (non-zero, not `0x800000`, enable bit 0); the bits 18, 16 and 15:0 of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and the bits 15:0 of `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` (non-zero patterns; every go bit and every enable bit stays 0); the word order of each of the three token arrays; seven past-extent offsets in `0x20` to `0xFC` of the interface-control window (word aligned); six reserved-span addresses in `0x1093_0604` to `0x1093_FFF8` (word aligned), one of them in `0x1093_0604` to `0x1093_0FFC` and one in `0x1093_1000` to `0x1093_FFF8`. Alias targets: at least one of the seven offsets is `0x20` or `0x24` (a `SYS_DIS` word), and at least one drawn address in each reserved zone has bits [9:0] at a software-writable shadow word (byte offset `0x0`, `0x4`, `0x8`, `0x18`, `0x1C`, `0x20`, `0x24`, `0x1B8` or `0x1BC`, the registers with access RW in `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc`). The word index w of the control write (0 or 1, a `SYS_DIS` word). `reseed = 4` samples four sets of these draws per regression; no bin depends on a drawn offset or address, so more sets come from later regressions and no cell waits on them. These cells run on every seed: the reset reads, the control reads, the control write, and the pinned addresses `0x1093_041C`, `0x1093_0600`, `0x1093_0C1C` and `0x1093_FFFC`. The pinned addresses `0x1093_041C` and `0x1093_0C1C` have bits [9:0] at `0x1C`, the high word of `SIP_DIS` (access RW).

*Objective*: After sense, the interface-control window (`0x1093_0400`, 28 bytes) and the token window (`0x1093_0500`) hold the described registers with their reset values and read-write behaviour. No access past the interface-control extent, and no access in the reserved span at `0x1093_0600`, changes `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` or `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, or aliases a software-writable shadow word, after a real sense. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/memory_map.adoc` (rows eFuse Interface Control, eFuse Token Registers, Reserved); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (registers `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT`); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_mmr.adoc` (token arrays, `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc` (register `FEAT_CTRL`). Past the interface-control extent the APB decode answers SLVERR (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section OTP Fuse Controller, preamble). The test logs that response as a control and does not grade it, because `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` grades it. The response code and read data columns of `memory_map.adoc` state the view at the inbound AXI port (`hw/sys/sep/doc/memory_map.adoc`, address map preamble). `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` sends addresses past the last register window to the shim bank control registers. So no cited source states the CPU-side response of the reserved span, and none states its read data. The test logs that response and that read data and does not grade them. For the reserved span it grades only that `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and the 256 shadow words are unchanged after each access. The specification states no CPU-side read data past the interface-control extent (`0x1093_041C` to `0x1093_04FF`), so a refused read there can legally return any value. The test logs that read data and does not grade it, and it does not grade the exact code past the extent (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). A legal write to a set-only shadow word (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, row `SYS_DIS`: set-only, software writable; `sep_efuse_map.adoc`, register `SYS_DIS`, access RW1S) is the control that a write to a shadow word is visible.

*Test Coverage*: Reset values of the five interface-control registers after a real sense. OKAY reads of the two interface read-data registers. Write and readback of the writable fields of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with every go and enable bit at 0. Write and readback of all 24 token words of the three arrays in a drawn word order. The write and readback of both timeout counts with enable 0 and the reset value of `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` are logged controls, because `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` grades them. A write to the read-only status bits and to the read-only `FEAT_CTRL`. After the last graded window, a write to the read-only status bits with `efuse_read_addr_error` set and its clear bit at 1, which credits no bin. Eight past-extent accesses (the pinned offset `0x1C` and seven drawn offsets) and the reserved-span accesses (the pinned addresses `0x1093_0600`, `0x1093_0C1C` and `0x1093_FFFC` and six drawn addresses, at least one in each reserved zone), each read and each written, with the no-change compare of the three registers and, where bits [9:0] of the address select a software-writable shadow word, of that shadow word. One legal write of the complement of a `SYS_DIS` word, read back as all ones, as the control that a write to a shadow word is visible. The token-window span past `0x1093_0573` is not drawn, because `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` and `memory_map.adoc` give different codes for it (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The test extends three open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` keeps the refusal, the response code and the read data past the interface-control extent, the no-change of the two timeouts there, the timeout write and readback, the write and readback of one word of two token arrays, and the reset value of `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`, all run without a real sense. `sep_axi_map_refuse_test` keeps the response of the reserved span. `sep_efuse_image_test` keeps the shadow words after sense. This entry adds the reset values of the five interface-control registers after a real sense, the read-write behaviour of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, the write and readback of all 24 token words, and the no-change of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and the no-alias onto a shadow word of the past-extent and reserved accesses after a real sense. The response past the interface-control extent is logged as a control and is not graded. The open owners keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_map_geometry_cg` (`cp_window`, `cp_rsvd_zone`, `x_win_op_resp`, `cp_edge_addr`, `x_ctrl_reg_op`, and the token array cells of `x_token_blk_op`; the `TOKEN_EOP` write cell and the three match read cells belong to `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`). The logged controls (the response past the extent, the timeout writes, the match reads and the `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` reads) credit no bin.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the values of the Randomization field from the seed. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Stage the OTP image through the preload: TEST_DEV (`LC_STATE` word `0xF0`); `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` the fixed filter-legal pair of the image rules (no seed draw); each 256-bit field its own fixed vector; every lock bit 0; the other fields by the image rules. The staged vector words differ from 0, from all ones and from `0xBADCAB1E` (the read data that `memory_map.adoc` lists for an unmapped read at the inbound port). Every software-writable shadow word has at least one RW1S bit at 0 in the staged image (the vector filter admits only a pair with a 0 bit in each 32-bit word of `SIP_DIS` and of `SYS_DIS`, and `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and `REQUIRED_ALGS` have a used bit at 0), so a write of the complement that aliases onto the word changes it.
. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Reset reads. Before any write of the leaf, read through the frontdoor, in this order: `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` (`0x1093_0400`), `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` (`0x1093_0404`), `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` (`0x1093_0408`), `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` (`0x1093_0414`), `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` (`0x1093_0418`) and `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` (`0x1093_0570`). Then read the five interface-control registers again, each with a seeded AxPROT (0 to 7) and AxCACHE (0 to 15) from a separate stream, and log response and data (`OBS-EFUSE-AXATTR`); AxLOCK, AxUSER and AxATOP keep the bench default.
. Read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` (`0x1093_040C`) and `EFUSE_READ_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` (`0x1093_0410`). Log the response and the data. Do not grade the data.
. Read the three match registers (`0x1093_0564`, `0x1093_0568`, `0x1093_056C`). Log the values. Do not grade them: the retained digest and its valid bit survive reset and have no reset value. The leaf issues no go. Read no token array before its first write, because the arrays have no reset.
. Write the two drawn timeout counts with enable 0: `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT`, then `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT`. Read each register back.
. Write the drawn pattern to `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` and `efuse_program_go` at 0. Read the register back. Write the drawn pattern to `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` and `efuse_read_go` at 0. Read the register back.
. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor write of `0x0000_0071` to `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` (bits 0, 4, 5 and 6 at 1, the three clear bits at 0). Kind: write of 1 to read-only fields. Expected response: the register reads `0x1` after the write, and the B response is OKAY or SLVERR (`CTRL-RO-WR`); which of the two is not graded. Read the register.
. Write `TOKEN_T1` to `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I` (`0x1093_0500`), `TOKEN_T2` to `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_I` (`0x1093_0520`) and `TOKEN_OTHER` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` (`0x1093_0540`). Write the eight words of each array in its drawn order. Issue no go. Read the eight words of each array back, then read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` again.
. Read `FEAT_CTRL` (`0x1091_8000`, 64 bit) through the frontdoor.
. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor write to `FEAT_CTRL`, as one 64-bit write of the bitwise complement of the value of step 11. Kind: write to a read-only register. Expected response: the register value is unchanged. The response code is logged (`OBS-LCC-WR-RESP`) and not graded. The write tap of the lifecycle register block must count one write-address handshake at the `FEAT_CTRL` offset with the written data (`CTRL-LCC-WR-TAP`). Read `FEAT_CTRL` again.
. Control reads. Read the shadow word at `0x1093_03FC` and `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` at `0x1093_0400`. Both answer OKAY.
. Past-extent leg. For each address in the order `0x1093_041C` (pinned), then the seven drawn offsets: read the three graded interface-control registers (STATUS, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`) and the shadow word at address bits [9:0]; read the address and record the response and the data; read the same registers and word again; write the bitwise complement of that shadow word to the address and record the response; read the same registers and word a third time. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor read and write at the address. Kind: unmapped address past the interface-control extent. Expected response: no change of a register or a word. The read data and the response code are logged and not graded.
. Reserved leg. Run the loop of step 14 on `0x1093_0600` (pinned), `0x1093_0C1C` (pinned), `0x1093_FFFC` (pinned) and the six drawn addresses. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor read and write at the address. Kind: unmapped address in the reserved span. Expected response: no change of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` or a shadow word. The read data and the response code are logged and not graded.
. Repeat the control reads of step 13.
. Control write. Take the drawn word w of `SYS_DIS` (byte offset `0x20` plus 4 times w, shadow address `0x1093_0000` plus that offset). Write the bitwise complement of its staged value to it through the frontdoor. This is a legal shadow write: every lock bit is 0, `secure_tm_req_i` is 0 and the leaf runs in TEST_DEV. Read the word back; the OR-merge of the staged value and its complement is all ones. Set the golden of that word to all ones for the next step.
. Read the 256 shadow words through the frontdoor.
. With the graded window closed (this step credits no bin), set `efuse_read_addr_error`: write `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable`, `efuse_read_go` and `efuse_addr` at the first bit address past the bank (32 times the number of fuse words, `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`), read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` until the flag is 1 (at most 64 reads), then write `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` to 0 and read the status register once more. That read must return `0x00000041` (`CTRL-STATUS-ERR-SET`). Error injection. Point: the frontdoor write of `0x0000_0471` to `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` (bits 0, 4, 5 and 6 at 1 and `efuse_read_addr_error_clear` at 1). Kind: write of 1 to read-only fields together with a clear strobe. Expected response: read the register until `efuse_read_addr_error` is 0 (at most 64 reads); it then reads `0x1`. The response code is not graded.
. With the graded window closed, on `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` (a byte lane drawn in the `efuse_addr` bytes 0 and 1 and a halfword lane 0, with data inside `efuse_addr` so that `efuse_program_go` and `program_enable` stay 0) and on the `SYS_DIS` word that the control write did not use (a byte lane 0 to 3 and a halfword lane 0 or 2): a byte write, a halfword write and an unaligned halfword read at +1. Log each access (`OBS-EFUSE-SUBWORD`): strobe, write data, response, the word before and after, and the read data.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-CSR-RESET
|After sense done, each of these registers answers OKAY and reads its described reset value: `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` `0x00000001` (`efuse_sense_done` 1, the error flags and clear bits 0); `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` `0x00000000`; `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` `0x00800000` (enable 0, cycles `0x800000`) (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, reset column of each field; for the sense-done flag, which has no reset value, the description of `efuse_sense_done`, bit 0, and `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, the sense-completion indicator). A DUT with a wrong reset value, a missing sense-done bit or a stuck error flag fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of each register. Control: the status read comes after `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` is high, so a clear sense-done bit is a DUT fault and not a read that came too early. On Verilator only the rows with a non-zero reset value count; the zero rows log `INFO-CSR-RESET LOG` and no `PASS` field. Evidence: `CHK-CSR-RESET PASS seed=<n> reg=<name> exp=0x<v> got=0x<v>` (one line per register).
|CHK-CSR-DATA-RESP
|After sense done, each of the two read-data registers answers OKAY. Their data has no stated value at this point, so it is logged and not graded. A DUT that answers an error on either fails. Evidence: `CHK-CSR-DATA-RESP PASS seed=<n> reg=<read-data register> resp=OKAY` and `OBS-CSR-DATA LOG seed=<n> reg=<name> resp=OKAY rdata=0x<v>` (one pair per register).
|CHK-CSR-RW
|Each write to a writable register answers OKAY, and the register reads back the value written: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` bits 18, 16 and 15:0 (with `program_enable` and `efuse_program_go` at 0, and the status bits 26:24 reading 0), `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` bits 15:0 (with `read_enable` and `efuse_read_go` at 0, and the status bits 26:24 reading 0). The drawn patterns are non-zero, so a read of the reset value cannot equal the written value (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, the access column of each field). A write of `0x71` to `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` leaves the register at `0x00000001`: the status bits are read only, and the response code of that write is logged on the line (`wr_resp`) and not graded beyond `CTRL-RO-WR`. That write cannot show that it reached the register, so step 19 repeats it with `efuse_read_addr_error` set and `efuse_read_addr_error_clear` at 1: the register then reads `0x00000001` (the strobe clears the flag, and the read-only bits 4 and 5 stay 0 and bit 6 does not stay 1), which differs from the `0x00000041` read before the write, so a lost or refused write fails. The 28-bit cycle count of each timeout register (enable at 0) reads back the value written; that row is a logged control with no `PASS` field, because `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` grades the timeout write and readback. A DUT with a stuck or unwritable field, a writable status bit, a status write that does not reach the register, or an error response to a legal write fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of the field bits of each register; the reserved bits are logged. Control: the reset-value reads of CHK-CSR-RESET come before the first write, so each readback differs from the reset value for the written fields; `CTRL-STATUS-ERR-SET` shows the flag set before the step 19 write. Evidence: `CHK-CSR-RW PASS seed=<n> reg=<name> wr=0x<v> got=0x<v> resp=OKAY`; `CHK-CSR-RW PASS seed=<n> reg=STATUS wr=0x00000071 wr_resp=<r> got=0x00000001`; `CHK-CSR-RW PASS seed=<n> reg=STATUS pre=0x00000041 wr=0x00000471 wr_resp=<r> got=0x00000001 exp=0x00000001 polls=<k>`; `OBS-CSR-RW LOG seed=<n> reg=<timeout register> wr=0x<v> got=0x<v>`.
|CHK-TOKEN-RW
|Each token array returns the eight words written to it in the drawn order, and each write answers OKAY (`efuse_mmr.adoc`, registers `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I`, `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_I`, `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I`). `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` writes and reads back one word of two arrays, and this check adds the walk of all 24 words. `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` is read before and after the token writes, because no go is issued, and is logged as a control with no `PASS` field, because that test grades its reset value (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: reset clears the sticky fault flags). The match register values before any go are logged and not graded. A DUT whose array loses a word or swaps two words fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of each word. Control: the three arrays hold three different vectors, so a read that returns the word of another array differs. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-RW PASS seed=<n> arr=<sip/chp/sdis> words=8 equal=1`; `OBS-TOKEN-FAULT LOG seed=<n> fault_before=0x<v> fault_after=0x<v> match_logged=0x<v>`.
|CHK-FEAT-RO
|`FEAT_CTRL` keeps its value after one 64-bit write of its complement (`sep_lifecycle_ctrl.rdl`: `FEAT_CTRL` `sw = r`). A DUT whose `FEAT_CTRL` takes a bus write fails. The response code of the write is not graded (`OBS-LCC-WR-RESP`). Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: the written value differs from the value read, because it is the complement. Evidence: `CHK-FEAT-RO PASS seed=<n> before=0x<64b> wr=0x<64b> after=0x<64b>`.
|CTRL-LCC-WR-TAP
|The `FEAT_CTRL` write reaches the AXI-Lite port of the lifecycle register block: the write tap counts one write-address handshake, at the `FEAT_CTRL` offset of the generated RDL, with the written data. A missing tap event fails the leaf.
|CTRL-RO-WR
|The `0x71` write to `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` gets a B response that is OKAY or SLVERR, so the write reached a mapped register and an unchanged readback is not a write that never arrived. Which of the two codes answers is not stated and not graded. A missing response or a DECERR logs `CTRL-MISSING RO-WR` and fails the leaf. The `FEAT_CTRL` write shows its arrival through `CTRL-LCC-WR-TAP`. Evidence: `CTRL-RO-WR seen: seed=<n> reg=STATUS resp=<OKAY\|SLVERR>`.
|CTRL-STATUS-ERR-SET
|A read at the first bit address past the bank sets `efuse_read_addr_error` (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, description of `efuse_read_addr_error`), and `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` reads `0x00000041` after the `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` write of 0 and before the step 19 write. A missing flag fails the leaf. Evidence: `CTRL-STATUS-ERR-SET seen: seed=<n> read_bit=<b> status=0x00000041 exp=0x00000041 polls=<k>`.
|OBS-LCC-WR-RESP
|A logged line, not a graded check: the response of the `FEAT_CTRL` write. No document states the response of a write to a read-only lifecycle register.
|OBS-EFUSE-AXATTR
|A logged line per register, not a graded check: the five interface-control reset reads repeated with a seeded AxPROT and AxCACHE: response and data. No document states the response to these attributes. AxLOCK, AxUSER and AxATOP keep the bench default.
|OBS-EFUSE-SUBWORD
|A logged line per access, not a graded check: after the last graded step, a byte write, a halfword write and an unaligned halfword read on `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` (inside `efuse_addr`) and on one `SYS_DIS` shadow word: response, data and the word before and after. No document states sub-word access to these registers.
|CHK-WIN-DECODE
|After every access past the interface-control extent and in the reserved span, `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and the shadow word at address bits [9:0] read the values of the model: STATUS `0x00000001` (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, description of `efuse_sense_done`, bit 0; `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, the sense-completion indicator), the two control registers the values that step 8 wrote to them (the drawn patterns, with the go and enable bits 0), and the shadow word its staged value. The shadow-word compare is graded only where bits [9:0] of the address select a software-writable shadow word (access RW in `sep_efuse_map.adoc`), which has an RW1S bit at 0 in the staged image, because a write cannot change a word with access R; at every other address the shadow word is read and logged (`alias_graded=0`) and the register compare stays graded. Every leg has graded alias targets: the pinned addresses `0x1093_041C` and `0x1093_0C1C` and the alias targets of the draw. The read data of every access past the interface-control extent and in the reserved span is logged and not graded: the specification states no CPU-side read data past the extent (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, CPU-side response at the eFuse interface-control holes), and the reserved span goes to the shim bank control registers, whose contents the adopter defines. At the end of the leaf all 256 shadow words equal their staged values, except the word of step 17, which equals all ones. A DUT that wraps the address of a write onto a shadow word or a register, or takes the write, fails. The response of each access past the extent and in the reserved span is logged as `OBS-WIN-RESP LOG` and is not graded here (`sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` grades the refusal past the extent; the reserved span has no stated CPU-side response). Anchor: the frontdoor readback of the registers and words. The golden is the register model (reset value and the values the leaf wrote) and the staged image, not a value read from the DUT before the access. Control: a write of the same kind is visible. The legal writes of step 8 to `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` read back (CHK-CSR-RW), and the legal write of step 17, the complement of a `SYS_DIS` word, reads back as all ones (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, set-only OR-merge), so an alias write onto a register or onto a shadow word would show. The shadow word at `0x1093_03FC` and the register at `0x1093_0400` answer OKAY with the staged value and the reset value, before the first unmapped access and after the last one. Evidence: `CHK-WIN-DECODE PASS seed=<n> win=<ctrl/rsvd> addr=0x<a> op=<rd/wr> wdata=0x<v> alias_graded=<1\|0> alias_exp=0x<v> alias_got=0x<v> regs_equal=1`; `OBS-WIN-RESP LOG seed=<n> win=<ctrl/rsvd> addr=0x<a> op=<rd/wr> resp=<code> rdata=0x<v>` (`rdata=na` for a write); `CHK-WIN-DECODE PASS seed=<n> win=ctl addr=0x<a> op=rd resp=OKAY got=0x<v> exp=0x<v>`; `CHK-WIN-DECODE PASS seed=<n> win=ctlwr word=<w> addr=0x<a> staged=0x<v> wr=0x<v> got=0xffffffff`; `CHK-WIN-DECODE PASS seed=<n> win=sweep words=256 mismatches=0`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test does not grade the response past the interface-control extent (OKAY or not, and the exact code), the response code and the read data of the reserved span, the response code of a write to a read-only register, or the data of the two interface read-data registers. The token-window span past `0x1093_0573` is not drawn. A match result and the effect of a go belong to `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`. The refusal, the response code and the read data past the extent, and the no-change of the two timeouts there, belong to `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test`, the response of the reserved span to `sep_axi_map_refuse_test`, and the shadow words after sense to `sep_efuse_image_test`. A zero reset value is graded on VCS only.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test]]
===== 6.19 `sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. A leaf has one fuse image, so the seed draws the field values of that image (the preload hook stages it from the seed), and each leaf reads one value per field.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor). The port `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the sensed-shadow probe `efuse_shadow_probe_o` (approved). The OTP image preload (approved). The fixed vectors for the 256-bit fields. An offset table that the test builds from the RDL-generated register sources: the byte offsets from the generated register header (`hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/py/sep_reg.py`, the `SEP_EFUSE_MAP_*_REG_OFFSET` constants) and the widths from the register sizes of the generated IP-XACT (`hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/ipxact/sep.xml`) at the same address. The used-bit masks come from the RDL field masks of the same register map. The test parses no `.adoc` at run time. Added hook, a top-port drive that the test adds to the port table of the HDL Top section of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`: a drive of `jtag_id_i[31:1]`, which `tb_top` ties to 0, for CHK-JTAGID. The DUT port `jtag_id_i` is read as an observation tap. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The draws are: the value of each narrow field; the choice of the fixed vector of each 256-bit field (no two fields share a vector); the write-lock set of `LOCKS_SPARE`; and `jtag_id_i[31:1]`. Each graded narrow field draws its class first, with equal weight per class, and then a value inside the class, so the edge values take most of the draws: `SBOOT_DIS` 0 or 1, and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 1 - `SBOOT_DIS` (the test presents no token, so no update follows); `STATUS_RPT`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and each nibble of `REQUIRED_ALGS` (chiplet, SiP, system) one of 0 to 3; `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ` one of {1, 2047, a value in 2 to 2046}; `ROM_CTL` endianness 0 or 1, and swap field one of {`0b00000`, each single bit (five classes), `0b11111`, another value}; `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE` one of {0, one bit of bits 25:0, all of bits 25:0, two or more bits of bits 25:0 and not all}. `LOCKS` is a drawn non-zero set of write-lock bits (even bits 2 to 62, slots 1 to 31, so that slot 0 stays clear), with the two words different. `LOCKS_SPARE` is a drawn non-zero set of write-lock bits (even bits 0 to 16, slots 32 to 40). `LC_STATE` is TEST_DEV (word `0xF0`). `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` are a filter-legal pair. Trap filters: every two adjacent staged words differ (an equal pair redraws one of its words, a 256-bit vector choice first, else a narrow value inside its drawn class); no staged word equals any of the three 32-bit forms of `jtag_id_i`; every reserved bit is 0. `reseed = 4` gives four images per regression. The edge classes are seed-dependent cells: the coverage of `sep_efuse_field_value_cg` closes over a merge of regressions, about 4 regressions for the eight swap classes of `ROM_CTL`, the largest class list (8 ln 8 / 4).

*Objective*: The used-bit ranges of nine narrow registers read back as staged, at edge values that the seed draws: the ten 32-bit registers except `LOCKS_SPARE`, which is a lock word whose lock bits belong to `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`. The graded nine are `LC_STATE`, `SBOOT_DIS`, `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE`, `STATUS_RPT`, `ROM_CTL`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and `REQUIRED_ALGS`. The register layout is a control: the shadow map holds 43 registers at the described byte offsets and widths, which are ten 32-bit registers (the nine above and `LOCKS_SPARE`), three 64-bit registers (`LOCKS`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`) and thirty 256-bit registers, and the 96-bit `LOCK` field spans `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE`. A drawn non-zero `jtag_id_i` does not enter any shadow word: the JTAG ID is not in the fuse map and is a top-level port (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, the text after the field table). The word-by-word compare of all 43 registers is a control, because `sep_efuse_image_test`, `sep_efuse_sense_test` and `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test` grade it. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Fuse Fields, field table and the text after it); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc` (section Configuration Parameters: 256 words of 32 bits); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (address map and the registers `LOCKS_SPARE`, `LC_STATE`, `SBOOT_DIS`, `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`, `STATUS_RPT`, `ROM_CTL`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS`, `REQUIRED_ALGS`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE`, `SEP_CHIPLET_ID`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` (port `jtag_id_i[31:1]`, for the port only).

*Test Coverage*: The offset and width of all 43 registers, from the generated register header and IP-XACT, as a control: the table must tile the 256 words before any DUT access, and the word-by-word compare is logged and not graded. The used bits of the nine graded narrow registers (the ten 32-bit registers except `LOCKS_SPARE`), with values drawn from these edge classes over a merge of regressions: `SBOOT_DIS` 0 and 1; `STATUS_RPT`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and each `REQUIRED_ALGS` nibble 0 to 3; `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ` 1, 2047 and a middle value; `ROM_CTL` endianness 0 and 1, and the swap field at 0, each single bit, all five bits and one other value; `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE` 0, one bit, bits 25:0 all set and other values. The reserved bits of the narrow fields, logged. The absence of a drawn non-zero `jtag_id_i` in the 256 shadow words. The test extends two open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_efuse_image_test` compares the shadow field by field against the image golden after sense, and `sep_efuse_sense_test` senses an image. This entry adds the used-bit compare of the narrow fields with edge values and the absence of `jtag_id_i` in the shadow. The lock exemption of `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` belongs to `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`, and the end of the map to `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test`. The word-by-word compare of the 43 registers is recorded in `INFO-FIELD LOG` lines and is not graded here. The open owners keep that grade. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_map_geometry_cg` (`cp_shadow_field`, and the non-zero bin of `cp_jtag_id_class`) and `sep_efuse_field_value_cg` (seed-dependent: one leaf fills one class per field, and the edge bins close over a merge of regressions).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Build the offset table: the byte offset of each register from the generated register header (`SEP_EFUSE_MAP_*_REG_OFFSET`), and its width from the register size of the generated IP-XACT at the same address. The table is the only source of the offsets. Require 43 rows that tile the words 0 to 255 with no gap and no overlap. A table that does not tile fails the leaf before any DUT access with `SETUP FAIL reason=table_tile`. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Draw the values of the Randomization field from the seed, each narrow field with its class. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value, with the class of each narrow field.
. Build the image. Every 256-bit field takes its own fixed vector. The narrow fields take the drawn values. Reserved and unused bits stay 0. Every lock bit is 0 except the drawn write-lock sets of `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE`. The leaf makes no program and no shadow write, so a write-lock changes no read. Check the adjacent rule on the 256 staged words. Redraw one word of an equal pair by the trap filter. If no redraw separates the pair, fail the leaf with `SETUP FAIL reason=adjacent_equal` before the first read.
. Draw `jtag_id_i[31:1]`, non-zero, so that none of its three 32-bit forms equals a staged word. Drive it from reset release to the end of the leaf.
. Preload the image. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Read every register through the frontdoor, word by word, at its table offset: the 43 registers cover words 0 to 255. Log each word with its staged value as `INFO-FIELD LOG seed=<n> field=<name> off=0x<o> words=<k> exp=0x<hex> got=0x<hex>` (one line per register, 43 lines). These lines are bench records and are not graded. For a 32-bit narrow register the logged compare covers the bits of the used mask only: `LOCKS_SPARE` `0x0003FFFF`, `LC_STATE` `0x000000FF`, `SBOOT_DIS` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` `0x1`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE` `0x03FFFFFF`, `STATUS_RPT` and `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` `0x3`, `ROM_CTL` `0x3F`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ` `0x7FF`, `REQUIRED_ALGS` `0x333`. Each mask is the RDL field mask of the used field, except two sub-ranges that the field description names reserved: bits 31:26 of `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE.select` and bits 3:2 of each `REQUIRED_ALGS` nibble.
. Read each of the nine graded narrow registers again as one 32-bit access. Split the word into its used bits and its reserved bits. Compare the used bits with the staged value. Log the reserved bits. `LC_STATE` is among the nine; `LOCKS_SPARE` is not read again.
. Sample the DUT port `jtag_id_i` and the 256 sensed words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. The port must equal the drawn ID (hook control). Compare each word with the three 32-bit forms of `jtag_id_i`: `{id, 1'b0}`, `{id, 1'b1}` and `{1'b0, id}`. With the same compare, compare each word with the staged word of its index, with `LC_STATE` as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}` (positive control of the compare).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-FIELD-USED
