|The used bits of each of the nine graded narrow registers (`LC_STATE`, `SBOOT_DIS`, `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE`, `STATUS_RPT`, `ROM_CTL`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS`, `REQUIRED_ALGS`) equal the staged value, in a separate 32-bit read of the word at its table offset (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields; `sep_efuse_map.adoc`, address map). The reserved bits are logged and not graded (`sep_efuse_map.adoc`, the bit table of each narrow register). Bits 31:26 of `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE.select` and bits 3:2 of each `REQUIRED_ALGS` nibble lie inside an RDL field with an access and a reset value, but the field description names them reserved, so the used mask leaves them out and they are logged in `rsvd_got`: the image rules stage them at 0, and a bit that the image never stages at 1 gives no compare that fails on a dropped bit. The 256-bit, 64-bit and `LOCKS_SPARE` words are not graded here, and the lock exemption of `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` belongs to `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`. A DUT that drops a used bit, shifts a field inside its word or reads a used bit as 0 for an edge value fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback, split into used and reserved bits. Control: in every leaf one of `SBOOT_DIS` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` is staged at 1 and the staged `LC_STATE` byte is `0xF0`, so a read of 0 differs; the class draw gives the edge values (0 and 1, the lowest and highest value of the used range, the single bits of `ROM_CTL`, bits 25:0 all set) most of the draws, so over a merge of regressions each used bit is read at 1 and at 0 and an unwritten or stuck bit shows. Evidence: nine lines per run, one per graded register, `CHK-FIELD-USED PASS seed=<n> field=<name> class=<c> used_mask=0x<m> exp=0x<v> got=0x<v> rsvd_got=0x<v>`; a run with fewer than nine lines fails.
|CHK-JTAGID
|No sensed shadow word equals any of the three 32-bit forms of the drawn non-zero ID: `{id, 1'b0}`, `{id, 1'b1}` and `{1'b0, id}` (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields: the JTAG_ID field is not in the fuse map and is a top-level port, so no shadow field takes it; `port_table.adoc`, port `jtag_id_i[31:1]`, for the port only). A DUT that stores the ID in a shadow word at any of these alignments fails. Anchor: the 256 words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and the frontdoor readback of step 6. Control: the DUT port `jtag_id_i` equals the drawn ID during the reads, logged as `CTRL-HOOK seen: seed=<n> where=<sense_done/field_reads/probe_sample> port=0x<id>`, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` (hook control); the trap filter makes all three forms differ from every staged word; and the same compare finds each of the 256 sensed words equal to the staged word of its index (`LC_STATE` as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}`), so the compare can see an equal word. Evidence: `CHK-JTAGID PASS seed=<n> id=0x<v> port=0x<v> f0=0x<v> f1=0x<v> fz=0x<v> words_checked=256 equal=0 staged_equal=256`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0. Each of the three hook controls logs `CTRL-HOOK seen`. A leaf whose DUT port `jtag_id_i` does not equal the drawn ID fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.

*Scope limit*: The test makes no program, no shadow write and no lock write. It does not grade the reserved bits of a narrow field, the meaning of a field value, the lock behaviour or the access policy of a field. The value of every field against the image, and the word-by-word compare of the 43 registers, belong to `sep_efuse_image_test`, and the sense sequence to `sep_efuse_sense_test`. The reset values of the interface-control registers, the end of the map and the response past it belong to `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test`. The `jtag_id_i` check does not grade the IDCODE path of the JTAG TAP.

'''''

[[sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test]]
===== 6.20 `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. A leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the nibble of that image (the preload hook stages it from the seed). The JTAG master is live in that target. Leg C runs only in a leaf whose drawn nibble is `0000` (TEST_DEV), after legs A and B on the same image. It uses the added error inject `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i`, and the flag at `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o` is its hook control.

*Source Module*: `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor). The JTAG master on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_i` and `axil_sep_otp_jtag_resp_o` (approved, leg C). The bench input `lc_sigint_inject_i` (approved), the ports `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`, `security_disable_o` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, the probes `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` and `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, and the OTP image preload (all approved). The register `FEAT_CTRL` is read through the frontdoor. A profile function written for this test from the Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile table. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_ZERO` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. Added hook, for leg C (CHK-JTAG-SIGINT): the error inject `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i` at the decoder input of the JTAG lifecycle filter, which the test adds to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o`, the integrity-error flag at the decoder output of that filter, is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The approved `lc_sigint_inject_i` acts at the lifecycle decoder, and the leaf does not use it for a JTAG grade.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The draws are: the nibble n, one of the seven nibbles of the open-profile classes {`0000`, `0001`, `0010`, `0011`, `0110`, `0111`, `1000`}, with weight 2 for `0000` (the nibble that also runs leg C) and weight 1 for each of the others; the disable-vector pair; and, in leg C, the JTAG shadow-word offset, the control bit and the drawn bit of the `SIP_DIS` writes, the two timeout counts c1 and c2 (28-bit, non-zero, different, not `0x800000`) and the word order of the token write. The X bit of an RMA code is bit 0 of n and is never drawn. In leg A: the two walk orders (each a `SepSeededRng` shuffle of the 30 pairs of pair mask 1 to 15 and bad rail value 0 or 1), the demotion set between the walks (`DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2` or both) and one `rsvd` marker bit per `DEMOTE` register. Trap filter: the vector filter (so the profile row of the control epoch is non-zero for every nibble); in leg C, the control bit and the drawn bit are two different bits that the staged `SIP_DIS` lacks (the vector filter leaves at least one 0 bit of `SIP_DIS` in each group). `reseed = 4` gives four nibbles per regression. The nibble is a seed-dependent cell: the seven nibbles of `x_sigint_state_secdis_out` close over a merge of regressions, about 4 regressions (7 ln 7 / 4). Leg C runs in a leaf that draws `0000` (weight 2 of 8), so its cells close over a merge of regressions.

*Objective*: A non-complementary byte at the lifecycle decoder input raises `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` and forces `FEAT_CTRL` to 0, unless SEC_DIS holds, which forces all ones even on an integrity error. On an LC differential-decode integrity error the OTP JTAG wrapper admits only the MMR region, which holds the token inputs. The specification does not say that the wrapper takes the error from the decoder of the lifecycle controller, so leg C injects at the decoder of the JTAG filter. The OTP cannot hold such a byte, because the fuse sensing loads the raw nibble and stores it differentially encoded in the shadow. So the leaf injects the byte with the bench input `lc_sigint_inject_i`. The profile of the 16 raw codes (legal and INVALID) is a leg of `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections Life Cycle Controller, Lifecycle States, Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS), Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile, Debug and Test Port Path Gating, row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`; Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control); `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` (sections Token Activation, Implementation Requirements); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` (port `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc` (paragraph on the lifecycle state load, `HAS_LC_STATE`).

*Test Coverage*: Leg A: after a control epoch of the same state and vector pair that shows a non-zero profile, two walks of the 30 injected non-complementary bytes (pair mask 1 to 15 x bad rail value 0 or 1), the first without demotion and the second with the drawn demotion set. Every epoch grades `FEAT_CTRL`, the demote pairs, the gated `dbg_disable` ports and the fuse DFT ports on every clock. Leg B: SEC_DIS over the injected error (a `TOKEN_ZERO` match), then a `TOKEN_OTHER` mismatch. Leg C: the JTAG policy under the filter-side injection in a TEST_DEV image, with a control epoch without injection that reads two targets and writes one `SIP_DIS` bit and one interface-control register. The injection epoch covers every class of the policy: a shadow word read and write, an interface-control register read and write, a token word read and write, the `TOKEN_EOP` go and the match read. The nibble `0111` of leg A is the cell that `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` injects after its chain, so it runs as a control with `role=control`; its walks do not sample the sigint cells of the functional coverage, so those cells close over merged regressions. The other six nibbles of leg A and every nibble of leg B are new cells. No check grades anything after an injection input falls, because the specification does not state a release of `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`; `OBS-LC-SIGINT-RELEASE` logs the state two clocks after the first epoch's inject falls. The test extends two open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` walks the lifecycle to feature-control path and injects one integrity error through `lc_sigint_inject_i`. `sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test` reads the shadow map and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` over JTAG in TEST_DEV and is the control of leg C. This entry adds the injected error in six open-profile nibbles (the nibble `0111` of leg A is a control), SEC_DIS over the integrity error in each of the seven, and the JTAG policy under the error. The open owners keep the rest. Phase 1 group `sep_lc_state_cg` takes hits. Functional coverage: `sep_lc_sigint_failclosed_cg` (seed-dependent: `x_sigint_state_secdis_out`, one drawn nibble per leaf, closed over a merge of regressions) and `sep_efuse_jtag_policy_cg` (the integrity-error bin of `cp_state` and the integrity-error cells of `x_state_class_op`, seed-dependent: they fill in a leaf that draws `0000`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the values of the Randomization field from the seed. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n> n=<nibble>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0.
. Legs A and B. Run `CHK-BIND` first (the leaf makes a SEC_DIS go in leg B).
. Legs A and B. Stage `{~n, n}` as the `LC_STATE` byte and a filter-legal disable-vector pair. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Leg A, control epoch. Read `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor and at `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Sample `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`. Expect the profile row of the class of n (non-zero, by the vector filter) and `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` at 0.
. Leg A, first walk, no demotion. Read each demote pair (`lcc_demote_state_1_probe_o`, `lcc_demote_state_2_probe_o`) as the level of the epoch. For each pair mask m (1 to 15; bit i is the pair `{data[4+i], data[i]}` of the lifecycle decoder input) and each bad rail value v (both rails at 0 or both at 1), in the seeded order, run an inject epoch. Error injection. Point: `lc_sigint_inject_i` with `lc_sigint_pair_mask_i` = m and `lc_sigint_bad_value_i` = v. Kind: the selected pairs of the byte with both rails at v. Expected response: `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` = 1, `FEAT_CTRL` = 0 and the demote pairs at their level. Raise the inject. A sampler reads `lcc_sigint_err_probe_o`, `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` and both demote pair probes on every clock from the first clock the error reads 1 until the inject falls. Hold the inject for 16 clocks, read `FEAT_CTRL` (64 bit) once through the frontdoor, and lower the inject. Log `lc_state_o` in each epoch (`OBS-LC-PORT-SIGINT`). After the first epoch, log the state two clocks after the inject fell (`OBS-LC-SIGINT-RELEASE`).
. Leg A, demotion and second walk. Write the drawn demotion (`DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2` or both) with one drawn `rsvd` marker bit in each register (a marker-only write to the register that stays undemoted), each by a 64-bit write graded by readback; the write response is not graded. Read both pairs again as the demoted level, and log the undemoted and the demoted level of each register (`OBS-DEMOTE-PAIR-LEVEL`). Run the 30 epochs of step 5 again in the second seeded order. After the last epoch read `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` (64 bit) (`CHK-SIGINT-DEMOTE-REG`).
. Leg B. Raise the inject again on every pair with both rails at 0 (mask 0, value 0), with the drawn demotion still set. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` (`0x1093_0540`) and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 16 (`0x1093_0560`). Wait, bounded, until `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` (`0x1093_056C`) reads `0x15`. `security_disable_o` rises. If it does not rise, fail the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. Leg B. Read `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor. Expect all ones. Sample `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`; expect 1. Read every `dbg_disable_<name>_o` port and both fuse DFT ports as the open controls of `CHK-DBG-DISABLE` and `CHK-FUSE-DFT-CLASS`.
. Leg B. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 16. Wait, bounded, until `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x2a`. The code before this go is the `0x15` of the previous step, so a go that has no effect times out the wait. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect 0 with `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` still 1.
. Legs A and B. Lower `lc_sigint_inject_i`. Cold reset. Grade nothing after the input falls. Log `OBS-DTP-PORT-LEVEL` and grade the summary lines of `CHK-DBG-DISABLE` and `CHK-FUSE-DFT-CLASS`.
. Leg C. Leg C runs only in a leaf whose drawn nibble is `0000`: its image is the TEST_DEV image of legs A and B, with a filter-legal pair whose `SIP_DIS` has at least one 0 bit in each group, and a leaf with another nibble ends after step 10. When leg C runs, wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` after the cold reset of step 10.
. Leg C, control epoch (no injection). Read the drawn shadow word and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` over the JTAG master. Both answer OKAY. Sample `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o`; expect 0. Record the responses.
. Leg C, control epoch (no injection). Write 1 in the control bit of `SIP_DIS` over the JTAG master. The write answers OKAY. Read `SIP_DIS` (`0x1093_0018`) through the frontdoor. The control bit reads 1 and the drawn bit reads 0. Record the value. Write the count c1 with enable 0 to `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` (`0x1093_0414`) over the JTAG master. The write answers OKAY. Read the register through the frontdoor. It reads c1.
. Leg C, injection epoch. Error injection. Point: `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i`. Kind: both rails of the byte at the decoder input of the JTAG filter at 0. Expected response: `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o` = 1. Raise the injection. Wait, bounded, for the probe to read 1. If it stays 0, fail the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` and grade no JTAG access. Sample `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` and log it; it is not graded.
. Leg C. Read `SIP_DIS` through the frontdoor and record it. Repeat the two JTAG reads of the control epoch. Error injection. Point: the JTAG master. Kind: read of a shadow word and of an interface-control register while the filter error probe is 1. Expected response: a response that is not OKAY. The read data is not graded.
. Leg C. Write 1 in the drawn bit of `SIP_DIS` over the JTAG master. Error injection. Point: the JTAG master. Kind: write to a shadow register while the filter error probe is 1. Expected response: a response that is not OKAY and no change of `SIP_DIS` from the value recorded in the control epoch. Read `SIP_DIS` through the frontdoor.
. Leg C. Write the count c2 with enable 0 to `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` over the JTAG master. Error injection. Point: the JTAG master. Kind: write to an interface-control register while the filter error probe is 1. Expected response: a response that is not OKAY and no change of the register from c1. Read the register through the frontdoor.
. Leg C. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I` over the JTAG master, in the drawn word order. Read the first word written over the JTAG master. Write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 0. Read `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` over the JTAG master. Every access of this step answers OKAY. The read data is not graded.
. Leg C. Lower `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i`. Cold reset.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|Per the common rules of this bucket: the SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the SEC_DIS digest that the bench binds. This is a bench-binding precondition, not a specification value (common rules, SEC_DIS token). A bench whose bound digest is not that of `TOKEN_ZERO` fails the leaf before any SEC_DIS go. Anchor: the bound digest at the bench tap `sec_dis_digest_probe_o`, read by its simulator handle, against a SHA-256 of the 32 zero bytes. Control: none; the check grades the bench. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h> bound=0x<h> src=handle`.
|CHK-SIGINT
|Leg A walks the 30 pairs of pair mask (1 to 15; bit i is the pair `{data[4+i], data[i]}` of the lifecycle decoder input) and bad rail value (both rails 0, both rails 1) in a seeded order with no demotion, sets the drawn demotion (`DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2` or both), and walks the 30 pairs again in a second seeded order. In each epoch, on every clock from the first clock `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` (`lcc_sigint_err_probe_o`) reads 1 until the inject falls, `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` = 1 and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` = 0, and one `FEAT_CTRL` register read inside the epoch is 0 (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Lifecycle States and Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS): an integrity error forces the base feature vector to 0, after the demotion decode). Control: the epoch before the walk reads the non-zero profile row with `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` = 0. A DUT that keeps a profile bit for one broken pair, one rail value or with demotion set fails. The nibble `0111` runs as a control (`role=control`). Anchor: `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` on every clock, the `FEAT_CTRL` readback, `lcc_sigint_err_probe_o`. Evidence, one line per epoch: `CHK-SIGINT PASS seed=<n> n=0x<v> mask=0x<m> value=<0/1> demoted=<label> role=<new/control> clocks=<k> first_bad=none exp_feat=0x0 got_feat=0x0 got_probe=0x0 sigint_wait_clks=<c> applied_clks=<c> pre_sigint=0 pre_feat=0x<64b> ctl=<...>`.
|CTRL-SIGINT-EPOCHS
|The number of epochs whose stimulus reached the lifecycle decoder input (`u_lc_state_dec.data_i` shows exactly the pairs of the epoch's mask broken, both rails at its bad value) equals the number of epochs of the two walks (60). An epoch that the bench did not apply fails the leaf.
|CTRL-SIGINT-FRESH
|Control, not a check: before each epoch raises the inject, `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` reads 0 and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` reads the profile row of its walk (the undemoted row in the first walk, the row of the drawn demotion in the second; `lifecycle_controller.adoc`, Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile). Each epoch then starts from the state its own error must change, so an error that stays latched cannot carry the later epochs. The release of the error is not graded (no text states it): a DUT that keeps the error after the inject falls fails this control, and the leaf then gives no verdict on the later epochs. Evidence, one line per epoch: `CTRL-SIGINT-FRESH seen: seed=<n> n=0x<v> mask=0x<m> value=<0/1> demoted=<label> pre_sigint=0 pre_feat=0x<64b> exp_feat=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-SIGINT-PAIRS
|In each epoch, on every clock, each demote pair (`lcc_demote_state_1_probe_o`, `lcc_demote_state_2_probe_o`) equals its own level read before the walk (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Demotion: the demote bits hold until SEP cold reset; the pairs come from the bits). Control of the demoted walk: the pair of a demoted register differs from its level after the cold reset, and the pair of the other register does not. The levels are logged as `OBS-DEMOTE-PAIR-LEVEL`; no text states them.
|CHK-SIGINT-DEMOTE-REG
|In the last epoch of the demoted walk, the 64-bit `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` readbacks equal the value written before the walk: the drawn demote bit, one drawn `rsvd` marker bit, lock 0 (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Demotion; `sep_lifecycle_ctrl.rdl`, `DEMOTE_1`/`DEMOTE_2`, `rsvd` is `woset`). The same readback before the walk shows that the write landed.
|CHK-DBG-DISABLE
|In each epoch every `dbg_disable_<name>_o` port of a gated path (Case 1, 2 or 3 of the DTP path table) reads 1 (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS) and Debug and Test Port Path Gating; `doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc`, `dbg_disable_o` polarity). Control: under the leg B `TOKEN_ZERO` match every gated port reads 0. The gated ports are also sampled before each walk; the line carries `pre_open`, the number of (walk, port) pairs open before the walk, which are the ports the error is shown to close. Where every gated port reads 1 before both walks (for example nibble `1000`), the leaf logs `OBS-DBG-DISABLE-NODISC`: the check then grades the level only. One summary line after leg B carries the epoch count and the first mismatch.
|CHK-FUSE-DFT-CLASS
|In each epoch `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` and `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o` read their closed control. Controls from the same leaf: closed is the value at the first clock after the bring-up `rst_ni` release with fuse sense running, SEC_DIS 0 and the port at 0 or 1; open is the value under the leg B `TOKEN_ZERO` match; the two differ. No text states the level; it is logged as `OBS-DTP-PORT-LEVEL`. Each port is also sampled before each walk; `pre_open_walks` counts the walks that start with the port off its closed control. Where that count is 0, the error is not shown to move the port, and the leaf logs `OBS-FUSE-DFT-NODISC` for it. One line per port after leg B.
|CHK-SIGINT-SECDIS
|With the inject raised again on every pair (both rails 0) and the drawn demotion still set, with the SEC_DIS match, `FEAT_CTRL` is all ones with `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` still 1. After the `TOKEN_OTHER` mismatch, `FEAT_CTRL` returns to 0, so the all-ones value comes from the override (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS): the override applies after the integrity fail-closed step and wins). A DUT that lets the integrity error win over SEC_DIS shows 0 and fails. A DUT that holds all ones after the mismatch fails. Anchor: the `FEAT_CTRL` register readback, the ports `security_disable_o` and `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`, and the SEC_DIS match register. Control: `security_disable_o` rises on the match, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and the 0 before the match (CHK-SIGINT) is the contrast. Evidence: `CHK-SIGINT-SECDIS PASS seed=<n> n=0x<v> got_feat=0xffffffffffffffff sigint=1 sdis=1 after_mismatch=0x0 code_match=0x15 code_mismatch=0x2a`.
|OBS-LC-PORT-SIGINT
|A logged line per epoch, not a graded check: `lc_state_o` in the epoch and the legal `{~n, n}` byte. No text states the port under the error.
|OBS-LC-SIGINT-RELEASE
|A logged line once, not a graded check: after the first epoch's inject falls, the clocks until `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` falls, or `none`, and `FEAT_CTRL` with `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. No text states a release.
|OBS-DEMOTE-PAIR-LEVEL
|A logged line per register, not a graded check: the undemoted and the demoted level of `lcc_demote_state_<n>_probe_o`. No text states which complementary value means demoted.
|OBS-DTP-PORT-LEVEL
|A logged line, not a graded check: the open and closed level of each fuse DFT port, once both are known.
|CHK-JTAG-SIGINT
|Leg C, in a leaf that draws the nibble `0000`. Graded only when the injection is shown to reach the decoder of the JTAG filter: `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o` is 1 in the injection epoch. While the filter error probe is 1, a JTAG read of a shadow word and of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` answers a response that is not OKAY. The JTAG write to `SIP_DIS` answers a response that is not OKAY, and the frontdoor read of `SIP_DIS` equals its value from the control epoch, with the drawn bit still 0. The JTAG write of c2 to `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` answers a response that is not OKAY, and the frontdoor read of it equals c1. The token word write, the token word read, the go and the match read answer OKAY (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Debug and Test Port Path Gating, row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path: on an LC differential-decode integrity error the wrapper admits only the MMR region for reads and writes; the specification does not state that the wrapper uses the decoder of the lifecycle controller). The read data is not graded. A DUT that keeps the TEST_DEV policy under the error fails. Anchor: the JTAG response codes, the filter error probe and the frontdoor readback of `SIP_DIS`. Control: the control epoch, with the probe at 0, answers OKAY to the two reads (the open JTAG window test grades that cell) and answers OKAY to the JTAG write of the control bit and to the JTAG write of c1, and the frontdoor reads show that bit set and c1 in the register, so a JTAG write that never lands cannot pass the unchanged compares. A leaf whose injection epoch has the probe at 0 fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` (hook control). The control epoch logs `CTRL-JTAG-SIGINT-CONTROL seen: seed=<n> filter_err=0 ...`; a control epoch that does not show all of these logs `CTRL-MISSING JTAG-SIGINT-CONTROL` and fails the leaf. The `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` value of the injection epoch is logged as `OBS-LC-SIGINT-FILTER seed=<n> lc_sigint_err=<0/1>` and is not graded. A leaf with another nibble logs `CHK-JTAG-SIGINT N/A seed=<n> n=0x<v>`. Evidence: one line per access of the injection epoch, `CHK-JTAG-SIGINT PASS seed=<n> filter_err=1 tgt=<class> op=<rd/wr> exp=<allow/deny> resp=<code> sip_dis_before=0x<64b> sip_dis_after=0x<64b> ctl_resp=OKAY ctl_wr_resp=OKAY ctl_bit_set=1 ctl_cw_resp=OKAY ctl_cw_landed=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes, and `CHK-BIND`, `CHK-SIGINT`, `CHK-SIGINT-PAIRS`, `CHK-SIGINT-DEMOTE-REG`, `CHK-DBG-DISABLE`, `CHK-FUSE-DFT-CLASS` and `CHK-SIGINT-SECDIS` are required in every leaf. UVM_ERROR == 0. A leaf that draws the nibble `0000` requires a CHK-JTAG-SIGINT PASS line for each access of the injection epoch; a leaf with another nibble requires the CHK-JTAG-SIGINT N/A line. A leaf whose filter error probe stays 0 in the injection epoch fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.

*Scope limit*: The test injects corrupt values of both rails at 0 or at 1 on 1 to 4 pairs of the byte. It grades nothing after the injection input falls, because the specification states no release of `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`. It does not inject in an INVALID nibble, because the profile of INVALID is all zeros with and without the integrity logic. The profile of the 16 raw codes (legal and INVALID) belongs to leg A of `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`. The JTAG policy under the integrity error does not claim that the specification uses the same decoder in the filter as in the lifecycle controller: leg C injects at the decoder of the filter and does not use `lc_sigint_inject_i`. The open JTAG window tests keep the JTAG policy of the other states. The SEC_DIS revision enable is not set by the test (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>).

'''''

[[sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test]]
===== 6.21 `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all`, `cpu_stub` and `phase3_efuse_lifecycle`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. A leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the leaf plan (the follow-on leg, the raw state code and the strap of the leg A read), and the preload hook stages the image of that plan from the seed.

*Source Module*: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor): `FEAT_CTRL`, `DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, the token registers. Approved probes and ports: `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, `lcc_demote_state_1_o`, `lcc_demote_state_2_o`, the demotion lock probes, `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o`, `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o`, `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err`, `security_disable_o`, `secure_tm_req_i`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and the OTP image preload. A profile function written for this test from the Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile table and the disable-vector rules. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_ZERO` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. No inject. `otp_bank_probe_o` (the bank snapshot and diff of CHK-DEMOTE-OTP) and `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the post-guard statement of CHK-DEMOTE-OTP) are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Each leaf runs one leaf plan, which the seed draws in this order. First the follow-on leg, with the weights leg B 3, leg C 2, the RMA bypass of leg D 1, the earlier-owner cell of leg D 1, the walk of leg D 2 and leg E 2. Then the raw state code r that the leg allows: legs B and C draw r from the 16 raw codes with the weight 5 for each of TEST_DEV, PROD and PROD_END, 3 for each of the four RMA codes and 2 for each of the nine INVALID codes (45 in all; the X bit of an RMA code is part of r); the RMA bypass draws RMA_SIP or RMA_CHIPLET with its X bit; the earlier-owner cell draws TEST_DEV or PROD; the walk draws TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END or RMA_SIP (the X bit drawn); leg E takes TEST_DEV. Then the strap s of the leg A read, 0 or 1 with equal weight. Then the free dimensions: the disable-vector pair; in leg B the reset vector v (1 to 15, with half weight for the vectors 2 and 10, which the cell walk of the leg already takes into a reset), the register A (1 or 2), the count L in 6 to 12 and the L operations (each one of the operations that the Test Procedure lists for the leg B random sequence; an `rsvd` write draws its bit k in 2 to 63; a write that leaves the modelled register unchanged and the leg A SEC_DIS write also carry one `rsvd` marker bit that no write since the cold reset set, drawn from a stream salted from the seed), and the control bit b (a bit of the first `spare8` word that reads 0 in the image); in the earlier-owner cell the vector S; in the walk 16 of the 64 bit positions in a shuffled order, with double weight for the bits 0 to 4, 23 to 25, 47, 48 and 63, the owner of each other bit and the width of each write; in leg E the order in which the walk sets the five named bits. The bits 0 to 4, 23 to 25, 47, 48 and 63 take the owner `SIP_DIS` in TEST_DEV and `SYS_DIS` in PROD. A write takes one 64-bit access or two 32-bit accesses, drawn. Trap filters: the vector filter in every leaf except the walk, leg E and the earlier-owner cell; in each group `SIP_DIS` has a bit set and `SYS_DIS` has a bit set outside `SIP_DIS`, and in each debug group (DBG_1 and DBG_2) at least one bit lies outside both vectors; the earlier-owner cell stages `SIP_DIS` = S with a bit set in each group and `SYS_DIS` = 0; the L random operations include at least one set `demote`, set `lock` or set both operation. `reseed = 4` gives four leaf plans per regression, and every leaf grades one (raw code, strap) cell of leg A and one follow-on leg. These cells are seed-dependent and close over a merge of regressions. With 4 seeds the expected closure is about 35 regressions for the 16 raw codes of `x_raw16_sigint` (each INVALID code takes about 1 in 50 leaves), about 30 for the 15 reset vectors of leg B, and about 40 for every listed bin of the test; the case crosses of the two DFT ports close in about 6. The 32 combinations of leg E are not a coverage cell: at six combinations per leg E leaf they need about 50 regressions (`SEP_FCOV.adoc`, eFuse Bins not graded).

*Objective*: `FEAT_CTRL` equals the profile table row for every (raw state code, demotion, SEC_DIS) cell with a random disable-vector pair. All 16 raw codes give the profile of their class, with the X bit of the RMA codes part of the code, and the `LC_STATE` shadow word carries `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}` in bits [7:0]. Demotion has no effect in PROD_END, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET and INVALID. SEC_DIS gives all ones in every state. RMA bypass leaves both disable shadows unchanged. The Function group ignores demotion. `secure_tm_req_i` is not a term of `FEAT_CTRL`. Each bit of `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` closes the feature bit of the same index through `~(SIP_DIS OR SYS_DIS)` where both apply. The reserved DBG_1, DBG_2 and Function bits follow their group formula. Runtime shadow writes only add bits. A later owner cannot clear an earlier restriction. `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` are independent write-one-to-set registers: `demote` sets only while `lock` is clear, `lock` sets with `demote` at 0 or 1, a zero write clears nothing, cold reset clears both, a write needs no authentication, each demotion state is a differentially coded 2-bit output, and demotion does not change the OTP state. In TEST_DEV with demotion 0/0, `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` takes one value when Case 3 AND `SEP_FUSE_DBG` holds and the other value otherwise. `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o` takes one value when Case 2 AND `SMC_FUSE_DBG` holds and the other value otherwise. This holds over all 32 combinations of the five named disable bits, and no SMC-specific bit exists. The specification does not state the polarity of the two ports, so no check names a level (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, DFT fuse gate port polarity). Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections Lifecycle States, Debug and Functional Feature Control, Disable Vector Format (SIP_DIS / SYS_DIS), DIS Vector Ownership: Predecessor-Programs-Disables, Combining Rule: OR then Invert, When Each DIS Vector Applies, Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS), Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile, In-Field Debug Demotion, Programming and Locking, Reset Behavior, Debug and Test Port Path Gating, Feature-Bit to Scan-Chain Mapping); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (registers `LC_STATE`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc` (registers `FEAT_CTRL`, `DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections Fuse Fields, rows `LC_STATE`, `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS`; RTL Integration); `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` (sections Token Activation, Implementation Requirements); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` (ports `lcc_demote_state_1_o`, `lcc_demote_state_2_o`, `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o`, `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o`).

*Test Coverage*: Leg A, raw code and profile, in every leaf: the raw value of the leaf image at the drawn strap value, with no demotion, so that over a merge of regressions each of the 16 raw values runs at both strap values, the X bit of the RMA codes included. INVALID is read after `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` is high, so INVALID after sense is separated from "sense did not run". With the strap at 1 the cells equal the strap-0 row. Leg A then makes a `TOKEN_ZERO` match, writes `DEMOTE_1` under SEC_DIS, makes a `TOKEN_OTHER` mismatch, reads the demoted cell, and ends with a cold reset at strap 0. Leg B, demotion registers: a base state of each of the six classes, the four demotion cells (00, 10, 11, then 01 after a cold reset), the cold reset from each non-zero register vector (1 to 15), a random sequence of {set `demote`, set `lock`, set both, write 0, set one `rsvd` bit}, and a forced block in every leg B leaf (lock then demote on one register, demote then lock on the other, no reset between, a zero write after a set on both, a committed demote of 0 and of 1 each followed by a set and a zero write, and writes to the second register after the first is locked). Leg C, SEC_DIS: a `TOKEN_ZERO` match and a `TOKEN_OTHER` mismatch at each demotion cell of each state class. In RMA_CHIPLET the SEC_DIS cell is a control only. Leg D, bypass and owners: RMA bypass in RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET; the upper-bound gate on every read with SEC_DIS 0; a per-bit walk of 16 drawn bit positions in TEST_DEV, PROD (demoted), PROD_END or RMA_SIP, which covers all 64 bits of each state over a merge of regressions; the earlier-owner cell. Leg E, DFT fuse gate ports: a set-only walk in TEST_DEV with demotion 0/0 through six of the 32 combinations of {SEP_DBG 0, CHIPLET_DBG 1, SEP_FUSE_DBG 2, SMC_FUSE_DBG 3, SIP_DBG 24}, from all five open to all five closed. Every leg E leaf grades the gate formula on its drawn combinations. The test extends five open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_lcc_demote_feat_ctrl_matrix_test` walks TEST_DEV and PROD against the four demotion cells with a pinned disable pair and one PROD compose cell, locks one demotion group after its product walk and checks that a later demote write is ignored, and checks that the two DFT-inserted fuse-path disable ports follow `FEAT_CTRL` in its cells. `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test` grades the OR-merge and the rejected clear of every fuse-field shadow word except `LC_STATE`, including `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS`. `sep_lcc_uvm_inbound_filter_gating_test` grades that `DEMOTE_1` opens DBG_1 and that `DEMOTE_2` alone opens DBG_2 and leaves DBG_1 closed. `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` and the `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_*` tests walk the lifecycle states with a pinned vector. This entry adds the 16 raw codes and the six states with random vector pairs, SEC_DIS at each demotion cell, RMA bypass, the per-bit owner rule over all 64 bits, the earlier-owner rule, the lock and write rules of both demotion registers in both orders with the zero write after a set, the cold reset from each register vector, the output pair encoding and the invariance of the OTP state, and the case conjunction of the five named disable bits at the two fuse DFT ports on drawn combinations. The `FEAT_CTRL` effect of a single demotion is a control of the register rules. The open owners keep the rest. Phase 1 groups `sep_lc_state_cg`, `sep_feat_ctrl_cg` and `sep_lc_demote_cg` take hits. Functional coverage: `sep_lc_feat_ctrl_profile_cg`, `sep_lc_feature_bit_semantics_cg`, `sep_lc_sigint_failclosed_cg` (`x_raw16_sigint`), `sep_lc_demotion_cg`, `sep_lc_demote_lock_cg`, `sep_dtp_gating_ladder_cg`, `sep_sec_dis_activation_cg` (`cp_match_phase` after sense, `x_state_demote_forced`, and the Match and Mismatch cells of `x_verdict_out`, graded by CHK-FEAT-CELL in leg C) and `sep_sec_dis_robustness_cg` (`x_state_strap_feat`). The cells of all groups come from the drawn leaf plan, so they are seed-dependent and close over a merge of regressions (Randomization).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the leaf plan and the free dimensions (Randomization). Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n> leg=<leg> raw=0x<r> stm=<s>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value. Run leg A and then only the steps of the follow-on leg. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. Every read of `FEAT_CTRL` is a 64-bit frontdoor read, with a sample of `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` at the same time. `secure_tm_req_i` is the drawn strap s in leg A and 0 in every follow-on leg, because the specification blocks a shadow write and a fuse program while secure test mode is active (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection).
. Leg A, every leaf. Stage the image of the leaf plan: `{~r, r}` for the drawn raw value r, the vector pair of the follow-on leg (a filter-legal pair, or the exception that the leg states) and no lock bit. This is the only t=0 image of the leaf, and the follow-on leg uses it. Hold the drawn strap s from before reset release. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Leg A. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Sample `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` and the `LC_STATE` shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the profile row of the raw class, `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` at 0 and the shadow word `{~r, r}`. Leg A, SEC_DIS demotion, at the drawn strap: write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I`, write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 16 and wait for the match (`security_disable_o` must rise, else `CHK-HOOK FAIL`); write `demote` plus a marker bit to `DEMOTE_1`, read it back and read `FEAT_CTRL` (`CHK-SDIS-DEMOTE-SET`); write `TOKEN_OTHER` for a mismatch and run the cell check, which is the `DEMOTE_1` row of the state (`after=sdis_demote_mismatch`). End leg A with a cold reset with the strap at 0 and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`; the follow-on leg starts from that sense, with the strap at 0. A cold reset clears the demotion.
. Leg B. The image holds the drawn raw code, a filter-legal pair and no lock bit. Take the OTP bank snapshot at `otp_bank_probe_o`. Record as the undemoted reference: both output pairs, the `LC_STATE` shadow word and the profile row.
. Leg B. Define the check cycle. After an operation, read `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` (bits [63:2] read the `rsvd` bits written since the cold reset), read `FEAT_CTRL`, sample both output pairs and both demotion lock probes, sample the `LC_STATE` shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, take the OTP bank diff against the snapshot at `otp_bank_probe_o`, and record the program commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. Compare each with the golden.
. Leg B, cell walk. Run a check cycle (cell 00). Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_1`; run a check cycle (cell 10). Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_2`; run a check cycle (cell 11). Log the register vector. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, cell walk. Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_2` only; run a check cycle (cell 01). Log the register vector. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, reset vector. The register vector is (demote 1 as bit 3, lock 1, demote 2, lock 2 as bit 0) = v. From the clean state, write only the bits of v: for each register, in the order register 1, register 2, write `0x1` first when its demote bit is in v and then `0x2` when its lock bit is in v. Run a check cycle after each write. The path never overshoots, because it writes no bit outside v and writes demote before lock. Log the vector. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, random sequence. Draw the L operations from {set `demote` (write `0x1`), set `lock` (write `0x2`), set both (write `0x3`), write 0, set one bit k of `rsvd` (write `1 << k`, k in 2 to 63)} on a drawn register. Run a check cycle after each. After every set-both write, log the demote bit with `OBS-DEMOTE-LOCK-ONE-WRITE`. Log the vector. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, forced block. Take the drawn register A and the other register B. On A: set `lock`, then set `demote`, then write 0, then set `lock` again. On B, after A is locked: set `demote`, write 0, set `lock`, then set `demote` and write 0. Run a check cycle after each write. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor write to `DEMOTE_1` or `DEMOTE_2`. Kind: a `demote` write after `lock`, and a zero write after a set. Expected response: no change of the register. Log the vector (non-zero: both locks are set). Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, control program. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1 and `efuse_addr` = the bit address of a drawn bit b of the first word of `spare8` that reads 0 in the staged image (8 times the byte offset `0x3E0` plus b; the field is unlocked, and its word is not all ones, so such a bit exists). Write the register again with `efuse_program_go` = 1 in the same write as the other fields. Poll `program_done` until it reads 1. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read the first `spare8` shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`: bit b reads 1, and the `LC_STATE` shadow word equals the undemoted reference. Take the OTP bank diff against the snapshot at `otp_bank_probe_o`; it shows bit b and no other bit. Record the program commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the go write to the `program_done` read; one program command with the address of bit b shows.
. Leg C. Run `CHK-BIND` first. The image holds the drawn raw code and a filter-legal pair.
. Leg C. Reach demotion cell 00 (no write). Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect the profile row with SEC_DIS at 0.
. Leg C, SEC_DIS cell. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 16. Wait until `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`. On the first match of the leaf, `security_disable_o` must rise, else fail the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect all ones.
. Leg C, SEC_DIS cell. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 16. Wait until `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x2a`. The code before this go is the `0x15` of the previous step, so a go that has no effect times out the wait. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect the profile row of the cell (SEC_DIS 0) and `security_disable_o` low.
. Leg C. Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_1` (cell 10). Read `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` and `FEAT_CTRL`. Run the SEC_DIS cell of steps 14 and 15. Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_2` (cell 11). Read `DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2` and `FEAT_CTRL`. Run the SEC_DIS cell again. Each demotion write must be answered OKAY and both registers must read the RW1S golden (`sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc`, `DEMOTE_1`/`DEMOTE_2`), in the CHK-FEAT-CELL line of the cell.
. Leg C. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read `FEAT_CTRL` and run the cell check with both demotion registers reading 0 (`after=reset`). Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_2` only (cell 01). Read `DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2` and `FEAT_CTRL`, graded as in step 16. Run the SEC_DIS cell again. Cold reset.
. Leg D, RMA bypass. The image holds RMA_SIP or RMA_CHIPLET, with its X bit, and a filter-legal pair. Read `SIP_DIS` (`0x1093_0018`) and `SYS_DIS` (`0x1093_0020`) as 64-bit reads. Read `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg D, earlier owner. The image holds TEST_DEV or PROD with `SIP_DIS` = S, a vector with a bit set in each group, and `SYS_DIS` = 0 (an exception to the vector filter: the leaf writes `SYS_DIS`). In PROD, write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_1` and to `DEMOTE_2`. Read `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg D, earlier owner. Write the bitwise complement of S to `SYS_DIS`. Write 0 to `SIP_DIS`. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor write of 0 to `SIP_DIS`. Kind: a clear write to a write-one-to-set register. Expected response: no change. Read `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS` and `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg D, walk. The image holds TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END or RMA_SIP with `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` both 0 (an exception to the vector filter: the walk builds the vectors by writes), with no lock bit. In PROD, write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_1` and to `DEMOTE_2`. Read `FEAT_CTRL` (the start row).
. Leg D, walk. For each of the 16 drawn positions b, in the shuffled order: take the owner (pinned or drawn) and the write width. Write `1 << b` to the owner shadow, as one 64-bit write or as two 32-bit writes (the word without the bit gets 0). Read the owner shadow and `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg D, walk. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS` and `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg E. The image holds TEST_DEV, `SIP_DIS` = 0 and `SYS_DIS` = 0 (an exception to the vector filter: the walk builds the vector by writes) and no lock bit. Control cell 1 (all five named bits 0): read `FEAT_CTRL` and sample `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` and `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o`. Then set the five named bits one at a time in the drawn order: write `1 << b` to `SIP_DIS` (a set-only shadow write, legal because the leaf runs in TEST_DEV with every lock bit 0 and the strap at 0), read `SIP_DIS` and `FEAT_CTRL`, and sample both ports. The last write gives control cell 2 (all five named bits 1). The walk visits six of the 32 combinations, one for each count of set bits, with no further cold reset.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|Per the common rules of this bucket: the SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the SEC_DIS digest that the bench binds. This is a bench-binding precondition, not a specification value (common rules, SEC_DIS token). A leg C leaf runs it before the first SEC_DIS go. Anchor: the bound digest at the bench tap `sec_dis_digest_probe_o`, read by its simulator handle, against a SHA-256 of the 32 zero bytes. Control: none; the check grades the bench. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h> bound=0x<h> src=handle`.
|CHK-FEAT-CELL
|`FEAT_CTRL` equals the 64-bit profile row for each (raw code, demotion, SEC_DIS, strap) cell, and the `LC_STATE` shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` equals `{~raw, raw}` with `sep_ras_o.lc_sigint_err` low; `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` read back the RW1S golden (a write of 1 to a `DEMOTE` bit sets it and a write of 0 leaves it, `sep_lifecycle_ctrl.rdl`, `DEMOTE`; the demotion outputs are differentially encoded, a complementary bit pair, `otp_fuse_controller.adoc:431-433`, section RTL Integration), and each demotion output pair is complementary and differs from the undemoted reference pair (the pair of the first leg A cell with both registers 0) exactly when its register is demoted. The reference pair is read from the DUT because no document states which of the two complementary values means demoted: `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (RTL Integration) says only that the controller differentially encodes its two demotion outputs. The check therefore grades the encoding (complementary) and the change on demotion, not the absolute value (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile, Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS); `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`). A DUT that decodes a raw value to the wrong class, ignores the X bit, applies demotion in INVALID, forgets `SYS_DIS` in TEST_DEV, lets the strap open a bit or applies the wrong group formula fails. A leg C cell right after the first cold reset (`after=reset`) reads both demotion registers 0 and both pairs equal to the reference. In leg C the SEC_DIS 1 cell is all ones with `security_disable_o` high, and the cell after the mismatch is the row of the demotion cell with `security_disable_o` low. Leg A also grades the cell after the SEC_DIS demotion step (`after=sdis_demote_mismatch`): the `DEMOTE_1` row of the state with `security_disable_o` low. A leg C demotion cell also requires the write response OKAY and both `DEMOTE` registers equal to the RW1S golden, so a lost write fails also in a state whose row does not depend on demotion (fields `wr_resp`, `demote1_exp`, `demote1_rd`, `demote2_exp`, `demote2_rd`). Anchor: the `FEAT_CTRL` register readback and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` (both equal the golden), and the shadow probe. Control: the undemoted cell of the same image shows the closed bits that the demoted or SEC_DIS cell opens; in RMA_CHIPLET the SEC_DIS cell is all ones in both rows and is logged as a control with `ctl=1`. Hook control of the strap at the leg A read: the bench net `test_en_strap_i` (the DUT port `secure_tm_req_i`) and the latched `secure_tm_o` both equal the drawn strap s, logged as `CTRL-HOOK seen: seed=<n> reason=strap stm=<s> strap_port=<s>`; a miss fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL reason=strap`. Evidence: `CHK-FEAT-CELL PASS seed=<n> leg=<a/c> raw=0x<r> state=<name> dem=<d1d2> dem_regs=<r1>/<r2> dem_exp=<r1>/<r2> pairs=<p1>/<p2> pairs_ref=<p1>/<p2\|na> secdis=<0/1> stm=<0/1> sip=0x<64b> sys=0x<64b> exp=0x<64b> got=0x<64b> lc_shadow=0x<b> sigint=0 row=<profile row> sdis_port=<0/1/na> ctl=<0/1>`.
|CHK-RMA-BYPASS
|In RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET both disable shadows read the staged values and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the described row: `~SIP_DIS` per group in RMA_SIP and all ones in RMA_CHIPLET (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections DIS Vector Ownership: Predecessor-Programs-Disables, When Each DIS Vector Applies). A DUT that erases a disable shadow on RMA entry fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` and of `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: the vector filter makes the staged pair non-zero in each group, so an erased shadow differs from the staged value, and in RMA_SIP `~SIP_DIS` differs from `~(SIP_DIS OR SYS_DIS)`; the leaf logs the pair as `CTRL-RMA-BYPASS-PAIR seen: seed=<n> sip=0x<64b> sys=0x<64b>`, and a pair without these properties logs `CTRL-MISSING RMA-BYPASS-PAIR` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-RMA-BYPASS PASS seed=<n> state=<name> shadow_sip=0x<64b> shadow_sys=0x<64b> staged_equal=1 feat=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-GATE-BOUND
|On every read of `FEAT_CTRL` with SEC_DIS 0 and a mask that is not all ones, no bit of `FEAT_CTRL` lies outside the upper-bound mask of the state. The mask is the OR of the SEC_DIS = 0 rows of that state in the profile table, built with the model shadow values (the staged image and the OR-merge of the writes of the leaf), not with a shadow read-back. The SEC_DIS = 1 rows are all ones and are not part of the mask (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile). A DUT that opens a bit that no row opens fails. Anchor: the `FEAT_CTRL` register readback against the mask from the model shadow values; the shadow readback is graded against the same model in CHK-DIS-STEP and CHK-RMA-BYPASS. A read whose mask is all ones cannot show a stray bit and is not graded: it logs `INFO-GATE-BOUND LOG seed=<n> state=<name> mask=all_ones graded=0`. That holds for every read in RMA_CHIPLET and for a read in TEST_DEV or RMA_SIP with a zero vector (the start row of the walk) or with only the five named bits set (leg E). Control: for a filter-legal pair the mask is not all ones in TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END, RMA_SIP and INVALID, so a stray bit shows in the legs that stage such a pair. Evidence: `CHK-GATE-BOUND PASS seed=<n> state=<name> mask=0x<64b> feat=0x<64b> extra_bits=0x0`.
|CHK-DIS-STEP
|After each walk write the owner shadow equals the OR-merge golden and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the row for the cumulative vectors. In RMA_SIP a `SYS_DIS` bit changes the shadow and not `FEAT_CTRL`. After the cold reset that ends the walk, both shadows read 0 (the staged image) and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the undemoted row of that image (`sep_efuse_map.adoc`, registers `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS`: write-one-to-set; `lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Combining Rule: OR then Invert, Reset Behavior). A DUT that clears a bit, overwrites the register, ignores a reserved group bit or keeps a write over reset fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of the owner shadow and of `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: the bits 0 to 4, 23 to 25, 47, 48 and 63 take a pinned owner, and over a merge of regressions the drawn positions cover all 64 bits of each state; the start row has the bit open, so each write shows as a closed bit (where the profile exposes it). Before the first walk write, `FEAT_CTRL` (register and probe) equals the start row, logged as `CTRL-DIS-START seen: seed=<n> state=<name> feat=0x<64b> feat_exp=0x<64b>`; a miss logs `CTRL-MISSING DIS-START` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-DIS-STEP PASS seed=<n> state=<name> step=<k> bit=<b> owner=<SIP/SYS> wr=0x<64b> shadow_exp=0x<64b> shadow_got=0x<64b> feat_exp=0x<64b> feat_got=0x<64b>`; `CHK-DIS-STEP PASS seed=<n> state=<name> step=reset sip=0x0 sys=0x0 feat_exp=0x<64b> feat_got=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-DIS-EARLIER
|A later owner cannot clear an earlier restriction: `SIP_DIS` reads S after the write of 0 and the write of the complement of S to `SYS_DIS`, and `FEAT_CTRL` AND S is 0 (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Combining Rule: OR then Invert). In PROD, after the two demotion writes, `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` read back the RW1S golden. The leaf fails if S is 0. A DUT that lets the write of 0 clear `SIP_DIS` shows `FEAT_CTRL` = S and fails. Both writes are legal (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields: `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` are SW writable set-only, and no lock bit is set), so each is answered OKAY (`resp_sys_wr=0`, `resp_sip_wr=0`); a dropped write of 0 fails on its response. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS` and `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: S has a bit set in each group by the staging rule of the cell, and the complement written to `SYS_DIS` closes every bit outside S, so `FEAT_CTRL` can be non-zero only inside S. Evidence: `CHK-DIS-EARLIER PASS seed=<n> state=<name> S=0x<64b> sip_got=0x<64b> sys=0x<64b> feat=0x<64b> feat_and_S=0x0 dem_regs=0x<v>/0x<v> dem_exp=0x<v>/0x<v>`.
|CHK-DEMOTE-STEP
|After each operation both registers (frontdoor readback) equal the golden: `demote` sets only if `lock` = 0, `lock` sets always, zero changes nothing, and each `rsvd` bit [63:2] is write-one-to-set with no lock and holds every bit written since the cold reset (`hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_lifecycle_ctrl/sep_lifecycle_ctrl.rdl:40-45`; `sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc`, registers `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2`, access RW1S; `lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Programming and Locking). A DUT that lets `demote` change after `lock`, clears on a zero write or couples the two registers fails. `FEAT_CTRL` equals the profile row for the cumulative demotion; the `rsvd` bits are "Not used" and do not move it. A write of both bits while `lock` is 0 has no stated demote golden: the demote bit of that readback is not graded, the model takes it from the readback, and the lock bit and `FEAT_CTRL` stay graded. A write of both bits while `lock` is 1 leaves `demote` unchanged. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of both registers and of `FEAT_CTRL`; a write whose expected result is "no change" carries a marker bit that CHK-DEMOTE-MARK grades, and the model holds that bit. The write response is not graded (`OBS-LCC-WR-RESP`). Control: in every sequence the second register sets `demote` while its lock is clear and after the first register is locked. In TEST_DEV and PROD the `FEAT_CTRL` after a committed demote differs from the undemoted one, because the vector filter leaves a bit closed in each group and a bit open in each debug group; the leaf logs that contrast as `CTRL-DEMOTE-STEP-CONTRAST seen: seed=<n> state=<name> undemoted=0x<64b> demote_1=0x<64b> demote_2=0x<64b>`, and a golden without it logs `CTRL-MISSING DEMOTE-STEP-CONTRAST` and fails the leaf. In PROD_END, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET and INVALID demotion has no effect, so `FEAT_CTRL` equals the undemoted row after every operation. Evidence: `CHK-DEMOTE-STEP PASS seed=<n> step=<k> reg=<1/2> op=<set_demote/set_lock/set_both/zero/rsvd.<k>> reg_exp=0x<v> reg_got=0x<v> feat_exp=0x<64b> feat_got=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-LC-STATE-PORT
|In every CHK-FEAT-CELL cell the `lc_state_o` port (tap `lc_state_probe_o`) equals the `LC_STATE` shadow byte at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}` of the leaf raw code (`doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc`, `lc_state_o`; `port_table.adoc`, `lc_state_o`; `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`; `sep_efuse_map.rdl`, `LC_STATE.lc_state`). Over a merge of regressions each of the 16 raw codes runs, INVALID codes included. A DUT that drives a stale, swapped or partial byte fails. Anchor: the DUT port tap and the shadow probe. Evidence: `CHK-LC-STATE-PORT PASS seed=<n> leg=<a/c> raw=0x<r> lc_state_o=0x<b> lc_shadow=0x<b> exp=0x<b> secdis=<0/1> after=<cell>`.
|CHK-SDIS-DEMOTE-SET
|Leg A, every leaf: after a `TOKEN_ZERO` match raises `security_disable_o`, a write of `demote` to `DEMOTE_1` (lock 0, with a marker bit) reads back `demote` 1 and `lock` 0, and `FEAT_CTRL` (register and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`) is all ones with `security_disable_o` high at the write and at the read. `demote` sets while `lock` is 0 and the rule has no SEC_DIS term (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Programming and Locking; `sep_lifecycle_ctrl.rdl`, `DEMOTE_1.demote`); SEC_DIS forces all ones (section Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS)). After a `TOKEN_OTHER` mismatch, the next CHK-FEAT-CELL (`after=sdis_demote_mismatch`) is the `DEMOTE_1` row of the state, which differs from the undemoted row in TEST_DEV and PROD. A DUT that blocks the demote write under SEC_DIS fails. Leg A ends with a cold reset at strap 0, which clears the demotion before the follow-on leg. Evidence: `CHK-SDIS-DEMOTE-SET PASS seed=<n> state=<name> stm=<s> sdis_port=1/1 demote1_rd=0x<64b> demote=1 lock=0 feat=0x<64b> probe=0x<64b> exp=0x<64b> row=<row>`.
|CHK-DEMOTE-MARK
|Every `DEMOTE_1`/`DEMOTE_2` write that the RW1S golden leaves without effect (a `demote` write after `lock`, a repeated `lock`, a zero write, a both-bits write after `lock`, a repeated `rsvd` bit), and the leg A SEC_DIS write, also sets one `rsvd` bit k in [63:32] that no write since the cold reset set, drawn with `SepSeededRng`; the low 32-bit lane carries the operation value alone. The 64-bit readback right after the write has bit k set (`sep_lifecycle_ctrl.rdl`, `DEMOTE_1`/`DEMOTE_2` `rsvd`: write-one-to-set, no write enable). This proves that a write with no other effect reached the register. A dropped write fails. Evidence: `CHK-DEMOTE-MARK PASS seed=<n> reg=<1/2> op=<op> applied=<0/1> mark_bit=<k> got=1 readback=0x<64b>`.
|OBS-LCC-WR-RESP
|A logged line for every `DEMOTE_1`/`DEMOTE_2` write, not a graded check, with `applied=0` for a write the RW1S golden leaves without effect. No document states the response of a write the block does not apply.
|OBS-DEMOTE-PAIR-LEVEL
|A logged line once per register and leaf, not a graded check: the undemoted and the demoted level of `lcc_demote_state_<n>_probe_o`. No document states which complementary value means demoted.
|OBS-DEMOTE-LOCK-ONE-WRITE
|A logged line for every set-both write, not a graded check: the register, whether `lock` is already 1 before the write, the demote and lock bits of the readback, `FEAT_CTRL` and its probe. The specification does not state whether `demote` takes effect when one write sets it with `lock`. Evidence: `OBS-DEMOTE-LOCK-ONE-WRITE seed=<n> reg=<1/2> pre_lock=<0/1> demote_rd=<0/1> lock_rd=<0/1> feat=0x<64b> probe=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-DEMOTE-ENC
|Each output pair (port probe) is complementary, the pair of a demoted register differs from the pair of an undemoted one, and each lock bit probe equals the lock bit of the register readback (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section RTL Integration: the lifecycle controller differentially encodes its two demotion outputs; `port_table.adoc`, ports `lcc_demote_state_1_o`, `lcc_demote_state_2_o`). The test names no level of a pair. A DUT with a non-complementary pair, a pair that does not change on a demotion, or a lock probe that differs from the register fails. Anchor: the port probes and the register readback. Control: the undemoted reference pair is recorded after sense, so the demoted pair is compared with a pair that the same leaf sampled. Evidence: `CHK-DEMOTE-ENC PASS seed=<n> reg=<1/2> undemoted=0b<xx> demoted=0b<xx> complementary=1 distinct=1 lock_probe=<0/1> lock_reg=<0/1>`.
|CHK-DEMOTE-OTP
|The `LC_STATE` shadow word equals the staged byte `{~raw, raw}` of the image (the undemoted reference) after every operation and after every cold-reset sense of the leg, on every check cycle; the shadow word read at the start of leg B equals the staged byte, and every later read equals that start word in all 32 bits, because demotion does not change the OTP state (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Lifecycle States). The bank keeps every program over reset, and a program reaches the shadow at the next sense (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection), so a demotion that burns an `LC_STATE` fuse shows at the next sense. A DUT that burns an `LC_STATE` fuse or changes the shadow word on a demotion fails. Anchor: the `LC_STATE` word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Control: one frontdoor program of a `spare8` bit that reads 0 in the staged image, at the end of leg B, shows as 1 in the `spare8` shadow word after the next sense, so a burn can show at a resense and the control cannot pass without the burn. From the first check cycle of leg B to the end of the forced block, the check grades that no bank bit changes (the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` is none) and that no program command reaches the post-guard port (`efuse_shim_command_req_o`); the control program is outside this window. Control on the same taps: the control program of bit b shows one program command with the address of bit b at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`, and the bank diff after it shows bit b and no other bit; a missing command or a missing bit fails the check. The reference is the staged byte of the image (`{~raw, raw}` masked to the `LC_STATE` field), not a value read from the DUT. Evidence: `CHK-DEMOTE-OTP PASS seed=<n> lc_shadow=0x<v> ref=0x<v> lc_shadow_after_sense=0x<v> cycles=<k> violations=0 first_violation=none ctl_bit=<b> ctl_spare8_staged=0x<w> ctl_spare8=0x<w> ctl_cmd=1 ctl_cmds=<c> ctl_done_reads=<k> ctl_diff=<a>`.
|CHK-DEMOTE-RESET
|After a cold reset both registers read 0, and `FEAT_CTRL` and both pairs are at the undemoted reference (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections In-Field Debug Demotion, Reset Behavior). A DUT that keeps state over reset fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of both registers, `FEAT_CTRL` and the port probes after `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Control: the vector before the reset is non-zero and logged, and the reset follows a state that differs from the clean state. Evidence: `CHK-DEMOTE-RESET PASS seed=<n> stage=<cell/vec/rand/forced> vec_before=0x<v> regs=0x0 feat=0x<64b> pairs_ref=1`.
|CHK-DTP-FEAT
|In leg E, `FEAT_CTRL` bit k is the inverse of disable bit k for each of the five named bits (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Disable Vector Format (SIP_DIS / SYS_DIS), Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile: TEST_DEV with demotion 0/0 gives `~(SIP_DIS OR SYS_DIS)`). A DUT that maps a bit to the wrong index or drops the inversion fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: the staged `SYS_DIS` is 0, so each named bit closes its feature bit through `SIP_DIS` alone, and the walk starts with all five bits open and ends with all five closed, so each named bit is read at both values in every leg E leaf. Evidence: `CHK-DTP-FEAT PASS seed=<n> bits=0b<SEP,CHP,SEPF,SMCF,SIP> feat_bits=0b<xxxxx> feat=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-DTP-PORT
|In leg E, `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` has one value on every cell where SIP_DBG, CHIPLET_DBG, SEP_DBG and SEP_FUSE_DBG are all open and the other value on every other cell. `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o` has one value on every cell where SIP_DBG, CHIPLET_DBG and SMC_FUSE_DBG are all open and the other value on every other cell (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Debug and Test Port Path Gating, rows DFT-inserted SEP fuse path and DFT-inserted SMC fuse path; `port_table.adoc`). No check names a level or compares a port with a reference taken from the DUT: the cells compare with each other by case class. A DUT that drops a term, adds an SMC bit or ties the port constant fails. Anchor: the two port values per cell, compared with the port values of the two control cells of the same leaf. Control: the all-open cell and the all-closed cell of the same leaf show different values on each port (`CTRL-DTP-PORT-CLASSES seen: seed=<n> ctl_open=<0/1,0/1> ctl_closed=<0/1,0/1>`), else `CTRL-MISSING DTP-PORT-CLASSES` fails the leaf; so both classes of each port are non-empty in every leg E leaf. The port levels of the all-open and the all-closed cell are logged once per leaf (`OBS-DTP-PORT-LEVEL`, with `sep_open`, `sep_closed`, `smc_open` and `smc_closed`) and not graded. Evidence: `CHK-DTP-PORT PASS seed=<n> bits=0b<SEP,CHP,SEPF,SMCF,SIP> sep_port=<0/1> sep_class=<open_case/other> smc_port=<0/1> smc_class=<open_case/other>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test grades no demote bit of a write of `0x3` to a demotion register while `lock` is 0, because the specification states no effect for it (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); it logs that bit. It grades the write-one-to-set storage of bits [63:2] and grades no effect of them on any other output, because the register description marks them "Not used". It names no level of the two DFT fuse gate ports and no polarity of the demotion output pair. It runs the leg C SEC_DIS cell only at strap 0. It sets no SEC_DIS revision enable. The leg A SEC_DIS demotion step presents its token at the drawn strap and grades `DEMOTE_1`, `FEAT_CTRL` and `security_disable_o`, not `secure_tm_o`; no other leg presents a token with the strap at 1 (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The shadow write and program legs hold the strap at 0, and the effect of secure test mode on a write belongs to `sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test`. Leg E does not grade demotion, owner veto or RMA bypass. The meaning of a debug-disable bit at the DTP boundary is not graded. The open tests named in Test Coverage keep the cells that they grade.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test]]
===== 6.22 `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. A leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the enable and the start state of that image (the preload hook stages it from the seed). Every leaf runs leg A. A leaf whose image has `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 1 and starts in TEST_DEV or PROD also runs leg S after leg A. A leaf whose image is TEST_DEV with `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 0 runs leg B after leg A on the same image. Leg S and leg B never run in the same leaf. A leaf with another image logs `INFO-TRANSIENT-SIMUL-SKIP`.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor): the token registers, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `FEAT_CTRL`. Approved: `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, the OTP image preload and the direct OTP word read through the frontdoor read interface (`sep_efuse_direct_read_seq`; the read interface is not under test here). A SHA-256 model for the digest staging. A profile function written for this test from the Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile table. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. No inject. `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the post-guard statements of CHK-TRANSIENT-NOBURN and CHK-PROG-LC) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The draws are: the pair (enable, start state), where the pair (0, TEST_DEV) takes a weight of 1 in 3, because only that image also runs leg B, and the other nine pairs of enable {0, 1} and start state {TEST_DEV, PROD, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, INVALID (raw `0101`)} share the other 2 in 3 with equal weight; the token script, one of {SIP only, CHIPLET only, CHIPLET then SIP, SIP then CHIPLET} with equal weight; the X bit of an RMA start state; the disable-vector pair; and the free spare bit of the window control. Trap filters: one go bit per write; no program before sense done; the vector filter. `reseed = 4` gives four images per regression, and a regression runs leg B with a probability of about 0.8 (1 - (2/3)^4). The (enable, channel, start state) cells of leg A are seed-dependent and close over a merge of regressions, about 16 regressions for the 20 cells of `x_transient`.

*Objective*: With `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 1 a token match updates the `LC_STATE` shadow without a bus write. The chiplet path needs RMA_SIP set, and a CHIPLET match without it changes nothing. With `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 0 a match leaves `LC_STATE` unchanged and the result code still reads Match. A cold reset restores the sensed state. The update burns no fuse. A program of `LC_STATE` bit 1 lands in the OTP bank only when the RMA_SIP token has a match, and a program of bit 2 lands only when the RMA_CHIPLET token has a match. Bit 1 is set before every bit 2 cell of this leaf. A program with only the other channel matched changes no bank bit. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization; Fuse Fields, rows `LC_STATE` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` (sections Token Submission, Post Token Matching Handling); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (register `LC_STATE`); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (register `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (section Lifecycle States, row TEST_DEV, which places the token-digest programming). The specification does not say whether a retained match acts again after a cold reset with the enable at 1, or whether a blocked CHIPLET match acts after a later SIP match sets bit 1. So every match in this leaf is replaced by a mismatch go on the same channel before the next cold reset and before a later SIP step, except in leg S, which holds a CHIPLET match while the SIP match is presented and ends both matches with mismatch gos before the cold reset. With `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 1 a SIP match presented while a CHIPLET match is held, from TEST_DEV or PROD, does not update `LC_STATE` (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc:232-238`).

*Test Coverage*: Leg A, transient update: `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0 and 1, staged through the OTP image (the register has access R); five start states; four token scripts (`TOKEN_T1` on SIP, `TOKEN_T2` on CHIPLET); a CHIPLET match with no RMA_SIP state; the result code with the enable at 0; the reset restore; the absence of a fuse burn; a window control in the same leaf (a program of a free spare bit that lands inside the window W). Leg S, simultaneous match: a CHIPLET match alone, then a SIP match while the CHIPLET match is held, from TEST_DEV or PROD with the enable at 1; then a SIP match alone right after the script (CHK-TRANSIENT-AFTER-BLOCK); a cold reset; then a SIP match alone that lands RMA_SIP, and a cold reset that restores the sensed state. Leg B, program authorization: a TEST_DEV image with the enable at 0; a program of `LC_STATE` bit 1 with only the CHIPLET token matched and then with the SIP token matched; a program of bit 2 with only the SIP token matched and then with the CHIPLET token matched. The test extends three open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_transient_test` walks the transient update from one start state, and `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test` walks the SIP and CHIPLET match from PROD and grades the refusal of an `LC_STATE` program with a wrong token. `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test` grades the write-locked refusal of a program. This entry adds the enable 0 and 1 matrix, five start states, four token scripts, a CHIPLET match with no RMA_SIP state, the result code with the enable at 0, the reset restore, the absence of a fuse burn, and the program of `LC_STATE` bits 1 and 2 with only the other channel matched against the program with the matching channel. The program of bit 2 with only the SIP channel matched adds only the held SIP Match read to the refusal that `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test` grades with a mismatched CHIPLET token, so it runs as a control with `role=control`. The program of bit 1 with only the CHIPLET channel matched is a new cell. The open owners keep the rest. Phase 1 group `sep_lc_transition_cg` takes hits. Functional coverage: `sep_lc_illegal_transition_cg` (seed-dependent: `x_transient`, one drawn (enable, start state) pair and script per leaf, closed over a merge of regressions) `sep_efuse_lock_policy_cg` (`x_lc_prog_gate`, except the cell of bit 2 with only the other channel matched, which is a control here), `cp_simultaneous_match` (`blocked_from_test_dev`, `blocked_from_prod`) and `cp_reset_restore` (`sensed_word_restored`). The owner window opens after the window control and stays open to the end of the leaf, except for the lone SIP match of leg S with its cold reset and the bit-2 control program of leg B. Each legal outcome of the four points is held as pending and samples only on the rise of its bit of a pass label that the leaf raises after the check that grades it passed (CHK-TRANSIENT, CHK-TRANSIENT-SIMUL, CHK-TRANSIENT-RESET, CHK-PROG-LC). The first FAIL closes the window for the rest of the leaf and drops every pending outcome. `cp_simultaneous_match` comes only from leg S (about 15 percent of the seeds) and `x_lc_prog_gate` only from leg B, so both are seed-dependent and close over a merge of regressions; `cp_reset_restore` fills in every leaf whose leg A updates the shadow and in every leg S leaf.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the values of the Randomization field from the seed. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n> en=<0/1> start=<name> script=<s> leg_b=<0/1>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. A cold reset in this leaf follows a mismatch go on every channel that holds a match.
. Digest staging. Define the digest staging that every stage step of this leaf uses. Stage the RMA digests by the digest rule of this bucket: `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_DIGEST` as the digest of `TOKEN_T1` and `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_DIGEST` as the digest of `TOKEN_T2`. The packing is a stimulus choice, and no check grades it. The first Match read of each channel in the leaf is the control of that staging. A first Match read that shows another code fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and the later steps are not graded.
. Legs A and B. Stage the image of the leaf, which is its only t=0 image: the drawn enable and start state (the X bit drawn for an RMA state), the digests and a filter-legal pair. Leg B runs only when this image is TEST_DEV with `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 0, and it starts after the cold reset and sense of step 9; a leaf with another image ends after step 9. Preload. Cold reset. Measure the sense time from reset release to `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. The idle window W is a multiple of that time (the multiple is a test parameter). Log `INFO-TIME LOG seed=<n> t_sense=<t> w_cycles=<w> mult=<m>`. Every "never" wait below lasts W. Define the program wait. After the go write of a program, wait the full W with no early exit, because nothing shows that `program_busy` has risen before the first poll. Then read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and require `program_busy` 0. For a program that must land, require `program_done` 1 and read the OTP word of the programmed bit through the direct read; the bit reads 1. For a program that must be refused, log `program_busy` and `program_done`, and read the same OTP word through the direct read; the bit reads 0. A timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Legs A and B. Window control.
