Program a free spare bit: write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1 and `efuse_addr` = the bit address of the drawn spare bit (8 times the byte offset of its field plus the bit index), then write it again with `efuse_program_go` = 1. Run the program wait for a program that must land. Record the program commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the go write to the direct read. The control `CTRL-WINDOW-CTL` is seen when the program lands (`program_busy` 0 and `program_done` 1 after W, and the bit reads 1 at the direct read inside W) and exactly one program command shows, with the address of the spare bit, and no program command to any other address. Evidence: `CTRL-WINDOW-CTL seen: seed=<n> bit_addr=<a> busy=0 done=1 bit=1 cmds=1 cmds_all=1 w_cycles=<w>`. A miss logs `CTRL-MISSING WINDOW-CTL` and the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL reason=window_control` (no landing: a wait of W could not show a program that lands) or `CHK-HOOK FAIL reason=window_control_cmd` (the command count). Log `INFO-WINDOW-CTL LOG seed=<n> w_cycles=<w> landed=1`. Read the `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` word of `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and require its used bit to equal the staged enable (CTRL-TRANSIENT-EN); a miss fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL reason=transient_rma_en_shadow`.
. Leg A. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word and `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg A. Run the script step by step. For a step on channel c: write the eight words of the channel token (`TOKEN_T1` for SIP, `TOKEN_T2` for CHIPLET) to its array and write 1 to its `TOKEN_EOP` bit (SIP bit 0, CHIPLET bit 8). Wait until the match register of c reads `0x15`. Wait the window W. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word and `FEAT_CTRL`. Error injection, for a CHIPLET step with `LC_STATE` bit 1 clear. Point: the `TOKEN_EOP` go with a matching CHIPLET token. Kind: a transition request without its precondition. Expected response: no change of the `LC_STATE` shadow.
. Leg A. After a CHIPLET step and before a later SIP step, write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the CHIPLET array and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 8. Wait until `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` (`0x1093_0568`) reads `0x2a`.
. Leg A. After the script, for each channel whose match register reads `0x15` (a held Match), write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to its array and write 1 to its `TOKEN_EOP` bit. Wait until its match register reads `0x2a`; the `0x15` read before the go makes a go that has no effect time out the wait. A channel whose Match step 7 already ended takes no go here. Read the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP through the direct read; expect the staged word (the raw nibble of the start state).
. Leg A. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word and `FEAT_CTRL`. Then run leg S when the leaf has it. Each of its two scripts runs once:
.. Simultaneous script. Present the CHIPLET token and wait for Match, wait W, and read the `LC_STATE` shadow word, its probe and `FEAT_CTRL` (CHK-TRANSIENT, `step=simul_chp`). Present `TOKEN_T1` on the SIP channel while the CHIPLET match is held, wait for Match, read both match codes, wait W, read the shadow word, probe and `FEAT_CTRL`, and read the CHIPLET code again. Then write a mismatch go on SIP and on CHIPLET; a go that does not reach `0x2a` ends the leaf (CTRL-TRANSIENT-SIMUL-END).
.. Twin script. Present the SIP token alone right after the simultaneous script, wait W, read the shadow word, probe and `FEAT_CTRL` (CHK-TRANSIENT-AFTER-BLOCK). Write a mismatch go on SIP (CTRL-TRANSIENT-AFTER-BLOCK-END; a go that does not reach `0x2a` ends the leaf), pulse `rst_ni` and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, all with the owner window closed; the window opens again after this reset. Present the SIP token alone, wait W, and read the shadow word, probe and `FEAT_CTRL` (CHK-TRANSIENT, `step=simul_twin`). Write a mismatch go on SIP, pulse `rst_ni`, wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, and read the shadow word, probe and `FEAT_CTRL` (CHK-TRANSIENT-RESET, `leg=simul`).
. Leg B, bit 1 cells. Present `TOKEN_T2` on the CHIPLET channel and wait until `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`; the SIP channel holds no match. Program `LC_STATE` bit 1: write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1 and `efuse_addr` = the bit address of bit 1 (8 times `0xC`, plus 1), then write it again with `efuse_program_go` = 1. Error injection. Point: the `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go for `LC_STATE` bit 1 with only the CHIPLET channel matched. Kind: a token-gated program without its token. Expected response: no change in the bank. The status value is not stated and is not graded. Wait the full W. Read the status and log `program_busy`. Read the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP through the direct read; expect the staged raw nibble with bit 1 clear. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word.
. Leg B, bit 1 cells. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the CHIPLET array and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 8. Wait until `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x2a`. No CHIPLET match is held when the SIP match follows.
. Leg B, bit 1 cells. Present `TOKEN_T1` on the SIP channel and wait until `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`. Repeat the program of bit 1 and run the program wait for a program that must land; the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP reads with bit 1 set. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word; expect the value before the program.
. Leg B. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the SIP array and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 0. Wait until `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x2a`; `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` already reads `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word; expect the byte `{~r, r}` with r the raw nibble before the program OR bit 1 (`0010`, RMA_SIP).
. Leg B, bit 2 cells. Present `TOKEN_T1` on the SIP channel and wait until `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`. Read `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` and expect a code other than `0x15`: the CHIPLET mismatch of step 11 is the last CHIPLET go, so the channel holds no Match. Log the code. No CHIPLET go is written here, because a mismatch go from a held Mismatch cannot show that it ran. Program `LC_STATE` bit 2 (bit address 8 times `0xC`, plus 2). Error injection. Point: the `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go for `LC_STATE` bit 2 with only the SIP channel matched. Kind: a token-gated program without its token. Expected response: no change in the bank. The status value is not stated and is not graded. Wait the full W. Read the status and log `program_busy`. Read the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP through the direct read; expect raw `0010`, with bit 2 clear. This cell is the control of the open refusal and logs `role=control`.
. Leg B, bit 2 cells. Present `TOKEN_T2` on the CHIPLET channel and wait until `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`. Repeat the program of bit 2 and run the program wait for a program that must land; the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP reads with bit 2 set. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word; expect the value before the program.
. Leg B. Write `TOKEN_OTHER` to the SIP and CHIPLET arrays and write the two go bits in two writes. Wait until both match registers read `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word; expect the byte `{~r, r}` with r = `0110` (RMA_CHIPLET).
. Last step, start PROD or TEST_DEV and no leg B (other leaves log `INFO-PROG-LC-ORDER-SKIP`), with the graded window closed. Present `TOKEN_T2` on the CHIPLET channel and wait until `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15` (CTRL-PROG-LC-ORDER-MATCH); bit 1 of `LC_STATE` is clear. Program `LC_STATE` bit 2 (bit address 8 times `0xC`, plus 2). Error injection. Point: the `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go for `LC_STATE` bit 2 with the CHIPLET channel matched and bit 1 clear. Kind: an RMA stage out of order. The specification texts disagree on the expected response, so the result is logged and not graded (`OBS-PROG-LC-ORDER`). Wait the full W. Read the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP through the direct read. Write a mismatch go on the CHIPLET channel. The step runs last because a bit that lands changes every later state.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-TRANSIENT
|After each step, including `simul_chp` and `simul_twin` of leg S, the `LC_STATE` shadow (frontdoor readback, graded as the whole 32-bit word, bits 31:8 equal to 0), `FEAT_CTRL` and the match code equal the golden (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, rows `LC_STATE` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`; `token_processing.adoc`, section Post Token Matching Handling). With the enable at 1, a SIP match sets bit 1 from TEST_DEV and PROD, a CHIPLET match sets bit 2 when bit 1 is set, a CHIPLET match with bit 1 clear changes nothing, and INVALID stays INVALID. With the enable at 0 the shadow never changes. The code is `0x15` for each match in both cases. A DUT that updates with the enable at 0 or skips the RMA_SIP precondition fails. Anchor: the `LC_STATE` word readback, `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. The golden raw state comes from the rules above, and `FEAT_CTRL` from the profile function for the new state and the staged vectors. Control: the window control shows that a program started at its go lands inside W, and every cell with an update moves the state inside the same W, so a "never" wait of W is as long as a landing wait. In a leaf where every cell expects no change (enable 0, or enable 1 with start INVALID or RMA_CHIPLET, or a CHIPLET-only script from TEST_DEV or PROD), the code `0x15` shows that the match reached the matcher, and CTRL-TRANSIENT-EN shows the staged enable; the live control of the update path for such a leaf is the updated cell of the enable-1 TEST_DEV and PROD leaves of this test (named seeds 2 and 27) and the transient leaf `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_transient_test`. Evidence: `CHK-TRANSIENT PASS seed=<n> en=<0/1> start=<name> step=<k> tok=<sip/chp> code=0x15 exp_word=0x<w> got_word=0x<w> exp_feat=0x<64b> got_feat=0x<64b>`; the window control logs `CTRL-WINDOW-CTL seen:` and `INFO-WINDOW-CTL LOG` (step 4); the start values of leg A are logged as `INFO-TRANSIENT-BASE seed=<n> word=0x<w> feat=0x<64b> staged_byte=0x<b>` and are not graded.
|CTRL-TRANSIENT-EN
|After the first sense, the used bit of the `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` word of `efuse_shadow_probe_o` equals the staged enable, so every CHK-TRANSIENT cell runs with the enable it expects. Evidence: `CTRL-TRANSIENT-EN seen: seed=<n> staged=<0/1> shadow=<0/1>`.
|CHK-TRANSIENT-SIMUL
|Leg S. After a SIP match while the CHIPLET match is held, and after W, the whole `LC_STATE` word, its probe byte and `FEAT_CTRL` equal the start state (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc:232-238`; `sep_efuse_map.adoc`, register `LC_STATE`: the byte is `{~r, r}` and bits 31:8 are 0). A DUT whose SIP match falls through to RMA_SIP while the CHIPLET match is held fails. The same line carries the five codes of the script (CHIPLET go, SIP go, CHIPLET held, SIP held, CHIPLET after W), all `0x15`, so a script that never held both matches cannot pass. Evidence: `CHK-TRANSIENT-SIMUL PASS seed=<n> start=<name> chp_go=0x15 sip_go=0x15 chp_held=0x15 sip_held=0x15 chp_after_w=0x15 w_cycles=<w> exp_word=0x<w> got_word=0x<w> ...`.
|CTRL-TRANSIENT-SIMUL-HELD
|The five codes of the leg S script are all `0x15`. A miss logs `CTRL-MISSING TRANSIENT-SIMUL-HELD` and fails the leaf.
|CTRL-TRANSIENT-SIMUL-END
|After the script, the mismatch go on SIP and on CHIPLET each reads `0x2a`, so no match is left before the next reset. A miss logs `CTRL-MISSING TRANSIENT-SIMUL-END` and ends the leaf before that reset, so the leaf verdict carries this control.
|CHK-TRANSIENT-AFTER-BLOCK
|Leg S. A lone SIP match right after the simultaneous script, with no reset between, moves the shadow to RMA_SIP after W: the whole `LC_STATE` word, its probe byte, `FEAT_CTRL` and the match code equal the CHK-TRANSIENT golden for RMA_SIP. The transient update follows each token match without a reset (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc:232-238`; `token_processing.adoc:181-184`; `sep-programming.adoc:991-993`), and the block of CHK-TRANSIENT-SIMUL covers only a SIP match made while the CHIPLET match is held. A DUT whose blocked match uses up the SIP update until reset fails. Expected to fail until #3527 is fixed. *Issue*: #3527
|CTRL-TRANSIENT-AFTER-BLOCK-END
|The mismatch go on SIP after the graded lone SIP match reads `0x2a`, so no match is left before the cold reset that precedes the twin SIP match. A miss logs `CTRL-MISSING TRANSIENT-AFTER-BLOCK-END` and ends the leaf before that reset, so the leaf verdict carries this control.
|CHK-TRANSIENT-NOBURN
|The `LC_STATE` word of the OTP, read through the direct read after the scripts of leg A, equals the staged word: a match authorizes a change and burns no fuse (`token_processing.adoc`, section Post Token Matching Handling). A DUT that burns an `LC_STATE` bit on a transient update fails. Anchor: the direct read of the `LC_STATE` OTP word. Control: the free spare bit of the window control reads 1 at the same direct read, so the read can show a burn. The check also grades that no program command reaches the post-guard port (`efuse_shim_command_req_o`) from the first go of the leg A script (step 6) to the direct read of step 8; the window-control program of step 4 and the programs of leg B are outside this window. Control on the same tap: the window-control program of step 4 shows one program command with the address of the spare bit at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; a leaf in which it does not fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-TRANSIENT-NOBURN PASS seed=<n> lc_otp_word=0x<w> staged_word=0x<w> ctl_spare_bit=1 ctl_cmd=1 prog_cmds_in_window=0`.
|CHK-TRANSIENT-RESET
|After the final cold reset and sense done the shadow equals the staged whole word, the probe byte equals the staged byte, and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the staged profile; the check carries the field `leg=a` or `leg=simul` (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing and the opening paragraph: the sensing FSM copies each fuse field into its shadow register after cold reset). A DUT that keeps the update over reset fails. The mismatch go before the reset reads `0x2a`, so no retained match is left; a go that does not reach it fails this check with `reason=match_held_at_reset` before the reset and ends the leaf. Anchor: the `LC_STATE` word readback and the `FEAT_CTRL` readback after the sense. Control: the shadow before the reset differs from the staged byte in every cell that updated, and the pre-reset codes are `0x2a` on every channel that took a match. Evidence: `CHK-TRANSIENT-RESET PASS seed=<n> leg=<a\|simul> pre_reset_codes=<ch>:0x2a exp_word=0x<w> got_word=0x<w> probe_byte=0x<b> exp_feat=0x<64b> got_feat=0x<64b> probe_feat=0x<64b> updated=<0/1> shadow_pre_reset=0x<b>`.
|CHK-PROG-LC
|A program of `LC_STATE` bit 1 or bit 2 lands in the OTP only when the channel that gates the bit is matched, and changes no bank bit when only the other channel is matched (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization: only on a match can `LC_STATE[1]` and `LC_STATE[2]` be programmed; section Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`: setting bit 2 needs bit 1 set before the write). The shadow word equals its value before the program until the resense. After the resense its byte is `{~r, r}`, with r the raw nibble before the program OR the programmed bit (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection: a program reaches the shadow at the next sense; section Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`: the shadow byte is `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}`). A DUT that accepts the other channel's match fails. Anchor: the direct read of the `LC_STATE` OTP word and the `LC_STATE` shadow readback. Every refused-program wait lasts the full W, and the direct read follows it. Control: the program with the matching channel lands in the same leaf, within the same W, for bit 1 and for bit 2, and the window control shows that a program started at its go lands inside W. So a wrongly accepted program that starts late would land inside the refused wait and show at the direct read. The bit-1 cell with only the CHIPLET token matched is new. The bit-2 cell with only the SIP token matched adds only the held SIP Match read to the refusal that `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test` grades, so it logs `role=control`. The check also grades that no program command, to any address, reaches the post-guard port (`efuse_shim_command_req_o`) from the go of a refused program to the end of its wait W (`shim_cmd=0`, `shim_cmd_all=0`). The status bits of a refused program (`program_busy`, `program_done` and `efuse_req_error`) are logged and not graded: an RMA-authorization refusal sets `efuse_req_error`, and the field text names only a read lock or a program lock as its causes, so the specification does not state the value. Every match and mismatch go of leg B is graded before the program that it sets up: a go whose match register does not reach the expected code inside its bounded poll (`0x15` for a match go, `0x2a` for a mismatch go) fails the check and ends the leaf, so no refusal is graded against another channel state. Control on the same tap: the program with the matching channel shows one program command with the address of the programmed bit at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` within its wait W, for bit 1 and for bit 2; a missing command fails the check. Evidence: a refused cell logs `CHK-PROG-LC PASS seed=<n> bit=<1/2> match=other_only role=<new/control> shim_cmd=0 shim_cmd_all=0 w_cycles=<w> lc_otp_word=0x<w> landed=0 busy=<b> done=<d> req_err=<e> shadow_pre=0x<b> shadow_after_sense=na exp_after_sense=na state_after_sense=na ctl_own_landed=1 go_codes=<step:code,...>` (the bit-2 cell also `chp_code=0x<c>`); a landing cell logs `CHK-PROG-LC PASS seed=<n> bit=<1/2> match=own role=new shim_cmd=1 w_cycles=<w> lc_otp_word=0x<w> landed=1 shadow_pre=0x<b> shadow_after_sense=0x<b> exp_after_sense=0x<b> state_after_sense=<name> sip_end=0x2a go_codes=<step:code,...>`, with `chp_code=0x<c>` (bit 1) or `chp_end=0x2a` (bit 2). A go that misses its code logs `CHK-PROG-LC FAIL seed=<n> step=<b1.chp\|b1.chp_end\|b1.sip\|b2.sip\|b2.chp> exp_code=0x<c> code=<0x..\|none> w_cycles=<w>`. The held CHIPLET code before the bit-2 refused program is also logged as `INFO-PROG-LC-CHP seed=<n> chp_code=0x<c>`.
|CTRL-PROG-LC-ORDER-MATCH
|The CHIPLET channel reads Match `0x15` before the out-of-order program of bit 2, so the program runs with its token matched. Evidence: `CTRL-PROG-LC-ORDER-MATCH seen: seed=<n> chp_code=0x15`.
|OBS-PROG-LC-ORDER
|A logged line, not a graded check: with the CHIPLET channel matched and bit 1 clear, the result of a program of `LC_STATE` bit 2 after the full W (the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP, the program commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the go to the end of W, and the status bits). The specification texts disagree: `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`) and `lifecycle_controller.adoc` (section Lifecycle States) say that setting bit 2 requires bit 1, while `token_processing.adoc` (section Post Token Matching Handling) and `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization) gate the fuse program on the token match only. Evidence: `OBS-PROG-LC-ORDER seed=<n> start=<PROD\|TEST_DEV> bit=2 chp_code=0x15 shim_cmd=<n> shim_cmd_all=<n> w_cycles=<w> lc_otp_word=0x<v> staged_otp_word=0x<v> landed=<0/1> busy=<b> done=<d> req_err=<e> program_status=<s> chp_end=0x2a`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test does not grade the status value of a refused program, the effect of a retained match after a cold reset with the enable at 1, or the effect of a blocked CHIPLET match after a later SIP match sets bit 1 (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); every match is replaced by a mismatch go before those events. The lone SIP match right after a blocked simultaneous match is graded (CHK-TRANSIENT-AFTER-BLOCK). It does not grade the digest packing, or the response to a program of bit 2 under a CHIPLET match before bit 1 is set (it logs that program as `OBS-PROG-LC-ORDER`, because the specification texts disagree). The write-locked refusal of a program belongs to `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test`. A token match fault and a SEC_DIS go are not part of this test. The open tests named in Test Coverage keep the cells that they grade.
[[sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test]]
===== 6.23 `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 1 seed per regression, because every leaf walks all 18 legal mismatch cells and the independence cell on each of the three channels; tool: VCS, because the no-reset check needs X on unreset flops; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor: the CPU-side bench master of `lsu_stub_all_live` (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`), with the response code of every access (approved). The OTP bank model with its image preload (approved). Ports `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and `security_disable_o` (approved). `secure_tm_req_i` stays at 0. The test computes the SHA-256 golden of each token. The tap of the 24 token word registers (CHK-NORESET) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Dimensions: mismatch shape (7: flip, swap, byte swap, zeros, ones, digest as token, foreign token) and channel (3: RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, SEC_DIS); every leaf walks the 18 legal (shape, channel) cells in an order that the seed draws; on SEC_DIS only flip, ones, digest as token and foreign token are legal. Also drawn: the order of the 18 cells, the order of the three channels in the independence cell, the word-write order of every array write, the decoy subset, the flip position (0 to 255), and the words of the swap and of the byte swap. Trap filters: no `TOKEN_ZERO` on the SEC_DIS channel outside its match steps; a swap of two distinct words; a byte swap of a non-palindromic word; no go on an array with no written word; no word write between a go and its non-Idle result, except the second go of the go-while-busy leg; one go bit per write, except the multi-go write. Also drawn: the first channel and the delay of each go-while-busy leg (the leg runs on all three channels), and the two RMA channels of the HMAC-overlap legs. The last draws are the channel pair of the multi-go write (`multi_go.pair`) and then the word-write orders of its settle and right-token writes. The leaf runs one seed with no reseed; every run walks all 18 cells, and the seed selects the cell order and the values. The cell set is the same on every seed.

*Objective*: Each of three channels hashes its eight-word array (index 7 first, no byte swap) on its own go bit and shows Idle `0x00`, Match `0x15` or Mismatch `0x2a`, and no other code. A go clears the held result until the new result (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: a start clears digest validity, so the comparison returns idle while the new digest is unavailable). The specification gives no cycle count for that Idle interval, so the leaf grades the Idle observation relative to the hash time that it measures. Writing the token words of a channel alone does not change its result and does not start a comparison. The RMA channels compare to their own OTP digest and read Idle until sense completes. The SEC_DIS channel compares to a constant that does not depend on OTP. A digest used as a token never matches. The token arrays have no reset. After a cold reset a retained digest gives its result again once the comparison prerequisites hold. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` (sections Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures, Token Submission); `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` (sections Token Activation, Fuse Sensing and the Shadow Map, Reset Persistence, Implementation Requirements); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_mmr.adoc` (section eFuse Token Registers Address Map; registers `TOKEN_EOP`, `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH`). One `TOKEN_EOP` write that sets two go bits starts both lanes (`hw/ip/efuse/regs/efuse_mmr.rdl`, `TOKEN_EOP`), and the per-channel result of such a write is graded; the specification does not state its timing (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>).

*Test Coverage*: The unreset token arrays. A go before sense done on RMA_SIP and on RMA_CHIPLET. Word-write order and decoy writes before the final words, on each channel. Result hold under word writes, on each channel. The Idle read after a go while a result is held, on each channel. A second go on a busy channel (go while busy). A token go overlapped with a busy keyed HMAC engine, for one Match and one Mismatch. One `TOKEN_EOP` write with the go bits of two channels (per-channel result). The 18 legal (shape, channel) mismatch cells: the seven shapes on each RMA channel and four shapes on SEC_DIS. Independence of the three channels, with the SEC_DIS channel given an RMA token. Retention of a digest over cold reset, with sensing held off by a retained SEC_DIS match and with sensing running. The test extends `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test` (match and mismatch of the SIP and CHIPLET tokens), `sep_sec_dis_override_test` (the SEC_DIS match) and `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test` (the digest latch). This test grades only the new cells named above. The three open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_token_channel_cg` (every coverpoint; bins `0x00`, `0x15` and `0x2a` of `cp_code`), `sep_efuse_map_geometry_cg` (the `TOKEN_EOP` write cell and the three match read cells of `x_token_blk_op`) and `sep_sec_dis_robustness_cg` (`x_digest_replay`), `cp_go_while_busy` (all three channels in every leaf) and `cp_token_hmac_overlap` (`match_hmac_busy`, `mismatch_hmac_busy`, both in every leaf). The owner window opens before the no-reset cell and closes after CHK-TOKEN-CODES, and the first FAIL or missing control closes it. The two overlap coverpoints hold their event as pending and sample only when the leaf writes a pass label after CHK-TOKEN-GWB or CHK-TOKEN-HMAC of that leg passed. Every other sample is held with the tag of its graded step and samples only after every check of that step passed; the settle gos and the reference go of a go-while-busy leg are not graded steps, and their events drop.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the order of the 18 (shape, channel) cells, the order of the three channels of the independence cell, the word orders, the decoy subsets and the flip positions from the seed. In the steps below, "write token X to c" writes the eight words of X to the array of channel c in a drawn order, word k holding bits [32k+31:32k] of X. "Go on c" writes 1 to the go bit of c in `TOKEN_EOP` (bit 0 RMA_SIP, bit 8 RMA_CHIPLET, bit 16 SEC_DIS) with no other bit set. "Poll c" reads the match register of c until it reads a value other than `0x00`. A "read run on c" reads the match register of c back to back from the go until two consecutive reads return the same code that is neither `0x00` nor the held code H of the go (the code read before the go). A read of H does not end the run, so a DUT that clears H later than one bus read after the go is not read as a go with no effect; every graded go starts from a held code other than its expected final code, except the second go of the go-while-busy leg, so the rule never waits for the expected code to change. The run is bounded at 400 reads, and a run that reaches the bound with no such pair has no final code and fails CHK-TOKEN (a go with no effect). The re-go after sense in the pre-sense cell, whose held code equals its expected code, ends at the first two consecutive equal codes other than `0x00` after its first `0x00` read (a go clears the retained digest validity, `token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission), so a go that has no effect never reads `0x00`, reaches the bound and fails. It counts the reads as `W_hash` of the channel and the reads that return `0x00` as `n_idle`. Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. Every go on SEC_DIS runs with `secure_tm_req_i` at 0.
. Stage the OTP image: TEST_DEV; the SHA-256 digest of `TOKEN_T1` in `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_DIGEST` and that of `TOKEN_T2` in `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_DIGEST` (word k holding bits [32k+31:32k]); every other 256-bit field at its fixed vector; `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0, so that no match updates the state shadow; the fixed filter-legal disable-vector pair of the image rules (no seed draw).
. Run `CHK-BIND`. Release the reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise.
. No-reset cell. Before any token write of the leaf, read the tap of the 24 token word registers. Expect X on every word.
. Walk. Run the sub-steps below once per channel, in a drawn order. The token T is `TOKEN_T1` on RMA_SIP, `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_CHIPLET and `TOKEN_ZERO` on SEC_DIS. The foreign token F is `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_SIP and `TOKEN_T1` on RMA_CHIPLET and on SEC_DIS.
.. Write wrong decoy words to a drawn subset of the array words, then write token T to the channel (the decoys are overwritten). Read the token word tap at the first word written, as the control of the no-reset cell.
.. Go on the channel. Run a read run. Log the channel, the final code, the held code, the idle reads and the reads (`OBS-TOKEN-DIGEST-LAYOUT`), because no specification states the layout of the digest in the shadow words. The control `CTRL-TOKEN-DIGEST-LAYOUT` requires the final code `0x15` after a held code other than `0x15`; the hold, idle-first and later legs rest on it. On SEC_DIS also expect `security_disable_o` = 1: the SEC_DIS go of the walk is the first SEC_DIS match of the leaf and the control of the revision-enable assumption, and a leaf in which `security_disable_o` stays low fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. The code before this go is Idle, so the run sets `W_hash` and `n_idle` of the channel and grades no Idle-first rule. The first RMA channel of the walk is the control of the digest staging: a result other than `0x15` fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and the later steps are not graded.
.. Hold cell. Write token F to the channel with no go. Read the match register back to back through a window of a multiple of `W_hash` reads (the multiple is a test parameter). Expect every read to equal the code of the previous sub-step.
.. Idle-first cell. Go on the channel with the words of F. Run a read run. Expect the final code `0x2a`. The held code H is the code of the previous sub-step, `0x15` on every channel, so a go that has no effect leaves `0x15` and fails; CHK-TOKEN grades the run against H and `n_idle`. On SEC_DIS, read `security_disable_o` before the go and two clocks after the run. Expect 1 before and 0 after (CHK-SDIS-AFTER-FOREIGN).
. Mismatch walk. Run this step and the next once for each of the 18 legal (shape, channel) cells, in the drawn order. Write the words of the shape of the cell to the channel of the cell. The base token is `TOKEN_T1` on RMA_SIP, `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_CHIPLET and `TOKEN_ZERO` on SEC_DIS, and it is never presented unchanged. Flip: the base token with the bit at the drawn position inverted. Swap: two distinct words of the base token exchanged. Byte swap: one non-palindromic word of the base token byte-reversed. Zeros: eight zero words. Ones: eight all-ones words. Digest as token: the eight words that hold the expected digest of the channel (RMA: the staged digest; SEC_DIS: the digest constant bound at the bench), word k holding bits [32k+31:32k]. Foreign: the token F of the channel. Before the shape is written, set the held Match: write the base token unchanged to the channel, go and poll, and expect `0x15` (on SEC_DIS also `security_disable_o` = 1). This is the only unchanged presentation of the base token in the cell.
. Go on the channel of the cell. Run a read run. Expect the final code `0x2a`. The held code H is `0x15`, so a go that has no effect leaves `0x15` and fails; CHK-TOKEN grades the run against H and `n_idle`. For the digest-as-token shape, log the token words beside the digest value.
. Independence cell. Run this step and the next once for each channel, in the drawn order; that channel is the changed channel. Bring the three channels to non-Idle codes: write `TOKEN_T1` to RMA_SIP, go, poll (Match); write `TOKEN_OTHER` to RMA_CHIPLET, go, poll (Mismatch); write `TOKEN_OTHER` to SEC_DIS, go, poll (Mismatch), or, when SEC_DIS is the changed channel, write `TOKEN_ZERO` to SEC_DIS, go, poll (Match). Read the three match registers and log the held codes as `INFO-INDEP-HELD seed=<n> go=<ch> held=<ch>:0x<v>,...`.
. Write to the changed channel the token that changes its verdict: `TOKEN_OTHER` on RMA_SIP and `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_CHIPLET; on SEC_DIS write `TOKEN_OTHER`, which changes the held Match to a Mismatch. Go on the changed channel. Poll the changed channel and expect the changed verdict, so a go that has no effect fails. Read the other two match registers at once, then through a window of a multiple of `W_hash` reads. Expect both to equal their held codes.
. Write `TOKEN_ZERO` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x15`; this is the held code H. Write `TOKEN_T1` to SEC_DIS and go on SEC_DIS. Run a read run on SEC_DIS. Expect the final code `0x2a`; H is `0x15`, so a go that has no effect fails. Read the RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET match registers. Expect each to equal its code before this go.
. For each RMA channel that holds a Match, write `TOKEN_OTHER`, go and poll. Expect `0x2a` (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: Mismatch `0x2a`), so that no RMA match reaches the next cold reset by accident. Then read the match register of each RMA channel: after a mismatch go it reads `0x2a`, and a channel that took no go reads a code other than `0x15` (`CTRL-TOKEN-PRESENSE-CLEAR seen: seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp> mismatch_go=<0\|1> code=0x<v>`; a miss logs `CTRL-MISSING TOKEN-PRESENSE-CLEAR` and fails the leaf).
. Pre-sense cell. Write `TOKEN_ZERO` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x15`. Expect `security_disable_o` = 1; a leaf in which `security_disable_o` stays low fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. Cold reset. The retained SEC_DIS match holds from reset release, so sensing does not start.
. Error injection. Point: `TOKEN_EOP` go on an RMA channel. Kind: a go before sense done. Expected response: the result of the channel stays `0x00` (read in the step that follows the wait). Write `TOKEN_T1` to RMA_SIP and go on RMA_SIP. Write `TOKEN_T2` to RMA_CHIPLET and go on RMA_CHIPLET.
. Write no SEC_DIS go, so that the retained SEC_DIS match holds sensing off. Poll the SEC_DIS match register until it reads a value other than `0x00`. Expect `0x15`. Wait a window of a multiple of the larger `W_hash` of the two RMA channels (the multiple is a test parameter) with the match held. Read the SEC_DIS match register. Expect `0x15`.
. Sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` immediately before and immediately after each read. Read the RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET match registers. Expect `0x00` on both. A read with sense done high at either sample does not count, and a leaf with no counted read of a channel fails.
. Write `TOKEN_OTHER` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise.
. Before any new RMA go, poll the RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET match registers until each reads a value other than `0x00`. Expect `0x15` on both. This is the control of the cell: the hash of the pre-sense go completed, and only the sensing gate held the result at `0x00`.
. Go on RMA_SIP and on RMA_CHIPLET again, with the arrays unchanged. Read each from the go. Expect `0x00` and then `0x15` on both.
. Retention cell. Write `TOKEN_OTHER` to RMA_CHIPLET, go and poll. Expect `0x2a`, so that only RMA_SIP holds a Match. Read the RMA_SIP match register. Expect `0x15`.
. Write `TOKEN_ZERO` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x15` and `security_disable_o` = 1. Cold reset. The SEC_DIS match holds sensing off.
. Sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` immediately before and immediately after each read. Poll the SEC_DIS match register until it reads a value other than `0x00`. Expect `0x15`. Wait a window of a multiple of the larger `W_hash` of the two RMA channels with the match held. Read the RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS match registers. Expect `0x00`, `0x00` and `0x15`. Read the eight words of each of the three arrays. Expect the words last written: `TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_OTHER` and `TOKEN_ZERO`. A read with sense done high at either sample does not count, and a leaf with no counted read fails.
. Write `TOKEN_OTHER` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. With no new go, poll the RMA_SIP match register until it reads a value other than `0x00`. Expect `0x15`.
. Go-while-busy legs. Run the leg once on each channel c, a drawn channel first and then the other two in channel order. Draw a delay d per leg. Time one go on c with `TOKEN_OTHER`: T is the `clk_i` count from its B handshake to the first verdict on the comparator rails (`token_cmp_match_{p,n}_o`). Write the right token to c and set the held code to Mismatch. Go on c (go 1), then write a second go on c with the words unchanged so that its B handshake lands d cycles after that of go 1, with d in [T/4 + T/8, 3T/4 - T/8]. Read the rails and the match register through the window to 2T + 8 cycles after go 1. This is the one write between a go and its result that the leaf allows.
. HMAC-overlap legs. Run two legs, a Match go on a drawn RMA channel with the right token and a Mismatch go on a drawn RMA channel with the foreign token. Start keyed HMAC-SHA256 with a drawn key and push two message blocks, so that `STATUS.hmac_idle` reads 0. Write the go. Read `STATUS.hmac_idle` after the verdict. Push the rest of the message, wait for HMAC done, and read the eight `DIGEST` words and `ERR_CODE`. Run the same go with the HMAC idle as the positive twin.
. Multi-go cell. After CHK-TOKEN-CODES, with the graded window closed, settle each channel of the drawn pair to Mismatch, write its right token and read its match register (CTRL-TOKEN-MULTI-GO). Write one `TOKEN_EOP` word that sets the go bits of the pair. For each channel, wait, bounded, for the verdict on the comparator rails and read the match register (CHK-TOKEN-MULTI-GO), and log the clocks from the write to the verdict (`OBS-TOKEN-MULTI-GO`).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|A bench-binding precondition, not a specification value: the SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the SEC_DIS digest that the bench binds. `security_disable.adoc` (section Implementation Requirements) states only that the digest is a 256-bit constant whose token is a SHA-256 preimage; it states no value. The bench binds the digest of the all-zero token, and the leaf presents `TOKEN_ZERO` as the right token on that binding. A wrong binding makes `TOKEN_ZERO` a mismatch token and can make `TOKEN_OTHER` the right one, so every SEC_DIS verdict of the leaf would rest on a false premise. Anchor: the bound digest at the bench tap `sec_dis_digest_probe_o`, read by its simulator handle, against the SHA-256 of the 32 zero bytes. Control: the first SEC_DIS match of the leaf (the SEC_DIS go of the walk) raises `security_disable_o`, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h> bound=0x<h> src=handle`.
|CHK-TOKEN
|The final code of each go (frontdoor readback of the match register) equals the SHA-256 golden: `0x15` for the right token, `0x2a` for each mismatch shape, token F and `TOKEN_OTHER`. The Idle observation is relative: in a read run after a go from a non-Idle held code H, no read after the first `0x00` returns H when H differs from the final code, no read returns a code outside {H, `0x00`, the final code}, and the run holds at least one `0x00` when `n_idle` of the first run of that channel is 2 or more (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: a start clears digest validity, so the comparison returns idle while the new digest is unavailable). The check never grades a single read at a fixed offset from the go. Every go starts from a held code other than its expected final code: Idle `0x00` at the first go of a channel, `0x15` before each mismatch go, and `0x2a` before the verdict-changing Match go of the independence cell. The held code is read before the go and logged as `held`, so a go that has no effect leaves the held code and fails. A DUT that accepts a digest as a token, swaps the word order, shares one result across channels or keeps the old code through a go whose hash outlasts the read period fails. Control: the right token returns `0x15` on each channel in the walk (`CTRL-TOKEN-DIGEST-LAYOUT`, not graded here), and again on each RMA channel and on SEC_DIS in the pre-sense cell of the same leaf; the Idle-first grade applies only where the code before the go is non-Idle (the idle-first cell, the mismatch cell and the independence cell) and `n_idle` of the first run of the channel is 2 or more, and the first run of the channel from Idle measures `n_idle`. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> shape=<match\|other\|flip@<p>\|swap<i,j>\|bswap<i>\|zeros\|ones\|digest_as_token\|foreign> exp=0x<15\|2a> got=0x<v> held=0x<v\|na> idle_reads=<n> n_idle_ref=<n> idle_first_graded=<0\|1> [tok_eq_digest=1]`. A digest-as-token line logs the token words beside the digest value.
|CHK-TOKEN-CODES
|Every value read from a match register in the leaf is one of `0x00`, `0x15` and `0x2a` (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission, table Token result encodings; the Error code `0x3f` needs a comparator fault, which this test does not inject). A DUT that emits another 6-bit value fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-CODES PASS seed=<n> allowed=00,15,2a seen=<list> reads=<n>`.
|CHK-TOKEN-HOLD
|After a Match or a Mismatch, new word writes into the same channel array with no go leave the result unchanged: every back-to-back read through the window equals the held code (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: writing the token words alone does not invalidate the previous result or launch a comparison). A DUT that clears or recomputes the result on a word write shows `0x00` in the window and fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback. Control: the window is a multiple of the `W_hash` reads that the leaf measured at the go of the same channel, and the go after the window reaches a result inside `W_hash` reads. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-HOLD PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> held=0x<v> reads_in_window=<n> all_equal=1`.
|CHK-TOKEN-INDEP
|A go on one channel leaves the held results of the other two unchanged, at once and after the window, and `TOKEN_T1` on the SEC_DIS channel gives `0x2a`, because its constant is not the OTP digest (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A DUT with a shared result register or a shared comparison fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback of all three match registers. Control: the held codes of the three channels are non-Idle before the go, the go channel changes its verdict (a go that has no effect fails), and the read run of the go channel follows the Idle rule of CHK-TOKEN. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-INDEP PASS seed=<n> go=<ch> held=0x<v>,0x<v> others=0x<v>,0x<v> sdis_t1=0x2a`.
|CHK-NORESET
|After the first sense of the leaf and before any token write, all 24 token word registers read X at the tap (`token_processing.adoc`, opening paragraph: the arrays have no reset). A DUT that resets the arrays fails. Control: the tap shows the first word written in the walk with its written value, so the tap is alive; a tap that does not show it fails the check (`CHK-NORESET FAIL`). Evidence: `CHK-NORESET PASS seed=<n> arrays=3 x_words=24 ctl_word=0x<w>`.
|CHK-TOKEN-GWB
|With the second go written to a busy channel c, the match register holds the held code until the first verdict, the rails show the Match verdict at T +/- 4 cycles after go 1 and keep it with no change to 2T + 8 cycles after go 1, and the frontdoor reads after go 2 return only the held code, `0x00` and `0x15`, with the first code that is neither the held code nor `0x00` equal to `0x15`, all later reads `0x15`, and the final read after 2T + 8 cycles `0x15` (`token_processing.adoc:107-108`: no submission queue, a new operation starts only while idle; `:118-120`: codes; `:132-133`: valid rails). A DUT that restarts on go 2 shows its verdict at the B handshake of go 2 plus T. A DUT that queues a second hash shows Match, Idle, Match. A DUT that ignores go 2 with a late or wrong verdict fails the timing term. The leg logs the channel, held code, T, d, the verdict time and its expected time, the number of rail changes to 2T + 8 cycles, the reads, the first non-held code and the final code. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-GWB PASS ch=<ch> held=0x2a t=<T> d=<d> ...`.
|CTRL-TOKEN-GWB-BUSY
|After go 1 the rails read cleared before the B handshake of go 2, and the rails verdict appears after it, with 4 d in [T, 3T]. It proves that go 2 reached a busy channel.
|CHK-TOKEN-HMAC
|With keyed HMAC busy during the go, the final code of the go equals the golden (`0x15` for the Match leg, `0x2a` for the Mismatch leg), the code held before the go differs from it, and the Idle rule of CHK-TOKEN holds (`token_processing.adoc:44`: each token has a dedicated SHA-256 engine). All eight `DIGEST` words equal HMAC-SHA256 of the key and the message in the big-endian read of each word (`digest_swap` = 0, convention "HMAC digest order" in `crypto_entropy.adoc`), and `ERR_CODE` is 0 (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/hmac/regs/gen/adoc/hmac.adoc:111-120`: `key_swap` and `endian_swap` at 0). A DUT that routes the token hash through the shared HMAC shows a wrong digest, a wrong code, or a stalled token. The line logs the channel, the expected and the final code, the held code, the number of idle reads, the digest order (`be`, `le` or `mismatch`) and `ERR_CODE`.
|CTRL-TOKEN-HMAC-BUSY
|`STATUS.hmac_idle` reads 0 after the head of the message is pushed and before the go, and again after the verdict (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/hmac/regs/gen/adoc/hmac.adoc:161`).
|CTRL-TOKEN-HMAC-IDLE
|The positive twin: the same channel, token and held code with the HMAC idle (`STATUS.hmac_idle` 1 before and after) give the same final code.
|CHK-TOKEN-RETAIN
|After a cold reset with the SEC_DIS match held and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` low at both samples of each read, once the SEC_DIS result has polled to non-Idle and a window of a multiple of `W_hash` has passed with the match held, the RMA results read `0x00`, the SEC_DIS result reads the code of its retained digest (`0x15`) and the 24 words equal the written words (`security_disable.adoc`, section Reset Persistence; `token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission). After sense done the retained RMA_SIP digest, polled to non-Idle with no new go, gives `0x15`. A DUT that clears a digest or an array on reset fails. A read with sense done high at either sample does not count. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` port. Control: the pre-reset code of RMA_SIP is `0x15` and the SEC_DIS match raised `security_disable_o`. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-RETAIN PASS seed=<n> pre=0x15 sense_done_pre=0 sense_done_post=0 post_reset_rma=0x00 post_reset_sdis=0x15 words_equal=24 post_sense=0x15`.
|CHK-TOKEN-PRESENSE
|With `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` low at both samples of each read, a go on each RMA channel reads `0x00` after the retained SEC_DIS match has polled to `0x15` and a window of a multiple of `W_hash` has passed with the match held (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures: RMA results stay idle until fuse sensing completes). A DUT that compares before sensing shows `0x15` or `0x2a` once the hash completes, so it fails; the window is a multiple of the `W_hash` that the leaf measured at the walk go of the same channel. Control on the same channel: after the SEC_DIS mismatch, the cold reset and sense done, and before any new RMA go, the result polls to `0x15` from the digest that the pre-sense go computed, so the hash completed and only the sensing gate held the result at `0x00`; the leaf fails if it does not. The same go made again after sense done reads `0x00` and then `0x15`, so a go that has no effect fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the port. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-PRESENSE PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp> sense_done=0 sdis_held=0x15 window=<n> pre=0x00 retained_post_sense=0x15 post=0x15`.
|CHK-SDIS-AFTER-FOREIGN
|In the idle-first cell of the SEC_DIS walk, `security_disable_o` reads 1 before the go with the words of F and 0 two clocks after its read run ends at Mismatch `0x2a` (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, section Post Token Matching Handling: SEC_DIS activates the emergency bypass while its match and revision-enable conditions hold). A DUT that keeps the bypass after a mismatch fails, and an X or Z on the port at the post-go read fails the leaf. Anchor: the port. Evidence: `CHK-SDIS-AFTER-FOREIGN PASS seed=<n> exp=0 secdis=0 secdis_before_go=1 code=0x2a`.
|OBS-TOKEN-DIGEST-LAYOUT
|A logged line for the Match go of the right token of each walk channel, not a graded check. The specification does not state how the digest splits into the shadow words and bytes. Evidence: `OBS-TOKEN-DIGEST-LAYOUT seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> final=0x<c\|none> held=0x<c> idle_reads=<n> reads=<n>`.
|CTRL-TOKEN-DIGEST-LAYOUT
|The final code of that go is `0x15` and the held code before it is not `0x15`. It is the precondition of the hold, idle-first and later legs, which need the held Match. Evidence: `CTRL-TOKEN-DIGEST-LAYOUT seen: seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> final=0x15`.
|CTRL-TOKEN-MULTI-GO
|Each channel of the drawn pair holds Mismatch `0x2a` after its settle go and the write of its right token, so a go bit that starts nothing leaves Mismatch and fails the check. Evidence: `CTRL-TOKEN-MULTI-GO seen: seed=<n> <ch>_held=0x2a` for each channel of the pair.
|CHK-TOKEN-MULTI-GO
|One `TOKEN_EOP` write sets the go bits of two channels. Each channel shows a fresh verdict on the comparator rails (the rails clear after the write, then all three pairs show Match) and its match register reads Match `0x15` (`hw/ip/efuse/regs/efuse_mmr.rdl`, `TOKEN_EOP`: each go field generates a pulse to begin token hashing; `token_processing.adoc`, each lane has its own token array, go bit, SHA-256 engine and comparator). A DUT that drops or serializes away one go bit leaves that channel at Mismatch with no new verdict and fails. Anchor: the comparator rails `token_cmp_match_{p,n}_o` and the frontdoor match read. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-MULTI-GO PASS seed=<n> exp=0x15 bits=0x<v> <ch>_rails=0x15 <ch>_final=0x15` for each channel of the pair.
|OBS-TOKEN-MULTI-GO
|A logged line, not a graded check: the go bits written and, per channel of the pair, the clocks from the write to the verdict on the comparator rails. The specification does not state whether the channels of one write hash in parallel or one after the other. Evidence: `OBS-TOKEN-MULTI-GO seed=<n> bits=0x<v> <ch>_cycles=<n\|none>` for each channel of the pair.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test grades one write of two go bits in one `TOKEN_EOP` write for its per-channel result only (CHK-TOKEN-MULTI-GO), not its timing (`OBS-TOKEN-MULTI-GO`), does not grade the digest word and byte order in the OTP shadow (the staging is a stimulus choice that the first Match step of the walk controls; the walk logs it, `OBS-TOKEN-DIGEST-LAYOUT`), does not present a token with `secure_tm_req_i` at 1, and does not inject a comparator fault; the fault matrix test owns the error code `0x3f`. A match does not update the state shadow, because `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` is 0. The open tests `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test`, `sep_sec_dis_override_test` and `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test` keep the cells that they grade.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test]]
===== 6.24 `sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 3 seeds; every leaf runs one drawn fault cell on each of the three channels, each ended by a cold reset (the cold reset clears the sticky flags; the image, the token arrays and the digests stay), and the two common-mode cells and the three freeze cells, which set no sticky flag and take no cold reset, so a leaf takes three cold resets after its first sense; tool: Verilator and VCS; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`. The PIC source 40 request line is read as a probe, so no CPU model is needed.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`, CPU-LSU master of `lsu_stub_all_live`). The OTP bank model preload. Injects, approved: `token_cmp_fault_inject_i` with `token_cmp_fault_sel_i` (copy 0 of each channel only) and `token_digest_test_en_inject_i` (the RMA_SIP digest latch only). Probes, approved: the PIC source 40 request line (`sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o[39]`), `security_disable_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the RMA_SIP digest latch (`token_digest_*`). Added hooks, error injects that the test adds to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`: the comparator copy select at the `token_cmp_fault` injection port (copy 1 and copy 2 cells) and the test-enable inject of the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS digest latches (their freeze cells). The digest latch probes of RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS and the comparator rail taps `cmp_match_p_i` and `cmp_match_n_i` are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The test computes the SHA-256 golden of each token.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The cell list: cells 1 to 18 are the fault cells (channel {RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, SEC_DIS} x kind {collapse, disagree} x copy {0, 1, 2}); cells 19 and 20 are the common-mode cells (held Match, held Mismatch) on RMA_CHIPLET; cells 21 to 23 are the freeze cells of RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS. Every leaf draws one fault cell per channel, with equal weight among the new cells of the channel: on RMA_SIP the four cells of copies 1 and 2 (collapse or disagree); on RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS those four and the copy-0 disagree cell. The copy-0 collapse cells and the copy-0 disagree cell of RMA_SIP are not drawn. On an RMA channel the held phase of the fault cell (Match or Mismatch) is drawn with equal weight; the SEC_DIS channel holds a Mismatch only. Every leaf also runs the cells 19 to 23, which need no cold reset. Also drawn: the order of the eight cells of the leaf, the word-write order of every array write and the disable-vector pair. Trap filters: inject only while the held result is non-Idle; release before the next comparison; never hold a SEC_DIS match while an injection is active; no common-mode flip on SEC_DIS (a flip of a SEC_DIS mismatch is a SEC_DIS match). `reseed = 3` gives three fault cells per channel per regression. The fault cells and their held phases are seed-dependent and close over a merge of regressions: the five fault cells of RMA_CHIPLET or SEC_DIS in about 3 regressions (5 ln 5 / 3), and the ten cells (fault cell and held phase) of RMA_CHIPLET in about 8 (10 ln 10 / 3). Every leaf has a new fault cell on each channel, and the cells 19 to 23 run on every seed.

*Objective*: A collapsed differential pair or a disagreeing comparator copy, injected while a comparison is valid, forces the result `0x3f`, sets the sticky flag of that channel (bit 0, 8 or 16 of `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`) and the PIC source 40 request. The flags have no write-one-to-clear, survive a later valid comparison and clear only on reset. A common-mode flip with legal pairs raises nothing and inverts the held result. A test enable at the digest latch of a channel freezes the retained digest and its valid bit, so a later token operation on that channel changes neither (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures: test enable freezes those retained latches). The specification names no test-enable signal and states no effect on a match in progress (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, Test enable and token freeze), so the freeze cells grade the two latches only and grade no result code under the freeze. No step writes `LC_STATE` (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` (sections Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures, Token Submission, Matching Logic Countermeasures); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_mmr.adoc` (register `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (section SEP CPU Interrupt Vector Map, row Token match fault).

*Test Coverage*: The 14 new fault cells (collapse and disagree at comparator copies 1 and 2 on each channel, and the copy-0 disagree cell of RMA_CHIPLET and of SEC_DIS), one drawn per channel per leaf, each in a drawn held-Match or held-Mismatch phase on the RMA channels and a held-Mismatch phase on SEC_DIS, all 14 over a merge of regressions. The common-mode flip of a held Match and of a held Mismatch on RMA_CHIPLET. The sticky flag over a valid retry, the absence of a write-one-to-clear, the clear by cold reset (the token words are read and logged), and the absence of a SEC_DIS match under a fault. The freeze of the retained digest and valid bit on each of the three channels. The test extends `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test`, `sep_efuse_token_match_fault_pic_test` and `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test`. This test grades only the new cells: the comparator copies 1 and 2 on every channel, the disagreeing copy on RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS, the common-mode flip with the exact flipped code on RMA_CHIPLET, the held-Match and held-Mismatch phases of each fault, the absence of a SEC_DIS match under a fault, and the freeze of the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS latches. The freeze cell of RMA_SIP is a control: it runs on every seed, a failing control fails the leaf, and it is not credited as new coverage. The copy-0 collapse cells of every channel and the copy-0 disagree cell of RMA_SIP are not drawn: the open tests grade them. The open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_token_fault_cg` (every coverpoint; `x_ch_kind_copy` and `x_ch_retry` are seed-dependent), `sep_token_channel_cg` (`cp_code`, bin `0x3f`) and `sep_sec_dis_activation_cg` (`x_verdict_out`, Error cells).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the fault cell of each channel (kind, copy and, on an RMA channel, held phase), the order of the eight cells of the leaf, the word orders and the disable-vector pair. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value. Run steps 3 to 13 once per cell, in the drawn order, with only the steps of the kind of the cell. After the last step of each cell, a channel that holds a Match takes `TOKEN_OTHER`, a go and a poll (expect `0x2a`). A fault cell then ends with the cold reset of step 10. A common-mode cell or a freeze cell sets no sticky flag and takes no cold reset. Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. "Poll c" reads the match register of channel c until it reads a value other than `0x00`. "Go on c" writes 1 to the go bit of c in `TOKEN_EOP` (bit 0 RMA_SIP, bit 8 RMA_CHIPLET, bit 16 SEC_DIS) with no other bit set. Every go on SEC_DIS runs with `secure_tm_req_i` at 0.
. Stage the OTP image: TEST_DEV; the digest of `TOKEN_T1` in `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_DIGEST` and that of `TOKEN_T2` in `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_DIGEST` (word k holding bits [32k+31:32k]); `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0, so that no held or flipped match updates the state shadow; every other 256-bit field at its fixed vector; a disable-vector pair that the vector filter admits. Run `CHK-BIND`. Release the reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise.
. SEC_DIS control step. Before each cell that uses the SEC_DIS channel (a fault cell or the freeze cell), first write `TOKEN_ZERO` to the SEC_DIS array, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x15` and `security_disable_o` = 1 (control of the revision-enable assumption: a leaf in which the probe stays low fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and it is also the control of the "never" check on `security_disable_o` in step 7). Write `TOKEN_OTHER`, go and poll. Expect `0x2a` and `security_disable_o` = 0, so that no SEC_DIS match is held when the injection starts.
. Fault and common-mode cells (cells 1 to 20). Create the held result on the channel of the cell: on an RMA channel write the right token (`TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2`) for a held Match or `TOKEN_OTHER` for a held Mismatch; on SEC_DIS write `TOKEN_OTHER`. Go. Poll. Read the held code. A held code other than the expected verdict fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL reason=held_result`.
. Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`, the PIC source 40 request line and `security_disable_o`. Expect 0, 0 and 0 (the control that the flags and the request are low before the injection).
. Error injection. Point: `token_cmp_fault_inject_i` with `token_cmp_fault_sel_i` at the channel and copy of the cell. Kind: pair collapse (both rails of the copy at the same value), disagreement of one copy, or unanimous legal flip (common-mode cells, no copy axis). Expected response: as in the Objective. Apply the injection only while the held result is non-Idle. In a collapse or disagree cell, the comparator copy select routes the injection to the copy of the cell. Hook control: while the injection is active, the comparator rail taps `cmp_match_p_i` and `cmp_match_n_i` show the injected kind on the copy of the cell only, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. A collapse cell shows the collapsed pair on the copy of the cell and legal pairs on the other two copies. A disagree cell shows legal pairs on all three copies, and the copy of the cell is the minority copy, whose pair differs from the equal pairs of the other two copies. The copy-0 disagree cell uses the existing inject, which sets copy 0 to the Mismatch pair and copies 1 and 2 to the Match pair in either held phase, so the hook control of that cell expects exactly that rail pattern.
. Read the match register of the channel until it differs from the held code (bounded). Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`, the PIC source 40 request line and `security_disable_o`. Expect, for a collapse or disagree cell, `0x3f`, the flag of that channel only, the request at 1, and `security_disable_o` at 0. Expect, for a common-mode cell, the flipped code (`0x2a` for a held Match, `0x15` for a held Mismatch), no flag and the request at 0.
. Release the injection. For a collapse or disagree cell: read the match register and log it, then make the retry with the token of the opposite verdict: the right token (`TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2`, or `TOKEN_ZERO` on SEC_DIS) after a held Mismatch, `TOKEN_OTHER` after a held Match. Go and poll. Expect the opposite verdict (`0x15` after a held Mismatch, `0x2a` after a held Match). The comparator result can return to the held verdict when the injection is released, so only a verdict change shows that the retry comparison ran. Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and the PIC source 40 request line. Expect the flag and the request still set. A common-mode cell ends here and logs the N/A lines of steps 9 and 10.
. Error injection. Point: frontdoor write of `0x0001_0101` to `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` (a 1 in each fault bit). Kind: write to a read-only register. Expected response: the flags and the request are unchanged; the response code is not graded. Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and the PIC source 40 request line.
. For a channel that holds a Match, write `TOKEN_OTHER`, go and poll. Expect `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and the PIC source 40 request line. Expect 0 and 0. Read the eight words of the array of the channel and log them as `OBS-FAULT-RESET-WORDS seed=<n> ch=<ch> words=0x<w>,...`. They are not graded here.
. Freeze cells (cells 21 to 23), on the channel of the cell. Token A is `TOKEN_OTHER`. Token B is `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_SIP and `TOKEN_T1` on RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS. Both are mismatch tokens on their channel, so no match is held. Write token B, go and poll. Count the polls as `W_hash`. Read the digest latch and its valid bit as L_B. Expect the valid bit at 1. Write token A, go and poll. Read the digest latch and its valid bit as L_A. Expect the valid bit at 1 and L_A different from L_B. Log the reference as `INFO-FREEZE-REF seed=<n> ch=<ch> code_b=<c> code_a=<c> hash_clocks=<b>,<a> window=<w>`. A valid bit at 0 or L_A equal to L_B fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL reason=latch_reference`. The specification gives the comparison equation and states no word or byte layout of the latch, so the leaf compares latch values with each other and never with a value computed from a token. The RMA_SIP cell uses the existing probes `token_digest_*`, and the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS cells read their latch probes (observation taps).
. Error injection. Point: the test enable of the digest latch of the channel (`token_digest_test_en_inject_i` on RMA_SIP, the added test-enable inject on RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS). Kind: a test enable that freezes the retained latches. Expected response: the sticky digest and its valid bit keep their values through a token operation. Assert the test enable. Hook control: the test-enable bit at the latch probe reads 1, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL reason=test_enable`. Write token B to the channel and go. Expect the word writes and the go to answer OKAY and the array to read back token B. Wait a window of a multiple of `W_hash` (the multiple is a test parameter). Read the digest latch and its valid bit. Expect L_A and the valid bit of step 11.
. Release the test enable. Go on the channel again with token B in the array. Poll the digest latch and its valid bit until the valid bit is 1 and the digest differs from the frozen value, inside the same window. Expect L_B.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|Runs once per leaf. The SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the SEC_DIS digest that the bench binds (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A wrong binding would make `TOKEN_OTHER` an unreliable mismatch token. Anchor: the bound digest at the bench tap `sec_dis_digest_probe_o`, read by its simulator handle, against the golden digest. Control: the SEC_DIS match of the SEC_DIS control step (step 3) raises `security_disable_o`, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h> bound=0x<h> src=handle`.
|CHK-FAULT-CELL
|The result code (frontdoor readback), `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` (frontdoor readback), the PIC source 40 request line and `security_disable_o` (probes) equal the golden of the cell. A collapse or disagree cell gives `0x3f`, the flag of that channel only (bit 0, 8 or 16) and the request at 1. A common-mode cell gives the flipped code of the held code (`0x2a` for a held Match, `0x15` for a held Mismatch) with no flag and no request, so a no-op injection leaves the held code and fails (`token_processing.adoc`, section Matching Logic Countermeasures). On the SEC_DIS channel `security_disable_o` stays 0 under the fault, because `0x3f` does not satisfy a match. A DUT that raises no flag, sets the wrong channel flag or raises a fault for a legal flip fails. Control: the injection is applied only while the held result is non-Idle, the read before the injection shows the flags and the request at 0, and the SEC_DIS-channel cells show `security_disable_o` at 1 for the match of step 3 (the control of the "never" check). Hook control of a collapse or disagree cell: while the injection is active, the comparator rail taps show the injected kind on the copy of the cell only (a collapsed pair on that copy, or that copy as the minority copy of a disagreement; the copy-0 disagree cell expects copy 0 at Mismatch and copies 1 and 2 at Match in either held phase), else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, so a copy select that misroutes the fault cannot pass a copy 1 or copy 2 cell. Evidence: `CHK-FAULT-CELL PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> kind=<collapse\|disagree\|cm_match\|cm_mismatch> copy=<0\|1\|2\|na> rail_copy=<0\|1\|2\|na> role=new held=0x<v> res=0x<v> exp=0x<v> flags=0x<v> exp_flags=0x<v> pic40=<0\|1> secdis=<0\|1> others=0x0`.
|CHK-SRC40-EXCL
|Collapse and disagree cells. At each rise of vector bit 39 (source 40, `interrupts.adoc:219-224`; bit i is source i + 1 at `:22-23`; slots individually assigned at `:33-34`) after the injection, every other vector bit equals its value one clock before; the six unstated idle bits are not compared. The cell must show a rise. Catches an OR-network smear or alias of the fault onto another source.
|CHK-FAULT-STICKY
|Collapse and disagree cells. After the release, the retry with the token of the opposite verdict returns that verdict (`0x2a` after a held Match, `0x15` after a held Mismatch) and the flag and the request stay set (`token_processing.adoc`, section Matching Logic Countermeasures: a later valid comparison can report match or mismatch while the flag and the interrupt remain asserted). A DUT that clears the flag on a valid retry fails. A retry whose go has no effect leaves the held verdict and fails, because the retry verdict differs from the held verdict. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the PIC probe. Control: the held verdict before the injection and the flag at 1 after the injection, so the retry starts from a set flag. Evidence: `CHK-FAULT-STICKY PASS seed=<n> held=0x<v> pre_retry=0x<v> retry_exp=0x<v> retry_res=0x<v> flags=0x<v> pic40=1`. A common-mode cell logs `CHK-FAULT-STICKY N/A seed=<n> kind=<cm_match\|cm_mismatch>`.
|CHK-FAULT-NO-W1C
|Collapse and disagree cells. A write of 1 to each fault bit leaves the flags unchanged and the request at 1 (`token_processing.adoc`, section Matching Logic Countermeasures: there is no write-one-to-clear operation to deassert the source). A DUT that clears a flag on a write of 1 fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback of `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and the PIC probe. Control: the flag of the channel reads 1 before the write. Evidence: `CHK-FAULT-NO-W1C PASS seed=<n> wr=0x10101 flags_before=0x<v> flags=0x<v> pic40=1`. A common-mode cell logs `CHK-FAULT-NO-W1C N/A seed=<n> kind=<cm_match\|cm_mismatch>`.
|CHK-FAULT-RESET
|Collapse and disagree cells. After a cold reset and sense done the flags and the request are 0 (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: reset clears the sticky fault flags). The retention of the token arrays over reset belongs to `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`; the words are read and logged here and are not graded. A DUT that keeps a flag over reset fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the PIC probe. Control: the flag and the request are 1 before the reset, and the leaf has ended any held Match with a mismatch go before the reset. Evidence: `CHK-FAULT-RESET PASS seed=<n> flags=0x0 pic40=0 words_logged=1`. A common-mode cell logs `CHK-FAULT-RESET N/A seed=<n> kind=<cm_match\|cm_mismatch>`.
|CHK-LATCH-FREEZE
|Freeze cells. With the test enable asserted at the latch of a channel, the sticky digest and its valid bit after the token operation equal their values before it (L_A), and after the release the token operation replaces the digest with L_B, the value that the same token B gave in a go without the freeze (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures: test enable freezes the retained latches). The comparison is relative, because the specification states no layout of the latch. A DUT whose latch takes the new digest or clears the valid bit under the freeze fails. No result code is graded under the freeze. Anchor: the digest latch probes. Control: the digests of the two non-frozen operations differ (L_A is not L_B, so the probe is alive and tells the two tokens apart), the word writes and the go under the freeze answer OKAY with token B read back, and the release operation replaces the digest inside the same window, so the freeze window is long enough for a change to show. The RMA_SIP cell is a control of the leg (`role=control`), and the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS cells are new (`role=new`). Evidence: `CHK-LATCH-FREEZE PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> role=<control\|new> digest_before=0x<h> digest_frozen=0x<h> valid_before=<0\|1> valid_frozen=<0\|1> digest_after_release=0x<h> digest_b=0x<h>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: For every cell of the leaf, every checker that the cell requires PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0. `CHK-BIND` runs once per leaf. A cell requires: in a fault cell (cells 1 to 18), `CHK-FAULT-CELL`, `CHK-SRC40-EXCL`, `CHK-FAULT-STICKY`, `CHK-FAULT-NO-W1C` and `CHK-FAULT-RESET`; in a common-mode cell (cells 19 and 20), `CHK-FAULT-CELL` only; in a freeze cell (cells 21 to 23), `CHK-LATCH-FREEZE`. The N/A lines that a checker defines for a common-mode cell are the required output of that checker in that cell and are not a miss. A checker that a cell does not require logs no line for that cell. A leaf whose hook control fails fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.

*Functional coverage*: owner window 624, opened per graded step (the injection with CHK-FAULT-CELL and CHK-SRC40-EXCL, the retry, the write of ones, the cold reset, the freeze) and closed after its checks; held phases, the SEC_DIS control, the release, the freeze reference and the token-array reads run with the window closed. Each legal sample of `sep_token_fault_cg` and the error value of `sep_token_channel_cg.cp_code` is held with the step tag (`ef_hold_tag`) and samples only when the leaf writes that tag to `ef_hold_pass` after the checks of the step passed. `cp_src_exclusive_other[s40]` and `x_verdict_out[error_off]` sample at the event.

*Scope limit*: The test grades no result code under the freeze and no match in progress under a test enable, because the specification states neither. It makes no SEC_DIS match while an injection is active. It does not write `LC_STATE`, and a match does not update the state shadow, because `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` is 0. It does not grade the result of a common-mode cell after the release. The open tests `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test`, `sep_efuse_token_match_fault_pic_test` and `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test` keep the cells that they grade. The RMA_SIP freeze cell is a control here, and the copy-0 collapse cells and the copy-0 disagree cell of RMA_SIP are not drawn. The CPU and PIC delivery of source 40 belongs to `sep_efuse_token_match_fault_pic_test`.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_interface_status_rand_test]]
===== 6.25 `sep_efuse_interface_status_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 1 seed per regression, because every leaf runs all eight cases; tool: Verilator and VCS; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_interface_status_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`, CPU-LSU master of `lsu_stub_all_live`). The OTP bank model with its image preload. The port `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. The JTAG AXI-Lite master of the eFuse map (`axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_i`, `axil_sep_otp_jtag_resp_o`). The read and program interface outputs (state, error, done, busy, and the request valid before the guard). No inject. The OTP bank probe `otp_bank_probe_o` (snapshot, bit diff, and a sample of the image on every clock cycle) and the post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The bank probe is the anchor of every statement about a bank bit, because the read interface is under test here and the direct read is no anchor. The command tap is the anchor of the read-command statements of CHK-IF-CMD and of the program-command statement of CHK-IF-PRESENSE.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Dimensions: the case order (all eight cases run on every seed), the out-of-range address (8192, 65535 and one address drawn in 8193 to 65534), the slot of the burned write-lock, the slot of the burned read-lock, and the spare fields and zero bits of the programs. Both lock slots are data slots from 1 to 39, and the read-lock slot is none of 11, 13 and 15. The JTAG pre-sense case draws three different program bits (the graded leg, the window leg and the twin) and one shadow word to read. Each program bit is 0 in the image and lies in a spare field that is not a lock-slot field of the image and is not programmed by another case. The shadow word has a non-zero staged value, is not read-locked or write-locked, is not `LC_STATE`, and is not a programmed field. Trap filters: an out-of-range address is never 8191 or lower; slot 0 (`LC_STATE`, which ignores both lock bits), slots 41 to 47 (unassigned) and slot 40 (the field of bit address 8191) are never a lock slot; the two lock slots, the spare fields of the twins and the pre-sense program are pairwise different; the field of the case-frame read has no burned lock bit; every program targets a bit that is 0 in the image, and the field of the write-lock slot has such a bit in its used range. The testlist sets no reseed (1 seed per regression); each regression draws other case orders, other out-of-range addresses, other lock slots and other spare bits. No bin depends on a drawn address, slot or bit, so later regressions add samples and no cell waits on them. The eight cases are cheap register-level cases with their error injection, and they run on every seed.

*Objective*: A read go with `read_enable` clear sets `read_status`. A read or program address of 8192 or more sets its own address-error flag, and `read_status` for a read, and the flag clears by write-one. A request that a lock blocks sets `efuse_req_error` and neither address-error flag. `program_enable` keeps a read from reaching the bank. No bank bit changes from reset release until sense completes, because the command multiplexer gives sensing priority until it completes. A program with data 0 programs nothing and sets no flag. Sources: `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (registers `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` (section Command-path ownership: software program and read requests share one command path to the OTP bank after sense completes); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (the opening section, sentence on the command multiplexer: automatic sensing priority until it completes; sections Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control; Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization, for the write-lock of each field only). The OTP has 256 words of 32 bits, so 8192 bits (`hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc`, total storage capacity; `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields). The specification states only the status flags for an out-of-range address and not whether the guard drops the command, so the image after an out-of-range request is logged and not graded, and so is the bank command at the post-guard tap (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The data returned by a lock-blocked read and its `read_status` are not graded (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>).
