*Test Coverage*: Nine cases on every seed: a program go before sense done; a JTAG read and a JTAG program go before sense done, after the pre-sense case; a read go without enable; an out-of-range read at 8192, 65535 and a drawn address, after an accepted read of bit address 8191; an out-of-range program at the same three addresses; a program on a field with a burned write-lock; a read on a field with a burned read-lock; a read go with both enables set; a program with data 0. Each refusal case has an accepted twin in the same leaf. The flag clear by write-one for each address-error flag. The test extends `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test` (programs of unlocked and write-locked spare fields, image check after resense) and `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test` (the clear of a read error by a successful read and by a fresh legal request). This test grades only the new cells: the set of `read_status` by a go without enable, the status flags of each illegal request and of the data-0 program, the absence of a bank change for the data-0, write-locked and pre-sense programs, and the out-of-range flags at 8192, 65535 and a drawn address with the accepted read of bit 8191. The lock-blocked program grades its `efuse_req_error` flag, the unchanged bank and its accepted twin. The open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_if_status_cg` (every coverpoint except `cp_sense_status_word` and `x_prog_lifecycle`) and `sep_efuse_jtag_policy_cg.x_jtag_presense` (`map_rd_refused`, `prog_no_command`; owner window of the second JTAG leg). Every sample is held with the tag of its graded step and samples only after every check of that step passed; an event outside a graded step is dropped. The first FAIL or missing control closes the owner window for the rest of the run. Real sense is required, and the FCOV columns collect on VCS with `--fcov`.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the case order, the out-of-range address, the two lock slots and the spare bits from the seed. Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. A bit address is 8 times the shadow byte offset of the field plus the bit index. W is a multiple of the sense time that the leaf measures at step 3 (the multiple is a test parameter). Nothing shows that a busy bit rises before the first poll, so no wait ends on a busy bit. "Run a program" writes `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable`, `efuse_data`, `efuse_addr` and `efuse_program_go` in one write, waits the full window W with no early exit, then reads the status registers and requires `program_busy` and `read_busy` at 0. After each refused go of steps 6, 8, 13 and 14, the leaf reads the written control register back and requires its software-owned fields (`sw=rw`, `hw=r`: `efuse_addr`, `efuse_data`, `efuse_program_read_back`, `program_enable`; for `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` `efuse_addr` and `read_enable`) to equal the written value (`CTRL-IF-GO-LANDED`), so a go write that did not land cannot pass a no-change check. "Run a read" writes `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable`, `efuse_addr` and `efuse_read_go` in one write, waits the full window W with no early exit, then reads the status registers and requires `program_busy` and `read_busy` at 0.
. Stage the OTP image: TEST_DEV; every 256-bit field at its fixed vector; a burned write-lock for the slot of the lock-blocked program and a burned read-lock for the slot of the lock-blocked read; no other lock bit; narrow fields at staged values that have at least one 0 in their used bits; a disable-vector pair that the vector filter admits.
. Release the reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Measure the sense time from reset release to the rise.
. Case frame. Before every case except the pre-sense case: write 0 to `program_enable`, `read_enable` and both go bits; write 1 to the three clear bits of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`; run a read of an unlocked in-range bit with `read_enable` = 1; read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`; expect the three error flags and `read_status` at 0; take a snapshot of the OTP bank at `otp_bank_probe_o`.
. Run the eight cases of steps 6 to 14 in the drawn order; step 7 follows step 6 at once. After each case, except the pre-sense case and the both-enables case, which state their own reads, read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, and take the bank diff against the snapshot at `otp_bank_probe_o`.
. Pre-sense case. Cold reset. The leaf has one t=0 image, and every program of an earlier case stays in the bank. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go. Kind: a go of a program before sense done. Expected response: no bank bit changes from reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises; the flags are logged and not graded. As the first access after the reset release, write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` over the OTP JTAG port (the SEP core is held in reset until sensing completes, `hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc`, so JTAG is the master that exists then) in one write with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, the address of an unlocked spare bit that no earlier case of the leaf programmed, and `efuse_program_go` = 1; log its response in `OBS-IF-STATUS-FLAGS` (`go_resp`). This case does not wait for the window W. Sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` at the completion of the go write. Expect low; if it is high, the leaf fails with `CHK-IF-PRESENSE FAIL` (the case did not run before sense done). At `otp_bank_probe_o`, take the bank image at reset release and compare it on every clock cycle from reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises. Log `t_go` (cycles from reset release to the completion of the go write) and `t_sense` (cycles from reset release to the rise). After the rise, read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` back (`CTRL-IF-GO-LANDED case=pre_sense`). Log the flags and do not grade them. Cold reset again, so that no held request carries over. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise.
. Accepted twin of the pre-sense case. After sense done, run a program of another unlocked spare bit. Expect `program_done` 1 and the flags at 0, and the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` to change by exactly that bit. Record the program command of the twin at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. Log `t_land`, the number of cycles from the completion of the go write to the first cycle in which the bank image differs from its snapshot. If `t_go` + `t_land` is not shorter than `t_sense`, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, because a program that a wrong DUT lets through at the go would land after the rise and the pre-sense check could not fail.
. JTAG pre-sense case, run right after step 6 and its twin. Error injection. Point: the JTAG AXI-Lite master of the eFuse map. Kind: a map read and an `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` program go before sense done. Expected response: the read returns SLVERR and does not return the staged word, and no program command and no bank change appear before the sense-done rise.
.. Twin control. After a clean frame, write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` over JTAG with the twin bit, data 1, enable and go, and wait W. Expect an OKAY response, exactly one program command at the twin address, a bank diff of exactly the twin bit, `program_done` 1, both busy bits 0, the flags 0 and `program_status` 0 (CTRL-IF-PRESENSE-JTAG-TWIN).
.. Graded leg, with the owner window closed. Hold `rst_ni` low for the reset hold, release it, and start the monitor. Read the drawn shadow word over JTAG, write the program go for the graded bit over JTAG, wait for the sense-done rise, read the same word over JTAG after the rise, wait W, and read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` back over the frontdoor (`CTRL-IF-GO-LANDED case=pre_sense_jtag.<leg>`).
.. Window leg. Only if the graded leg and its checks passed, repeat the same steps with the owner window open from before the `rst_ni` fall until the grade of the leg, with the window bit as the program bit.
.. Cold reset, so that no held go carries over.
. Read go without enable. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go. Kind: a read go with `read_enable` = 0 at an unlocked in-range bit. Expected response: `read_status` = 1 and the three error flags at 0. Run the read. Expect `read_status` = 1 and the three error flags at 0. Accepted twin: run a read of the same bit with `read_enable` = 1. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and the flags at 0.
. Out-of-range read. Run the case frame, then a read of bit address 8191 with `read_enable` = 1. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and the flags at 0 (the accepted twin). Then, for each address a in {8192, 65535, the drawn address}, run the case frame. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go. Kind: a read at the out-of-range address a with `read_enable` = 1. Expected response: `efuse_read_addr_error` = 1, `read_status` = 1, and the other two flags at 0. Run the read. Expect `efuse_read_addr_error` = 1, `read_status` = 1, and `efuse_program_addr_error` and `efuse_req_error` at 0. Log the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` and the number of commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the case frame to the clear (`OBS-IF-OOB-CMD`, `case=read_oob`). Write 1 to `efuse_read_addr_error_clear`. Expect `efuse_read_addr_error` = 0 and the other two flags unchanged.
. Out-of-range program. For each address a in {8192, 65535, the drawn address}, run the case frame. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go. Kind: a program at the out-of-range address a with `efuse_data` = 1 and `program_enable` = 1. Expected response: `efuse_program_addr_error` = 1, and the other two flags at 0. Run the program. Expect `efuse_program_addr_error` = 1, and `efuse_read_addr_error` and `efuse_req_error` at 0. Log the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` and the number of commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the case frame to the clear (`OBS-IF-OOB-CMD`, `case=prog_oob`). Write 1 to `efuse_program_addr_error_clear`. Expect `efuse_program_addr_error` = 0 and the other two flags unchanged. Accepted twin (`case=oor_program`): run one program of a zero bit of an unlocked spare field that no plan program or read uses (drawn at the start from a separate seeded stream, with an `INFO-DRAW` line), after the case frame. Expect `program_done` 1, the flags at 0, `program_status` 0 and the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` to change by exactly that bit. Log the field, address, status flags, `program_done`, `program_status` and the bank diff (`OBS-IF-OOR-PROG-TWIN`).
. Lock-blocked program. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go on the field with the burned write-lock. Kind: a request that a lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1 and the other two flags at 0. Run a program of a zero bit of that field. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1 and the other two flags at 0. Expect the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` to be empty (CHK-IF-CMD). Write 1 to `efuse_req_error_clear`. Read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 0, the other two flags at 0 and `read_status` at 0. Accepted twin: run a program of a zero bit of an unlocked spare field. Expect `program_done` 1 and the flags at 0, and the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` to change by exactly that bit. Then, outside every graded step, run the enable-clear order control: write `program_enable` = 1 with no go bit and `read_enable` = 1; write `efuse_read_go` on the frame read bit; wait W and count the READ commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` while `program_enable` holds; clear `program_enable` first with `read_enable` still 1; wait W and count again; read the flags; clear `read_enable` (`OBS-IF-ENABLE-CLEAR-ORDER`).
. Lock-blocked read. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go on the field with the burned read-lock. Kind: a request that a lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1 and both address-error flags at 0 (`architecture.adoc`, section Hardware vs. Software Locks: a locked target completes with an error and sets `efuse_req_error`; the address is in range); the data and `read_status` are not graded. Run a read of a bit of that field. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1, `efuse_read_addr_error` = 0 and `efuse_program_addr_error` = 0. Do not grade the data. Log `read_status` (`OBS-IF-LOCK-READ-STATUS`). Write 1 to `efuse_req_error_clear`. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 0, both address-error flags at 0 and `read_status` unchanged by the clear. Accepted twin: run a read of a bit of an unlocked field. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and the flags at 0.
. Both enables. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go while `program_enable` is set. Kind: a read request with both enables set. Expected response: no read command reaches the bank at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; the flags and `read_status` are logged and not graded. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1 and no go bit. Write `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` = 1, an unlocked in-range bit and `efuse_read_go` in one write. Do not wait for `read_busy`, because the specification does not say that this read completes. After a wait of a test-parameter number of cycles, read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`; log the status and do not grade it; require both control registers to read back as written (`CTRL-IF-GO-LANDED case=both_enables`). Write 0 to `read_enable`, then write 0 to `program_enable`. The specification does not order the two writes; the leaf clears `read_enable` first. Cold reset, so that no held read carries over. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Run the case frame. Accepted twin: with `program_enable` = 0 and `read_enable` = 1, run the same read. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and the flags at 0.
. Data 0. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go. Kind: a program with `efuse_data` = 0 and `program_enable` = 1. Expected response: no bank change and the three error flags and `read_status` at 0 (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`: a data-0 program is ignored; the address is in range and the field unlocked, so no flag meets its set condition). Run a program of a zero bit of an unlocked spare field with these values. Read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` back (`CTRL-IF-GO-LANDED case=data0`). Expect the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` unchanged and the flags at 0. Accepted twin: run a program of the same bit with `efuse_data` = 1. Expect `program_done` 1 and the flags at 0, and the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` to change by exactly that bit.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-IF-STATUS
|After each case the error flags of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and `read_status` of `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` equal the golden of the case (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`): a read go without enable sets `read_status` only; an out-of-range read sets `efuse_read_addr_error` and `read_status`; an out-of-range program sets `efuse_program_addr_error` only; a lock-blocked program sets `efuse_req_error` only; a lock-blocked read sets `efuse_req_error` and neither address-error flag (`architecture.adoc`, section Hardware vs. Software Locks); a data-0 program sets no flag and leaves `read_status` at 0. Each address-error flag clears by its write-one clear bit with the other flags unchanged, and in the lock-blocked program case `efuse_req_error` clears by `efuse_req_error_clear` with the other flags unchanged. A DUT that raises the wrong flag, sets a second flag or does not clear fails. The status flags of the pre-sense and both-enables cases are logged as `OBS-IF-STATUS-FLAGS seed=<n> case=<pre_sense\|both_enables> addr=<a> status=<...> graded=0`, and `read_status` of the lock-blocked read is logged as `OBS-IF-LOCK-READ-STATUS seed=<n> addr=<a> read_status=<0\|1> graded=0`, and its `CHK-IF-STATUS` line carries `exp=0bx001`; none of these is graded. Anchor: frontdoor readback of the status registers. Control: CHK-IF-CTRL (the flags read 0 before the case, and the accepted twin leaves them at 0). Evidence: `CHK-IF-STATUS PASS seed=<n> case=<name> addr=<a> status=0b<rs,rae,pae,re> exp=0b<rs,rae,pae,re> cleared=<1\|na>`, where status is {`read_status`, `efuse_read_addr_error`, `efuse_program_addr_error`, `efuse_req_error`}.
|CHK-IF-CMD
|The OTP image is unchanged after the data-0 program (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, field `efuse_data`: a program with data 0 is ignored) and after the program of a 0 bit of the write-locked field (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, field `efuse_program_go`: ignored if the lock bit is set; `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`: a write-lock blocks fuse programming); the bit reads 0 in the bank before that program. A DUT that lets an empty or write-locked program change a bank bit fails. Anchor: the OTP bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`. Control: CHK-IF-CTRL, because the accepted twin of the same leaf changes the image by exactly its bit. For the out-of-range cases the image diff is logged only. No read command reaches the bank port (anchor: `efuse_shim_command_req_o`) from the go to the end of W for the lock-blocked read, and from the go to the assertion of the cold reset for the read under both enables; the sense after that cold reset is outside the window. Control on the same tap: the accepted read twin of each of these two cases shows exactly one read command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; a missing or repeated command fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-IF-CMD PASS seed=<n> case=prog_data0 otp_diff=none`; `CHK-IF-CMD PASS seed=<n> case=prog_wlocked addr=<a> pre_bit=0 otp_diff=none`; `CHK-IF-CMD PASS seed=<n> case=<read_rlocked\|both_enables> read_cmds=0 ctl_twin_cmd=1`; logged cases: `OBS-IF-CMD LOG seed=<n> case=<read_oob\|prog_oob> addr=<a> otp_diff=<none\|a>`.
|CHK-IF-CTRL
|Each refusal case has an accepted twin in the same leaf that completes. The out-of-range program case ends with one accepted in-range program of a zero bit of an unlocked spare field that no other case uses; it changes the bank by exactly that bit. An accepted twin completes as follows: a program twin ends with `program_done` high, `program_busy` low and the flags at 0 after the window W, and changes the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` by exactly its bit; a read twin ends with `read_done` high, `read_status` low and the flags at 0, and shows exactly one read command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from its go to the end of W, because `read_done` stays high from the frame read and cannot show a new read alone. The three error flags and `read_status` read 0 before every case. A leaf that lacks a twin or a clean start fails, because a refusal check without its control cannot show that the interface works. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the OTP bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`. Evidence: `CHK-IF-CTRL PASS seed=<n> case=<name> accepted=1 flags=0x0 landed=<1\|na> read_done=<1\|na>`; a read twin also logs `read_cmds=1`.
|CTRL-IF-GO-LANDED
|After each refused go (pre-sense program, each JTAG pre-sense program leg, both enables, data 0), the software-owned fields of the written control register read back equal to the written value (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`: `sw=rw`, `hw=r`). A go write that did not land would leave the bank and the command tap as before and pass the no-change checks; the readback makes that case fail. A miss logs `CTRL-MISSING IF-GO-LANDED` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CTRL-IF-GO-LANDED seen: seed=<n> case=<pre_sense\|pre_sense_jtag.<leg>\|both_enables\|data0> reg=<EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL\|EFUSE_READ_CTRL> wrote=0x<v> readback=0x<v>`.
|CHK-IF-PRESENSE-JTAG
|From reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises, a JTAG read of a shadow-map word returns SLVERR and does not return the staged word, a JTAG program go sends no program command to `efuse_shim_command_req_o`, and the bank has no changed bit; `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` reads 0 when each JTAG access completes (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc:103`: map accesses return SLVERR until sense done; `:41-44`: the JTAG port reaches the same AXI-Lite multiplexer and APB bridge through the lifecycle filter, which answers only a blocked access; `:130-131`: programming only after the locks are sensed; `lifecycle_controller.adoc:714-721`: TEST_DEV JTAG reaches the map and the program controls, and nothing executes before sensing completes). A DUT that returns the staged word, or lets a JTAG go reach the bank or the shim, before sense fails. In TEST_DEV the filter blocks neither access, so the refused read returns the SLVERR of the APB decode. The code of the program write is logged in `OBS-IF-PRESENSE-JTAG-RESP`. Positive probes in the same line: after the rise the same JTAG read is OKAY and returns the staged word, `efuse_shadow_probe_o` holds that word, the staged word is not 0, and CTRL-IF-PRESENSE-JTAG-TWIN passed. The check runs once per leg, `leg=graded` and `leg=window`, and a CHK-HOOK line fails when a JTAG access completes after the rise. Evidence: `CHK-IF-PRESENSE-JTAG PASS leg=<graded\|window> ... sense_done_at_rd=0 sense_done_at_wr=0 map_resp=SLVERR prog_cmds_presense=0 otp_diff_at_sense_done=none post_map_resp=OKAY ...`.
|CTRL-IF-PRESENSE-JTAG-TWIN
|The JTAG program write of the twin bit gets OKAY, shows exactly one program command at the twin address, changes exactly the twin bit, ends with `program_done` 1, both busy bits 0, the flags 0 and `program_status` 0. A miss logs `CTRL-MISSING IF-PRESENSE-JTAG-TWIN` and fails the leaf, so a dead JTAG program path cannot pass CHK-IF-PRESENSE-JTAG.
|CHK-IF-PRESENSE
|From reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises the OTP image has no changed bit, with `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` low at the program go (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, opening section: the command multiplexer gives automatic sensing priority until it completes; `architecture.adoc`, section Command-path ownership: software program and read requests share one command path to the bank after sense completes). A DUT that lets the program reach the bank during sensing fails. The image after sense done is not graded, because the specification does not say whether a held request runs later. Anchor: the OTP bank image on every clock cycle from reset release to the rise of the port, against the image at reset release (`otp_bank_probe_o`), and the port sampled at the go. Control: the accepted twin of step 7, a program of another spare bit after sense done, lands in the image, and the leaf logs `t_go`, `t_sense` and `t_land` of the twin; the leaf logs `CTRL-HOOK seen: seed=<n> check=IF-PRESENSE t_go=<n> t_land=<n> t_sense=<n>` and fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` when `t_go` + `t_land` is not shorter than `t_sense`, so that a program let through at the go would land before the rise. From reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises, no program command reaches `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; the read commands of the sense are allowed. Control on the same tap: the accepted twin of step 7 shows one program command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; a missing command fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-IF-PRESENSE PASS seed=<n> sense_done_at_go=0 otp_diff_at_sense_done=none ctl_diff=<a> t_go=<n> t_land=<n> t_sense=<n> prog_cmds_presense=0 ctl_cmd=1`.
|OBS-IF-OOR-PROG-TWIN
|A logged line, not a graded check: the in-range program after the out-of-range programs: field, address, status flags, `program_done`, `program_status` and the bank diff. The twin itself is graded by CHK-IF-CTRL.
|OBS-IF-ENABLE-CLEAR-ORDER
|A logged line, not a graded check: after the lock-blocked program case, a read held by `program_enable`, then `program_enable` cleared first with `read_enable` still set; the READ commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` while held and after the clear, `read_done` and the flags. The count after the clear and `read_done` log whether the held read reaches the bank once `program_enable` clears. No document orders the two enable clears, and no document states that outcome: the specification states only that a read does not reach the bank while `program_enable` is set. *Issue*: #3537
|OBS-IF-OOB-CMD
|A logged line for each out-of-range read and program, not a graded check: the number of commands that the guard passes to `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the case frame to the flag clear. The specification does not state whether the guard drops a command with an address of 8192 or more. Evidence: `OBS-IF-OOB-CMD LOG seed=<n> case=<read_oob/prog_oob> addr=<a> post_guard_cmds=<k>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test does not grade the image after an out-of-range request, the status flags of the pre-sense and both-enables cases, the data and `read_status` of a lock-blocked read or the bank command at the post-guard port after an out-of-range request (it logs the command count, `OBS-IF-OOB-CMD`). The clear of `read_status` by a successful read and by a fresh legal request belongs to `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test`, and the unlocked and write-locked spare programs after resense belong to `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test`. The read timeout and the program timeout paths belong to `sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test`.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test]]
===== 6.26 `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: Verilator and VCS; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`. A leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the leaf kind and its image, with the lock layout (the preload hook stages it from the seed).

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`, CPU-LSU master of `lsu_stub_all_live`), including `FEAT_CTRL` read as `SEP_LIFECYCLE_CTRL.FEAT_CTRL`. The sensed-shadow output `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. The OTP bank model with its image preload. The read and program interface outputs, including the `efuse_req_error` flag. No inject. The OTP bank probe `otp_bank_probe_o` (snapshot and bit diff) the post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` and the bank request tap `efuse_bank_psel_o` (the APB `psel` of the shim request to the bank model) are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The bank probe is the anchor of the bank statements of CHK-LOCK-CELL and CHK-PROG-ROUND, because a read-locked field has no direct read. The command tap is the anchor of the post-guard statement of CHK-LOCK-CELL, and the command tap with the bank request tap is the anchor of CHK-GUARD-NOPULSE.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Each leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the leaf kind and the image of that kind. Leaf kinds and weights: the lock leg 6, the single-bit image 1, the exempt cell 1, the order cell 2 and the chain leg 2 (6 of 12 for the lock leg). Two classes. The lock leg, the single-bit image and the order cell are RAND-REP. The lock leg draws the lock layout of image A: the target slot (one of slots 3 to 40 with equal weight), which takes the lock kind write and read; a lock kind {none, write, read, write and read} with equal weight for every other data slot of 3 to 40, except that both neighbours of the target slot stay unlocked; three program slots of 3 to 40, other than the target slot and its neighbours; the bit positions; and the shadow-write bits. The single-bit image draws the slot (1 or 2) and the kind (read-lock or write-lock). The order cell draws the slot (3, 4 or 22) and the permutation of the three actions (one of the 6). Trap filters for these: no lock on a slot that the leaf needs later in the same image; in the lock leg at least one of the four shadow-write slots (3, 4, 21 and 22) has no write-lock, so every blocked shadow write has an unblocked shadow write of the same operation: when all four draw a write-lock, the leaf draws one of them and redraws its lock kind with equal weight until it has no write-lock (bounded), and both draws are `INFO-DRAW` lines; in image A slots 1 and 2 carry no lock bit (their lock cells run in the single-bit image); the kind read alone is never drawn on slots 11, 13 and 15; the staged value of a read-locked field is non-zero in every word, so that the sensed-shadow compare shows the fill; in image C the staged `REQUIRED_ALGS` value has at least three used bits at 0, so that a, b and c are distinct free bits of slot 22; no reserved or unassigned bit. The chain leg is RANDCFG: round count 1 to 4, field, bit, read-back flag, version field {`BL1_VERSION`, `BL2_VERSION`} and burned index class {0, 255, a middle value in 1 to 254}, each with equal weight. Trap filters for the chain leg: data 1 only; the drawn bit is 0 in the image; the unlocked rounds never draw the version field of the version round; no program before sense done; no reserved or unassigned bit. `reseed = 4` gives four leaves per regression. The draws after the plan are, in this order, `jtag_rlock_twin` and `jtag_rlock_locked` and `uid_rlock_slot` and `uid_rlock_bit` (lock leg), and `exempt_prog_bit` (exempt cell). Every cell of this test is seed-dependent and closes over a merge of regressions. A lock leaf reads every data slot of 1 to 40 under its drawn layout, so the read cells of the 40 slots close in about 4 regressions. A lock leaf programs about five slots (the target slot, its neighbours and the program slots), so the 76 program cells (slot 3 to 40, write-lock 0 or 1) close in about 35 regressions. The 6 cells of `x_version_bit` close in about 22 (about 15 chain leaves at 0.67 per regression), the four single-bit cells in about 26, the six order permutations in about 23, and the exempt cell in about 3. Every listed bin of the test closes in about 40 regressions.

*Objective*: Slot n has its write-lock at `LOCKS` bit 2n and its read-lock at bit 2n+1 (slots 32 to 40 in `LOCKS_SPARE` bits 2(n-32) and 2(n-32)+1). A write-lock stops programming of its field and shadow writes where the field takes them. A read-lock stops the shadow read. The sensing state machine still fills a locked shadow. One slot never locks another field. `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE` and `LC_STATE` stay readable with every assigned lock bit burned. A programmed bit reaches the shadow only at the next sense (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection). A burned bit never clears across senses, and the result is the OR of all rounds. A burned `BL1_VERSION` or `BL2_VERSION` flag stays set. No step writes the `LC_STATE` shadow (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); the exempt cell programs `LC_STATE` bit 0 or bit 3 through the program interface, and the program of its other bits belongs to `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control, Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization, Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection, Fuse Fields, lock slot statement and field table, Anti-Rollback Security-Version Flags); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (registers `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (registers `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, fields `program_enable`, `efuse_program_go`, `efuse_data`; `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, fields `read_enable`, `efuse_read_go`, `read_done`, `read_status`; `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, field `efuse_req_error`). A read-lock also blocks a read-interface request on the field, and the blocked request sets `efuse_req_error` (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, register `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, field `efuse_req_error`: the eFuse was blocked because of a read or a program lock). The specification does not state the response or the data of a frontdoor shadow read that a read-lock blocks, so the test logs them and does not grade them (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, Result of a blocked read).

*Test Coverage*: The lock bit positions of slots 1 to 40 under a drawn lock layout, with the target slot locked for write and read and every other data slot of 3 to 40 at a drawn lock kind. The read-lock of each read-locked slot, graded by the `efuse_req_error` flag of a read-interface request on the field, with an accepted request on the unlocked neighbours of the target slot (`sep_efuse_interface_status_rand_test` grades the status flags of one lock-blocked read). The unlocked neighbours of the target slot. The sense that ignores locks. The single-bit fields (`SBOOT_DIS` slot 1, `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` slot 2) in a read-lock image and a write-lock image. The shadow write of `SIP_DIS` (slot 3), `SYS_DIS` (slot 4), `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` (slot 21) and `REQUIRED_ALGS` (slot 22) on a write-locked field; the unlocked write of the same fields is a control. The JTAG read of a drawn word of a read-locked slot against the JTAG read of a drawn word of an unlocked slot, in the lock leg. The three exempt registers with every assigned lock bit burned, and a program of `LC_STATE` bit 0 or bit 3 with every assigned lock bit burned. The order of a shadow write against the lock on slots 3, 4 and 22, with a zero write to `LOCKS`. A chain of one to four unlocked program rounds over lock slots 1 to 40 with a resense after each, and a version round on `BL1_VERSION` or `BL2_VERSION` with the burned index at 0, 255 or a middle value. The test extends `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test` (unlocked and write-locked cells on `SPARE0` to `SPARE8`), `sep_locked_field_access_irq_path_test` (a write-lock and a read-lock on two spare fields), `sep_efuse_km_public_id_test` (the shadow read of a read-locked field against its unlocked twin), `sep_efuse_image_test` (ten random bits and a resense), `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test` (the read-back flag) and `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test` (the unlocked shadow write of `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `REQUIRED_ALGS` and `REQUIRED_SIGNERS`: the OR-merge or overwrite, and the write of 0). This test grades only the new cells: the lock bit positions of slots 1 to 40, the unlocked neighbour of each, the sense that ignores locks, the three exempt registers with every assigned lock bit burned, the write-locked shadow write of the four fields and its order against the lock (the unlocked write is a control, and the open owner keeps its grading), the absence of a command and of a bank request on every clock of a blocked read-interface request and a blocked program, the programs on lock slots 1 to 40, the OR over a chain of rounds and the version field storage. The open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: Phase 1 groups `sep_efuse_program_lock_cg` and `sep_efuse_sense_cg` (`cp_episode`, the re-sense bin, filled by the resense after each program round); `sep_efuse_lock_policy_cg` (every coverpoint except `x_lc_prog_gate`; every coverpoint is seed-dependent, one leaf kind per leaf, and closes over a merge of regressions) `sep_efuse_field_value_cg` (`x_version_bit`, seed-dependent, from the drawn chain leaves), `sep_efuse_jtag_policy_cg.x_jtag_rlock` (`locked_data_differs`, `twin_okay_equal`; lock leg only, seed-dependent) and `sep_efuse_lock_policy_cg.x_exempt_write` (`bit3_prog_prod_end`, `bit0_prog_prod`; exempt cell only, seed-dependent, about one leaf in twelve). `x_jtag_rlock` samples only in the second JTAG read pair, after the first pair passed with the window closed. Every sample except `x_exempt_write` is held with the tag of its graded step and samples only after every check of that step passed (the second JTAG pair after its own CHK-LOCK-JTAG-RD line). The leaf kind is one draw per seed, so the order cell (CHK-LOCK-ORDER, `cp_lock_order[zero_write_keeps_lock]`) runs in about 0.17 of the leaves and closes over a merge of regressions; seed 3 draws it. `x_exempt_write` holds the resensed `LC_STATE` word and samples the legal bin only on the rise of a pass label that the leaf raises after CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT-PROG passed. The owner window opens only while no check of the leaf has failed. A FAIL of any check or control closes the window, and it does not open again.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the leaf kind and the values of the Randomization field of that kind from the seed: the lock layout, the target slot and the program slots, the bit positions and the shadow-write bits; or the single-bit slot and kind; or the order slot and permutation; or the chain round count, version field, index class, fields, bits and read-back flags. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n> kind=<kind>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value. The lock leg also draws, after the lock layout, a twin word and a locked word for the JTAG read (a choice over the slot and word pairs with a non-zero staged word, without the words that took a shadow write in the loop of step 3), and the exempt cell draws the `LC_STATE` bit (0 or 3). Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. A bit address is 8 times the shadow byte offset of the field plus the bit index. W is a multiple of the sense time that the leaf measures at its first sense (the multiple is a test parameter). "Program bit b of f" writes `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` and `efuse_program_go` in one write, waits the full window W with no early exit, because nothing shows that a busy bit has risen before the first poll, then reads `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, requires `program_busy` 0 and logs `program_done`, then writes `program_enable` = 0. "Read bit b of f" writes `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` = 1, `efuse_addr` and `efuse_read_go` in one write, waits the full window W with no early exit, then reads `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, requires `read_busy` 0 and logs `read_done`, then writes `read_enable` = 0. After a refused request, write 1 to the three clear bits of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`.
. Lock leg. Stage image A: TEST_DEV; every 256-bit field at its fixed vector; narrow fields other than `SBOOT_DIS` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at non-zero staged values that have at least one 0 in their used bits; `SBOOT_DIS` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0; the drawn lock layout (the target slot locked for write and read, its two neighbours unlocked, slots 1 and 2 unlocked, every other data slot of 3 to 40 at its drawn lock kind); a disable-vector pair that the vector filter admits. Release the reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Measure the sense time.
. For each data slot of 1 to 40, in a drawn order, run the steps 3 to 6 in this order, with the step 5 only for a program slot (the target slot, its neighbours slot - 1 and slot + 1 where they are data fields, so not slot 0 and not slot 41, and the three drawn program slots), because each program waits the full window W. Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged words, locked or not.
. Read the field through the frontdoor, word by word. Where the field is not read-locked, expect each word to equal the staged word. Where the field is read-locked, expect no word to equal its staged word (every staged word of a read-locked field is not 0); a read that times out returns no word. Log the response and the data of each word as `OBS-LOCK-READ seed=<n> slot=<s> word=<k> resp=<code\|timeout> rdata=0x<w> staged=0x<w>`; the response is not graded. For a read-locked field, and for each unlocked neighbour of the target slot, read a drawn bit of the used range of the field through the read interface. Where the field is read-locked, this is an error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go on the field. Kind: a request that a read-lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1; the read data and the other flags are not graded. Read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` before the go and expect `efuse_req_error` = 0. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1 after W. Write 1 to the three clear bits of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, read it back and expect `efuse_req_error` = 0. Where the field is not read-locked, expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and `efuse_req_error` = 0. Sample `efuse_shim_cmd_valid_o` and `efuse_bank_psel_o` on every rising clock edge from the go write to the disable write. Where the field is read-locked, both stay 0 on every edge. Where it is not, both read 1 on at least one edge.
. Program a zero bit of the used range of the field. Where the field is write-locked, this is an error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go on the field. Kind: a request that a write-lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1 and no change in the bank. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1 and the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` to be none, with no program command at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the go to the end of W. Write 1 to the three clear bits of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, read it back and expect `efuse_req_error` = 0. Where the field is not write-locked, expect `efuse_req_error` = 0, `program_done` = 1 and exactly that bit in the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`, with one program command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` within W. Sample `efuse_shim_cmd_valid_o` and `efuse_bank_psel_o` on every rising clock edge from the go write to the disable write. Where the field is write-locked, both stay 0 on every edge. Where it is not, both read 1 on at least one edge.
. JTAG read, once per lock leg after the slot loop. Read the drawn twin word of an unlocked slot over JTAG, then the drawn word of a read-locked slot. Grade the pair with the owner window closed (`phase=proof`). Only if it passes, open the owner window, read the same two words again, grade (`phase=binned`) and close the window. At the end of the lock leg, log a read-lock alone on one UID slot: where one of the slots of `CHIPLET_UID`, `SIP_UID` and `SYS_UID` has no lock in the drawn layout, draw one such slot, program its read-lock bit into `LOCKS`, resense (the golden of the resense is the bank content), draw a 0 bit of the used range of its field, and program that bit. Log the slot, the field, whether the lock landed, `efuse_req_error`, the program commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`, the bank diff and the program flags (`OBS-UID-RDLOCK-PROG`). With no unlocked UID slot, log `slot=none`. Grade nothing.
. Where the field is `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` or `REQUIRED_ALGS`: write through the frontdoor the staged value with one bit that the shadow lacks. For `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` the staged value has at least one of bits 1:0 at 0. Where the field is write-locked, this is an error injection. Point: frontdoor shadow write of the field. Kind: a write that a write-lock blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged value. Where the field is not write-locked, the write is a control of the shadow write path, and an open test grades its OR-merge. Expect the write to answer OKAY. Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged value OR the new bit. Where the field is write-locked, log the write response as `OBS-LOCK-CELL-WR-RESP`.
. Single-bit fields (the drawn slot, 1 or 2, with the drawn kind). Read-lock image: stage image A with the used bit of the slot staged at 1 and a burned read-lock on the slot only. Write-lock image: stage image A with the used bit of the slot staged at 0 and a burned write-lock on the slot only. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Measure the sense time. For a read-lock image run the steps 3 and 4 on the slot, with the frontdoor read and the read-interface request of an unlocked neighbour as the twin. For a write-lock image run the steps 3 and 5 on the slot, with the program of an unlocked neighbour as the twin. No unused bit is programmed.
. Exempt cell. Stage image B: TEST_DEV; `LOCKS` all ones (64 bits); `LOCKS_SPARE` bits 17:0 at 1 and bits 31:18 at 0; `CLASS_KEY` (slot 7) at a fixed vector with no zero word. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` through the frontdoor. Expect the staged values. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go on a drawn bit of `CLASS_KEY`, which has its read-lock burned in image B. Kind: a request that a read-lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1. Read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and expect `efuse_req_error` = 0. Read the bit through the read interface. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1. Write 1 to the three clear bits of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, read it back and expect `efuse_req_error` = 0. Twin: read a drawn bit of `LOCKS`, which has no lock slot, through the read interface. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and `efuse_req_error` = 0. Read the eight words of `CLASS_KEY` through the frontdoor and log the response and the data of each word as `OBS-LOCK-READ`; they are not graded. This is the control of the exempt reads. Then program the drawn bit (0 or 3) of `LC_STATE` through the frontdoor with the program interface (`efuse_program_read_back` = 0), and write the label 0 before the program. Expect one program command at the bit address, a bank gain of exactly that bit, `efuse_req_error` 0 and an unchanged `LC_STATE` word before the next sense. Cold reset and sense with the owner window open. Expect the `LC_STATE` word and the sensed shadow word to equal the whole word `{~r, r}`, and `FEAT_CTRL` and its probe to equal the profile row of r. After CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT-PROG passes, raise the pass label for two `clk_i` cycles and drop it.
. Order cell. Stage image C: TEST_DEV, no lock bit burned, the fields at their fixed vectors, and `REQUIRED_ALGS` at a non-zero staged value with at least three of its six used bits (bits 1:0 of each nibble) at 0, so that the bits a, b and c of the next two steps exist. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Control write: write to the field of a second slot of {3, 4, 22}, which the cell does not lock, the staged value with one bit added. Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged value OR the new bit.
. For the drawn slot s (3, 4 or 22), build the value A as the staged shadow value of the field with a bit a added and the value B as the staged shadow value with a bit b added (a and b differ and are absent from the staged value). Run the three actions in the drawn permutation: write A to the field, write `LOCKS` with the write-lock bit 2s set, write B to the field. Each write of the field that follows the lock in the permutation is an error injection. Point: frontdoor shadow write of the field. Kind: a write that a write-lock blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. Expect each field write before the lock and the `LOCKS` write to answer OKAY. After each action read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and `LOCKS` through the frontdoor.
. Error injection. Point: frontdoor write of 0 to `LOCKS`. Kind: a clear of a set-only register. Expected response: the write-lock bit stays set and the write answers OKAY. Write 0 to `LOCKS`. Read `LOCKS`. Expect the write-lock bit still set. Error injection. Point: frontdoor shadow write of the field. Kind: a write that the write-lock blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. Write the staged value with a third bit c added to the field (c absent from the staged value, from a and from b). Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect no change. Log the responses of the field writes after the lock as `OBS-LOCK-ORDER-RESP`. Then write both bits of slot 0 (`LOCKS` bits [1:0], `LC_STATE`) in one write and read `LOCKS`: both bits read 1. Write 0 to `LOCKS` and read it again: both bits still read 1. Both writes answer OKAY. Control: both bits read 0 before the first write. The effect of these bits on `LC_STATE` is not graded.
. Chain leg. Stage image D: TEST_DEV; every lock bit at 0; the version field of the version round at 0 (an exception to the image rule, so that the programmed flag is the only bit set after resense); every other field at its fixed vector or a drawn non-zero narrow value. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Measure the sense time. The reference is the model image: the staged image D. Read the 256 shadow words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and compare them with the reference (a mismatch fails CHK-PROG-ROUND).
. For each round k of the chain: take a drawn field of lock slots 1 to 40 (not `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE` and not the version field) and a drawn bit of its used range that is 0 in the image. Program the bit with the drawn read-back flag. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 0 and the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` to be exactly that bit. Read the word of the field through the frontdoor. Expect the value before the program. Log `program_status`.
. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read the 256 shadow words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the reference with the bit of the round added in its word and no other change. Read the word of the field through the frontdoor. Expect the old value OR the bit. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect the profile row of the new image. The reference of the next round is the model: the reference of this round OR the bit of this round, never a shadow read-back.
. Version round. Program the bit at the drawn index of the version field (index 0, index 255, or the drawn middle index). Expect `efuse_req_error` = 0 and the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` to be exactly that bit. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read the eight words of the field. Expect only the programmed flag set.
. Cold reset again. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read the eight words of the field. Expect the same value.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-LOCK-CELL
|For each slot that the lock leg or the single-bit image reads, the sensed-shadow output equals the staged value (the sense ignores locks); the frontdoor read equals the staged value when the field is not read-locked, and returns no word equal to the non-zero staged word when it is read-locked (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc:108-109`: a read-lock blocks software reads of the shadow; a read that returns the staged word fails); a read-interface request on a read-locked field sets `efuse_req_error` from 0 (read before the go), and the write-one clear returns it to 0 (read back after the clear), so a flag left set by an earlier refusal cannot pass the request; the same request on an unlocked neighbour of the target slot ends with `read_done` 1, `read_status` 0 and `efuse_req_error` 0 (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control: a read-lock blocks software reads of the fuse field and its shadow; `efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, register `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, field `efuse_req_error`: the eFuse was blocked because of a read or a program lock); a program on a write-locked field sets `efuse_req_error`, which the write-one clear returns to 0, and changes no bank bit (diff none at `otp_bank_probe_o`), and a program on an unlocked field leaves `efuse_req_error` at 0, ends with `program_done` 1 and lands exactly the programmed bit at `otp_bank_probe_o` (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, register `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, field `efuse_req_error`: the request was blocked by a program lock); a shadow write is blocked when the field is write-locked and gives the OR of the staged value and the new bit when not, a control whose OR-merge an open test grades (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, sections Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control and Fuse Fields); the write that is not blocked answers OKAY, because it is a legal access (`sep_efuse_map.rdl`: `sw = rw`, no lock set; AMBA AXI IHI0022, `OKAY`: normal access success). The specification does not state the response of a shadow write that a write-lock blocks, so the leaf logs it as `OBS-LOCK-CELL-WR-RESP` and does not grade it. A DUT that places a lock bit one slot off, locks a neighbour or blocks the sense fails. Anchor: the sensed-shadow output, a frontdoor readback, the `efuse_req_error` flag and the read-interface status, the OTP bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`, and the post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. Control: the target slot has an unblocked twin on each of its unlocked neighbours in the same leaf, and every other blocked cell has an unblocked cell of the same operation on another slot of the same leaf. The lock leg logs `CTRL-LOCK-CELL-RD-TWIN seen:` (at least one accepted read), `CTRL-LOCK-CELL-PROG-TWIN seen:` (at least one landed program with its command at the tap) and, when a shadow write is blocked, `CTRL-LOCK-CELL-WR-TWIN seen:` (at least one OR-merge write); the single-bit image logs its twin cell as a `CHK-LOCK-CELL` line with `image=single_bit role=twin` and `CTRL-LOCK-CELL-SINGLE-TWIN seen: seed=<n> slot=<s> kind=<read\|write> twin=<t>`. A missing control logs `CTRL-MISSING <NAME>` and fails the leaf. The specification does not state the response or the data of a frontdoor shadow read that a read-lock blocks, so the leaf logs them as `OBS-LOCK-READ` and does not grade them. The check also grades that no program command reaches the post-guard port (`efuse_shim_command_req_o`) from the go to the end of W for a program on a write-locked field. Control on the same tap: a program on an unlocked field of the same leaf shows one program command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` within W; a missing command fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-CELL PASS seed=<n> slot=<s> field=<name> wl=<0\|1> rl=<0\|1> staged=0x<w> sense_probe=0x<w> read=<0x<w>\|blocked\|staged_returned> rd_if=<blocked\|accepted\|na> rd_req_err=<0\|1\|na> rd_pre_req_err=<0\|na> rd_clr_req_err=<0\|na> prog=<landed\|blocked> req_err=<0\|1> clr_req_err=<0\|na> otp_diff=<a\|none\|na> shadow_wr=<or\|blocked\|na> [wr_resp=OKAY when shadow_wr=or] shim_cmd=<0\|1\|na>`.
|CHK-GUARD-NOPULSE
|For each blocked request of the lock leg and the single-bit image (a read-interface request on a read-locked field, a program on a write-locked field), `efuse_shim_cmd_valid_o` and `efuse_bank_psel_o`, sampled on every rising clock edge from the go write to the disable write, are 0 on every edge, so no pulse of even one clock reaches the shim or the bank (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc:48`, `:108`; `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc:211-213`). A DUT whose guard lets a one-clock command or bank request through and then refuses the request fails. Anchor: the two taps on every clock, one line per blocked request. Control `CTRL-GUARD-NOPULSE-LIVE`: every accepted read and landed program of the same leaf shows valid and `psel` at 1 on at least one edge of the same window by the same sampler, one line per request kind; a leaf with no such accepted twin fails the control.
|CHK-LOCK-JTAG-RD
|Lock leg, two lines per leaf: `phase=proof` with the owner window closed, then `phase=binned` inside it. A JTAG read of a drawn word of an unlocked slot answers OKAY with the whole staged 32-bit word, and a JTAG read of a drawn word of a read-locked slot does not return the staged word, while the sensed-shadow output holds the staged word and the staged word is not 0 (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc:108-109`: a read-lock blocks JTAG reads of the shadow; `lifecycle_controller.adoc:714-719`: in TEST_DEV the JTAG path reaches the whole shadow map). A JTAG read that ignores the read-lock fails. A dead JTAG map path fails the twin. The response code of the locked read is logged and not graded, because the specification states none. The unlocked read is the positive probe, in the same line. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-JTAG-RD PASS phase=<proof\|binned> ... locked_eq_staged=0 ...`.
|CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT-PROG
|Exempt cell. With every assigned lock bit burned, a program of `LC_STATE` bit 0 or bit 3 lands: one program command at that bit address, a bank gain of exactly that bit, `efuse_req_error` 0, the frontdoor `LC_STATE` word and its shadow unchanged before the next sense. After the resense the frontdoor `LC_STATE` word and the sensed-shadow word equal the whole word `{~r, r}` with r = PROD (`4'b0001`, bit 0) or PROD_END (`4'b1000`, bit 3), bits 31:8 equal to 0, and `FEAT_CTRL` and its probe equal the profile row of r (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc:9-11, 112-113, 219-220`; `lifecycle_controller.adoc:118-131`; `sep_efuse_map.adoc`, register `LC_STATE`). A DUT that refuses the program under the locks, gates bit 3, updates the shadow before the sense, or resenses another word fails. A refused program ends the cell with one `step=program` FAIL line, before the resense. The landed program and the bank gain are the positive probe. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT-PROG PASS step=resense bit=<b> prog_cmds=1 otp_diff=<bit> exp=0x<w> got=0x<w> ...`.
|CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT
|With every assigned lock bit burned, `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` read their staged values through the frontdoor (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control: `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` have no lock slot, and `LC_STATE` ignores its assigned lock bits). A DUT that read-locks `LC_STATE` or lets `LOCKS` lock itself fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback. Control: image B has every assigned write-lock and read-lock bit burned, shown by the `LOCKS` read, and a read-interface request on `CLASS_KEY`, which has its read-lock burned in image B, sets `efuse_req_error` from 0 and the clear returns it to 0, and a frontdoor read of `CLASS_KEY` returns no word equal to its staged word (`CTRL-LOCK-EXEMPT-RL ... fd_read=blocked`), while the same request on `LOCKS`, which has no lock slot, ends with `read_done` 1 and `efuse_req_error` 0, so a DUT that ignores read-locks cannot pass the control. The leaf logs these controls as `CTRL-LOCK-EXEMPT-ALL-BURNED seen:`, `CTRL-LOCK-EXEMPT-RL seen:` and `CTRL-LOCK-EXEMPT-TWIN seen:`; a missing control logs `CTRL-MISSING <NAME>` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT PASS seed=<n> target=<LC_STATE\|LOCKS\|LOCKS_SPARE> exp=0x<v> got=0x<v> ctl_field=CLASS_KEY ctl_req_err=1 ctl_twin=LOCKS ctl_twin_done=1 ctl_twin_req_err=0`, and `OBS-LOCK-READ seed=<n> slot=7 word=<k> resp=<code\|timeout> rdata=0x<w> staged=0x<w>`.
|CHK-LOCK-ORDER
|The field shadow equals the golden for the drawn permutation: a write lands only before the lock, the lock bit stays set after a write of 0 to `LOCKS`, and a later write of a third bit leaves the shadow unchanged (`sep_efuse_map.adoc`, registers `LOCKS`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`). Every legal write of the cell answers OKAY: a field write before the lock, the `LOCKS` write that sets the lock bit and the zero writes to `LOCKS` are `sw = rw` accesses that no lock covers (`sep_efuse_map.rdl`, registers `LOCKS`, `LC_DISABLE`, `REQUIRED_ALGS`; AMBA AXI IHI0022, `OKAY`: normal access success). The specification names no response class for a field write that the lock blocks, so the leaf logs it as `OBS-LOCK-ORDER-RESP seed=<n> field=<name> blocked_wr_resp=<list>` and does not grade it. A DUT that lets a write land after the lock, clears the lock on a zero write or refuses a legal write fails. Anchor: the sensed-shadow output and a frontdoor readback of `LOCKS`. Control: the control write of the same image lands on the field of a second slot, so the shadow write path is alive; where a write precedes the lock, it lands; and the third bit is absent from the shadow before the third-bit write, so the write would add a bit if the lock were clear. The leaf logs these controls as `CTRL-LOCK-ORDER-PATH seen: seed=<n> field=<name> exp=0x<v> got=0x<v>` and `CTRL-LOCK-ORDER-THIRD seen: seed=<n> field=<name> bit_c=<b> shadow=0x<v>`; a missing control logs `CTRL-MISSING <NAME>` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-ORDER PASS seed=<n> field=<name> perm=<order> shadow=0x<v> exp=0x<v> lock_after_zero=<1> third_bit_landed=0 field_wr_resp=<list of OKAY> lock_wr_resp=OKAY locks_zero_resp=OKAY/OKAY`.
|CHK-LOCK-SLOT0-STORE
|Order cell, after CHK-LOCK-ORDER: a write of both bits of slot 0 (`LOCKS` bits [1:0]) reads both bits 1, and a later write of 0 leaves both 1 (`hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_efuse_map/sep_efuse_map.rdl:28-40`, `LC_STATE_WRITE_LOCK` and `LC_STATE_READ_LOCK`: `sw = rw`, `onwrite = woset`, reset 0), and both writes answer OKAY (legal accesses; AMBA AXI IHI0022, `OKAY`). A DUT that does not store the bits, clears them on a zero write or refuses either write fails. The effect of the bits on `LC_STATE` is not graded. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `LOCKS`. Control: `CTRL-LOCK-SLOT0-PRE` requires both bits to read 0 before the write; without it the leaf fails. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-SLOT0-STORE PASS seed=<n> set_resp=OKAY zero_resp=OKAY bits_after_set=0b<b> bits_after_zero=0b<b> locks_after_set=0x<v> locks_after_zero=0x<v>`.
|CHK-PROG-ROUND
|After a program the shadow word of the field (frontdoor readback) is unchanged, and the OTP image at `otp_bank_probe_o` equals the previous image plus exactly the programmed bit. After the resense the shadow word equals the old value OR the bit, no other of the 256 shadow words changes, and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the profile row of the new image (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection; `lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile and Combining Rule: OR then Invert). A DUT whose shadow follows before the sense or that clears an earlier bit fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback, the sensed-shadow output and the OTP bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`. The reference is the model image (the staged image D plus the bits programmed in earlier rounds), not a shadow read-back. Control: the first compare after the first sense matches the staged image, and each round compares all 256 words with the model, so an earlier bit that clears shows at the next compare. After the resense the frontdoor read of the field word equals the old value OR the bit, so the same frontdoor read that stays unchanged after the program can show the bit; a frontdoor word without the bit fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-PROG-ROUND PASS seed=<n> round=<k> field=<name> bit_addr=<a> rb=<0\|1> otp_diff=<a\|na> shadow_pre=0x<w> shadow_after_prog=0x<w> shadow_after_sense=0x<w> fd_after_sense=0x<w>`.
|CHK-PROG-VERSION
|After the resense only the programmed flag is set in `BL1_VERSION` or `BL2_VERSION`, and it stays set after a second resense (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Anti-Rollback Security-Version Flags). A DUT that sets a lower or higher flag, or loses the flag, fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback of the eight words of the field. Control: the field is staged at 0, so a second set bit shows; each resense fails the check unless `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` reads 0 on the last clock of its `rst_ni` hold (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`: the bank is sensed again at each cold reset), so the second readback follows a new sense, and the line logs the sense time of both resenses. Evidence: `CHK-PROG-VERSION PASS seed=<n> field=<BL1\|BL2> bit=<i> word_after_sense=0x<w> word_after_resense2=0x<w> sense1_clks=<n> sense2_clks=<n>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every checker that the leaf kind requires PASSes, and the leaf logs the list as `INFO-EVIDENCE seed=<n> kind=<kind> required=<list>`. UVM_ERROR == 0. The lock leg requires `CHK-LOCK-CELL`, `CHK-GUARD-NOPULSE` and `CHK-LOCK-JTAG-RD`; the single-bit image requires `CHK-LOCK-CELL` and `CHK-GUARD-NOPULSE`; the exempt cell requires `CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT` and `CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT-PROG`; the order cell requires `CHK-LOCK-ORDER` and `CHK-LOCK-SLOT0-STORE`; the chain leg requires `CHK-PROG-ROUND` and `CHK-PROG-VERSION`. The program-round fields belong to the chain leg.

*Scope limit*: The test does not write the `LC_STATE` shadow; the exempt cell programs `LC_STATE` bit 0 or bit 3 through the program interface, and the program of its other bits belongs to `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`. It does not draw the kind read alone on slots 11, 13 and 15 in the lock layout, and the program of a UID bit under a read-lock alone is logged only (`OBS-UID-RDLOCK-PROG`). It does not grade the response of a frontdoor shadow read that a read-lock blocks, or the response or the data of a read-interface read that a read-lock blocks; of the blocked frontdoor read it grades only that no word equals the staged word. It does not grade the `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` write class, because the shadow write data is the staged value with one added bit. The unlocked shadow write of the four fields is a control here. The open tests `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test`, `sep_locked_field_access_irq_path_test`, `sep_efuse_km_public_id_test`, `sep_efuse_image_test`, `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test` and `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test` keep the cells that they grade.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test]]
===== 6.27 `sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 9 seeds; one testlist entry; the seed draws the state of the t=0 image (the preload hook stages it from the seed); tool: Verilator and VCS; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`. The JTAG master is live in this target.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`, CPU-LSU master of `lsu_stub_all_live`), including `FEAT_CTRL`. JTAG master on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_i` and `axil_sep_otp_jtag_resp_o` (`ocah_axi_vip` AXI-Lite master). Ports and probes, approved: `secure_tm_req_i`, `secure_tm_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `security_disable_o` and `efuse_shadow_probe_o` (the masked sensed-shadow output). The OTP bank model with its image preload, and the direct OTP word read through the frontdoor read interface in the strap-0 epoch (the strap-1 epoch blocks it). No inject. The post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the command statements of CHK-STM-PROG and CHK-STM-PROG-CTL) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The seed draws the state {TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET} with equal weights. The JTAG leg runs in TEST_DEV, PROD_END and RMA_CHIPLET, because the JTAG restriction to the token block holds in PROD and RMA_SIP only (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control). Every leaf writes all four targets {an unset `LOCKS` bit, an unset assigned `LOCKS_SPARE` bit (17:0), a `SIP_DIS` bit, a `SYS_DIS` bit}. Dimensions: the target order and the bit of each target; the field and the bit of each of the five program classes; the written bits of both write sources; after the plan, the digest field (`digest_prog_field`) and the 0 bit of that field (`digest_prog_bit`) of the logged program. Trap filters: the strap is stable during an epoch and changes only while the cold reset is asserted; each written bit is absent from the shadow at its write, and the frontdoor bit and the JTAG bit of a target differ; no target bit locks a slot that another target, a program target or a secret field uses; the secret fields are non-zero; programs burn only in the strap-0 epoch; no step writes `LC_STATE`. `reseed = 9` gives nine states per regression. The state is a seed-dependent cell: the five states close over a merge of regressions. The strap order is the same on every seed, and every leaf runs both epochs, the frontdoor targets, the five program classes and the secret cells.

*Objective*: With the TEST_EN strap (`secure_tm_req_i`) high at sense completion, `secure_tm_o` is 1 until cold reset clears it. While it is 1 no program changes the bank for any field, the four secret fields read zero at the sensed-shadow output while the stored value and the register read stay, and `FEAT_CTRL` equals its value at strap 0. The protection shadows `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`, `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` ignore software writes from the frontdoor, which is a control of the other cells because an open test grades it, and ignore writes from the JTAG master (a new cell), in every lifecycle state in which JTAG reaches the shadow registers. No step writes `LC_STATE` (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/test_mode.adoc` (sections Test Mode Entry, Entry and Exit, Protection While Active); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections OTP Fuse Controller, Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection); `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS), `secure_tm` and the Secure Scan Chain); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` (section Secure Test Mode (secure_tm_i), for the command block only; its list of masked fields is not used, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` (ports `secure_tm_o`, `secure_tm_req_i`). The secret-field cells grade the four fields that `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` and `test_mode.adoc` name, at the sensed-shadow output. The view of the masked fields at the Key Manager consumer belongs to `sep_km_otp_interface_rand_test`. Both chapters that list the blocked sources name JTAG (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`: software, JTAG and TRoot; `lifecycle_controller.adoc`: SEP, OTP, JTAG and TRoot), so the test grades the software source and the JTAG source. TRoot is not defined, so the test does not drive it (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The meaning of the reset-only exit is not stated, so the test holds the strap stable. The JTAG cells run in TEST_DEV, PROD_END and RMA_CHIPLET: in PROD and RMA_SIP the lifecycle gate refuses every JTAG shadow write (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control, which states the restriction for those two states only), so a refusal under strap 1 and a control under strap 0 cannot differ there. The protection of secure test mode names no lifecycle qualifier (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection; `test_mode.adoc`, Protection While Active), so it holds in every state. The four targets are set-only in every state, so a strap-0 JTAG write of a new bit lands as the OR of the old value and the bit. The strap-0 JTAG write is the control of each strap-1 JTAG cell (CHK-STM-JTAG-CTL).

*Test Coverage*: A strap-1 epoch and a strap-0 epoch in each of TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END, RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET. The latch of `secure_tm_o` before and after sense done. Frontdoor shadow writes of the four targets, as a control. JTAG shadow writes of the same targets in TEST_DEV, PROD_END and RMA_CHIPLET, strap 1 against the strap-0 control. Programs of a zero bit in each of five classes (a 256-bit field other than the two token-digest fields, a 32-bit field, a disable bit, a `LOCKS` bit, a spare word outside the five protection shadows). The four secret fields at the sensed-shadow output and at the frontdoor. A SEC_DIS match and a mismatch at the end of the strap-0 epoch. A closing strap-1 leg with each demotion, three cold resets; `sep_lc_demote_hold_cg.cp_prod_strap1_demote` (3 bins) fills in a PROD leaf (named seeds: 2, 3). The test extends `sep_efuse_secure_tm_write_lock_test` (writes `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`, `LC_STATE`, `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` with the strap low and high and checks that they do not move) and `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` (the strap-following of `secure_tm_o`, the secret blanking and the program block under secure test mode). This test grades only the new cells: the five program classes at the OTP bank (by the direct read of the strap-0 epoch), the added clauses of the secret check, and the JTAG source of the protection shadow writes. The secret check is a stated superset of the secret blanking of `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test`, which grades that the strap-1 value at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` differs from the staged value; the added clauses are the exact zero at the probe, the staged value at the frontdoor read and the staged value in the OTP word. The strap latch, the frontdoor shadow write block and the SEC_DIS result are controls here, and the open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_secure_tm_cg` (`x_secret_probe` (strap-1 exact zero), `x_secret_read_kept`, `x_prog_block` and `x_shadow_wr_block`, 16 cells: target {`LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`} by source {frontdoor, JTAG} by strap; the 8 frontdoor cells fill in every leaf, and the 8 JTAG cells fill only in a TEST_DEV, PROD_END or RMA_CHIPLET leaf, so they are seed-dependent). The leaf has two owner windows. Window one opens after the strap-1 epoch start and the baseline `FEAT_CTRL` read, and holds the frontdoor and JTAG writes, the programs and the secret reads of that epoch. Window two opens after the strap-0 epoch start and the baseline read, and holds the strap-0 writes and the grades and credits of all target cells. The reset and sense of the strap-0 epoch are outside both windows. No window opens again after a FAIL. A cell of `x_shadow_wr_block` samples only on the rise of a pass label that the leaf writes, with the cell code, after the check of that cell passed. The other samples are held with the tag of their graded step and sample only after every check of that step passed: `x_prog_block` after CHK-STM-PROG (strap 1) or CHK-STM-PROG-CTL (strap 0), `x_secret_probe` and `x_secret_read_kept` after CHK-STM-SECRET, `cp_state` after every CHK-STM-JTAG and CHK-STM-JTAG-CTL line. `sep_efuse_jtag_policy_cg.cp_state` (the bins `prod_end` and `rma_chiplet`, sampled for this test only; the JTAG response cells credit no bin, see `SEP_FCOV.adoc`, Bins not graded of the eFuse section); Phase 1 group `sep_feat_ctrl_cg`. The SEC_DIS leg and the `FEAT_CTRL` reads at each strap are controls (CHK-STM-CTL) and credit no Phase 2 bin.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the state, the target order and target bits, the program targets and the written bits from the seed. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n> state=<name>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value. Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. "Program bit b of f" writes `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` (8 times the shadow byte offset of f plus b) and `efuse_program_go` in one write, waits for the window `W_prog`, then writes `program_enable` = 0. `W_prog` is a bounded wait of a multiple of the sense time that the leaf measures at its first sense; the strap-1 epoch and the strap-0 epoch use the same `W_prog`.
. Stage the OTP image: the drawn state in `LC_STATE`; the four secret fields `CHIPLET_UID`, `SIP_UID`, `SYS_UID` and `CLASS_KEY` at their fixed non-zero vectors; every other 256-bit field at its fixed vector; no lock bit burned; a disable-vector pair that the vector filter admits. Run `CHK-BIND`.
. Strap-1 epoch. Set `secure_tm_req_i` = 1 while the cold reset is asserted and hold it for the epoch. Release the reset. Sample `secure_tm_o` on every cycle from reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises. Expect 0. After sense done poll `secure_tm_o` until it is high. Expect 1.
. Read `FEAT_CTRL` as the baseline of the epoch. Expect the profile row of the state and the staged pair.
. Frontdoor shadow writes. Error injection. Point: frontdoor shadow write of each of the four targets at strap 1. Kind: a write that secure test mode blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. For each of the four targets write the shadow value with one bit that the shadow lacks. Read the target through the frontdoor. Expect the value before the write. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect the baseline. Each write returns a response (CTRL-STM-WRITE-ISSUED), and a word that differs from its value before the write fails at its readback.
. JTAG leg, in TEST_DEV, PROD_END and RMA_CHIPLET. Error injection. Point: JTAG shadow write of each of the four targets at strap 1. Kind: a write that secure test mode blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. For each of the four targets write through the JTAG master the shadow value with one other bit that the shadow lacks. Read the target through the frontdoor. Expect the value before the write. The response code is not graded. A strap-1 cell passes only after the strap-0 twin of the same target and source has landed in step 9, so its line carries that probe. After a cell passes, write its code to the pass label, wait 2 `clk_i` cycles, write 0, and wait 2 `clk_i` cycles.
. Programs. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go at strap 1. Kind: a program that secure test mode blocks. Expected response: no change in the bank; the status value is not graded. For each of the five classes, program a zero bit of the drawn field, with the drawn bit. After `W_prog` read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`: its software-owned fields (`efuse_addr`, `efuse_data`, `efuse_program_read_back`, `program_enable`; `sw=rw`, `hw=r`) must equal the go (`CTRL-STM-PROG-ISSUED`; a miss logs `CTRL-MISSING STM-PROG-ISSUED` and fails the leaf), and the whole word is logged (`OBS-STM-PROG-STATUS seed=<n> strap=<s> cls=<c> ... status=0x<v>`), because `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` says both that a blocked program start completes with an error and that blocked requesters get all-zero default responses. The strap-0 programs of step 11 run the same control. The direct read is blocked at strap 1, so the bank bits are read in the strap-0 epoch (step 11).
. Secret cell. Read the eight words of each of the four secret fields at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect 0. Read the same fields through the frontdoor. Expect the staged words.
. Strap-0 epoch. Assert the cold reset. Set `secure_tm_req_i` = 0 while the reset is asserted and hold it for the epoch. Release the reset. Sample `secure_tm_o` from reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises. Expect 0. After sense done expect `secure_tm_o` = 0. Read `FEAT_CTRL` as the baseline of this epoch. Expect the baseline of the strap-1 epoch.
. Repeat the writes of the steps 5 and 6 with the strap at 0, using another absent bit for each source. Read each target. Expect the value OR the new bit. In a leaf with the JTAG leg, each of the four strap-0 JTAG writes lands (CHK-STM-JTAG-CTL); a write that does not land fails the leaf. In PROD and RMA_SIP the JTAG leg does not run and the leaf logs `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-STM-JTAG` with `reason=lifecycle_gate`. Log the drawn state and the JTAG leg of the seed (`INFO-STM-STATE`).
. Bank read. Before any program of this epoch, read through the direct read the word of each of the five targets of step 7 and the first word of each secret field. Expect each of the five target bits at 0, so that no strap-1 program of step 7 landed, and each secret word equal to its staged word. Program the same five bits as in step 7, each with the same window `W_prog`. Read the five target words through the direct read. Expect each of the five bits at 1.
. Read the eight words of each secret field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged words (the control of the probe). Log the first word and the per-word counts of the two RMA token digest fields at strap 1 (read after the secret reads) and at strap 0, against the staged words (`OBS-STM-RMA-DIGEST-PROBE`). Then, with the strap at 0 and before the SEC_DIS leg, program one drawn 0 bit of a drawn digest field, wait `W_prog`, read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, clear `efuse_req_error` if it is set, and log the OTP bit, the program commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`, `efuse_req_error` and the register word (`OBS-DIGEST-PROG-STATE`). With no 0 bit in the field, log the line with `reason=no_zero_bit`. Grade neither line.
. SEC_DIS leg. Write `TOKEN_ZERO` to the SEC_DIS token array and go on SEC_DIS. Poll the SEC_DIS match register. Expect `0x15`. Expect `security_disable_o` = 1. This is the control of the revision-enable assumption: a leaf in which the probe stays low fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect all ones. Write `TOKEN_OTHER`, go and poll. Expect `0x2a` and `security_disable_o` = 0.
. Closing demotion leg.
