For each of the demotions {`DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2`, both} in the seeded order, run a cold reset with the strap at 1, a full sense, the wait for `secure_tm_o`, and open the graded window. Write the `demote` bit to each selected register, read `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` (32 bit) and `FEAT_CTRL` (64 bit), and close the window (`CHK-STM-FEAT-DEMOTED`). The golden of the three senses is the OTP bank as it stands after the strap-0 programs. A PROD leaf in which any demoted row equals the undemoted row is a test bug.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|The SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the SEC_DIS digest that the bench binds (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A wrong binding makes `TOKEN_ZERO` a mismatch token, so the SEC_DIS leg could not activate. Anchor: the bound digest at the bench tap `sec_dis_digest_probe_o`, read by its simulator handle, against the golden digest. Control: the SEC_DIS match of the SEC_DIS leg raises `security_disable_o`, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h> bound=0x<h> src=handle`.
|CHK-STM-CTL
|Controls of the other checkers, graded by open tests and not claimed as new contract. A strap-1 frontdoor cell fails at its readback when the word differs from its value before the write, and passes only after its strap-0 twin of the same target and source lands in the same word (the plan draws the strap-1 bit and its twin in one word). `secure_tm_o` is 0 from reset release until sense done and equals the strap after it (`test_mode.adoc`, section Entry and Exit). At strap 1 each target shadow stays unchanged and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the strap-0 baseline and the profile row (`test_mode.adoc`: `SECURE_TM` does not qualify the feature bits). At strap 0 each frontdoor write lands. In the strap-0 epoch the SEC_DIS match returns the match code, `security_disable_o` rises to 1 (`sdis_rose=1` on the line) and `FEAT_CTRL` reads all ones (`security_disable.adoc`, section Token Activation). A control that fails fails the leaf. Anchor: the ports `secure_tm_o` and `security_disable_o`, and frontdoor readback. Evidence: `CHK-STM-CTL PASS seed=<n> strap=<0\|1> secure_tm_before_sense=0 secure_tm_after_sense=<0\|1> target=<name> landed=<0\|1> feat=0x<64b> [secdis_feat=0xffffffffffffffff sdis_rose=1]`.
|CTRL-STM-WRITE-ISSUED
|Each target write of either source returns a B response code of OKAY, SLVERR or DECERR. An unreadable code (the JTAG port reports -1) fails the leaf with `CTRL-MISSING STM-WRITE-ISSUED`; a write that gets no B response fails at the bus timeout of the access. So a refusal check cannot pass with no request.
|CHK-STM-PROG
|At strap 1 none of the five program gos issued after sense done changes its bank bit: in the strap-0 epoch the direct read of the word of each target, taken before any program of that epoch, reads the bit at 0 (`test_mode.adoc`, section Protection While Active: the fuse write-enable inputs stay inactive; `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection). A DUT that lets a program through under secure test mode fails. Anchor: the direct read of the target word in the strap-0 epoch. Control: CHK-STM-PROG-CTL, which shows the same programs land inside the same window `W_prog` at strap 0, read by the same direct read, and show their commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; `CTRL-STM-PROG-ISSUED` shows that each strap-1 go reached `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`. The check also grades that no `PROGRAM` or `PROGRAM_READ_BACK` command reaches the post-guard port at strap 1 (anchor: `efuse_shim_command_req_o`). Evidence: `CHK-STM-PROG PASS seed=<n> strap=1 field=<name> bit_read=0`.
|CHK-STM-PROG-CTL
|At strap 0 each of the five programs lands in the bank, the spare word included, inside the same window `W_prog`, shown by the direct read. A leaf in which a program does not land fails, because the window of CHK-STM-PROG would not prove that no program runs. Each strap-0 program also shows one `PROGRAM` or `PROGRAM_READ_BACK` command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` within `W_prog`; a missing command fails the leaf, because the tap statement of CHK-STM-PROG would not prove that no command runs. Anchor: the direct read of the target word and `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. Evidence: `CHK-STM-PROG-CTL PASS seed=<n> strap=0 field=<name> bit_read=1 shim_cmd=1`.
|CHK-STM-SECRET
|At strap 1 the four secret fields read zero at the sensed-shadow output, while the frontdoor read returns OKAY and the staged value and the OTP bank keeps it (`test_mode.adoc`, section Protection While Active: the controller masks the secret shadow outputs to zero, masking does not erase the stored value, and register reads keep their access controls). This check is a stated superset of the secret blanking of `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` (strap-1 probe value differs from the staged value); it claims only the exact zero at the probe, the kept frontdoor read and the kept OTP word. A DUT that masks to a non-zero value or erases the stored value fails. Anchor: `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, a frontdoor readback and, in the strap-0 epoch, the direct read of the first word of the field. Control: the strap-0 epoch shows the staged secret on the same probe, and a secret field that is zero at the probe in the strap-0 epoch fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-STM-SECRET PASS seed=<n> strap=1 field=<name> probe=0x0 ctl_probe=0x<staged> reg_read_eq_staged=1 otp_word0_eq_staged=1`, printed after the strap-0 epoch of the same field.
|CHK-STM-JTAG
|In TEST_DEV, PROD_END and RMA_CHIPLET at strap 1, a JTAG write of one new bit to each drawn protection shadow leaves the shadow equal to its value before the write (`test_mode.adoc` and `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`: updates from every source are blocked, JTAG named, with no lifecycle qualifier; the JTAG restriction of `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` covers PROD and RMA_SIP only). A DUT that lets the JTAG source update a protection shadow under secure test mode in any of these states fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback of the shadow. Control: CHK-STM-JTAG-CTL, the strap-0 JTAG write to the same target in the same leaf, which lands; the line passes only when that twin landed. In PROD and RMA_SIP the leg does not run and the leaf logs `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-STM-JTAG reason=lifecycle_gate seed=<n>` (the state is in `INFO-STM-STATE`); that line grades nothing. Evidence: `CHK-STM-JTAG PASS: seed=<n> target=<name> strap=1 state=<name> bit=<w>:<b> before=0x<w> after=0x<w>`.
|CHK-STM-JTAG-CTL
|In TEST_DEV, PROD_END and RMA_CHIPLET at strap 0, a JTAG write of one new bit to each of the four protection shadows lands, in the word of the strap-1 JTAG write of that target: the readback equals the value before the write OR the bit (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`: the JTAG restriction covers PROD and RMA_SIP only, and the four fields are set-only in every state). A target whose control does not land fails the leaf, so a JTAG write path that never lands cannot pass CHK-STM-JTAG. Anchor: frontdoor readback of the shadow. Evidence: `CHK-STM-JTAG-CTL PASS: seed=<n> target=<name> strap=0 state=<name> bit=<w>:<b> before=0x<w> after=0x<w> landed=1 resp=<c>`.
|CHK-STM-FEAT-DEMOTED
|A closing leg after the strap-0 SEC_DIS control: for each demotion {`DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2`, both}, in seeded order, a cold reset with the strap at 1, a full sense, a write of the `demote` bit and a 64-bit `FEAT_CTRL` read. `secure_tm_o` reads 1, each written register reads `demote` = 1 and the other reads 0, and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the profile row of the leaf's state with that demotion, with the staged disable vectors plus the strap-0 program bits (`hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc:341-345`, `:367-379`: `SECURE_TM` is not a term of `feat_ctrl_o`; `:53-55`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc:25-26`). In a PROD leaf each demoted row differs from the undemoted row, so a demotion that does not take effect at strap 1 fails. The write response is logged in the line and not graded. Evidence: `CHK-STM-FEAT-DEMOTED PASS: seed=<n> state=<s> strap=1 secure_tm_o=1 demote=<d1d2> demote1_rd=0x<v> demote2_rd=0x<v> wr_resp=[<r\|na>,<r\|na>] feat=0x<v> feat_exp=0x<v> row=<k> undemoted=0x<v>`.
|INFO-STM-STATE
|Bench control, not a check. Logs `INFO-STM-STATE seed=<n> state=<name> jtag_leg=<0\|1>`, so the run shows which of the five states the seed drew and whether the JTAG leg ran. In RMA_CHIPLET the profile row is all ones with or without SEC_DIS, so there the SEC_DIS control of CHK-STM-CTL rests on `security_disable_o` and the match codes alone.
|OBS-STM-RMA-DIGEST-PROBE
|A logged line for each of `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_DIGEST` and `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_DIGEST`, not a graded check: the first word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` at strap 1 and at strap 0 against the staged first word, and per word counts (equal to the staged word at each strap, zero at strap 1). The specification does not state whether secure test mode masks these fields. Evidence: `OBS-STM-RMA-DIGEST-PROBE seed=<n> state=<name> field=<name> strap1=0x<v> strap0=0x<v> staged=0x<v> strap1_eq_staged=<k> strap1_zero=<k> strap0_eq_staged=<k> words=<k>`.
|OBS-DIGEST-PROG-STATE
|A logged line, not a graded check: a program at strap 0 of one 0 bit of a drawn token digest field. The specification does not state in which lifecycle states a program of a token digest lands. Evidence: `OBS-DIGEST-PROG-STATE seed=<n> state=<name> field=<name> bit=<word>:<bit> otp_bit=<0\|1> shim_prog=<k> req_err=<0\|1> program_ctrl=0x<v>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every checker that the leaf requires PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0. Every leaf requires `CHK-BIND`, `CHK-STM-CTL`, `CHK-STM-PROG`, `CHK-STM-PROG-CTL`, `CHK-STM-SECRET` and `CHK-STM-FEAT-DEMOTED`, and logs `OBS-STM-RMA-DIGEST-PROBE` for both digest fields and `OBS-DIGEST-PROG-STATE` once. A TEST_DEV, PROD_END or RMA_CHIPLET leaf also requires `CHK-STM-JTAG PASS` and `CHK-STM-JTAG-CTL PASS` for each of the four targets; a PROD or RMA_SIP leaf logs `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-STM-JTAG reason=lifecycle_gate` and prints no CHK-STM-JTAG line.

*Scope limit*: The test drives no TRoot source, writes no `LC_STATE`, holds the strap stable, and makes no SEC_DIS go with the strap at 1. It does not grade the exit of secure test mode other than the cold reset between the epochs, the status value of a blocked program, or the response code of a blocked write. It does not grade masking of fields other than the four named secret fields; it logs the two RMA token digest fields. It does not grade whether a program of a token digest lands, and logs one such program. The JTAG cells run in TEST_DEV, PROD_END and RMA_CHIPLET only. The open tests `sep_efuse_secure_tm_write_lock_test` and `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` keep the cells that they grade, and the Key Manager use of the masked fields belongs to the Key Manager tests.

'''''
[[sep_efuse_jtag_access_rand_test]]
===== 6.28 `sep_efuse_jtag_access_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense, 4 seeds; one testlist entry; the seed draws the state of the t=0 image (the preload hook stages it from the seed); tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_jtag_access_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: JTAG master on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_i` and `axil_sep_otp_jtag_resp_o` (approved). Frontdoor master, the CPU-side bench master of `lsu_stub_all_live`, with the response code of every access (approved). OTP image preload (approved), and the direct OTP word read through the frontdoor read interface (the read interface is not the unit under test; approved). `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and `secure_tm_o` (approved). A SHA-256 model that builds the golden match code from the array content at each go. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. The post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the command statement of CHK-JTAG) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RANDCFG). The seed draws the state, PROD or RMA_SIP with equal weight. The access list of every leaf holds each of the eight (class, operation) cells at least once: shadow word read and write, interface-control register read and write, token word read and write, `TOKEN_EOP` write and match register read; the seed draws the order of these eight accesses and the other N - 8 accesses. Dimensions: access count N (15 to 20; the eight cells need at least 15 accesses, because the `TOKEN_EOP` write follows the eight word writes of its array, the token word read follows one of those writes, and the match read and the four denied cells take one access each), the go channel (SIP or chiplet) and the result of its go (Match or Mismatch, equal weight), per drawn access the operation, the class, the in-class address and the written pattern, the two free spare bits, the `SYS_DIS` and `SIP_DIS` function-group bits, and the vector (`TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2` or `TOKEN_OTHER`) that each token-word write takes its word from. A draw never makes a token value. The required interface-control write of every leaf is a write of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` that programs p (`efuse_addr` = the bit address of p), so the p read at the end of the leaf grades a refused burn in every leaf; only an extra interface-control write draws its register. Trap filters: no `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` access; no concurrent frontdoor and JTAG traffic; no token word read before the leaf writes that word; a `TOKEN_EOP` write carries one go bit, on the SIP or the chiplet channel, and follows the write of all eight words of that array; no word write between a go and its result; the leaf makes one go, on the drawn channel, and the array content gives the drawn result: a Match array takes every word from the right vector of the channel, and a Mismatch array draws a vector per word and is redrawn (at most 64 tries) while the SHA-256 model gives Match; the go is the first go of its channel, so the result before it is the power-on value, which has no reset value, and the code read through the frontdoor before the go must differ from the golden of the go, so a stale read cannot equal it; the token-block accesses run in the drawn order, interleaved by kind; the SEC_DIS array holds `TOKEN_OTHER` only and gets no go; a match register is only read and `TOKEN_EOP` is only written; a shadow-word write draws only `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` or `REQUIRED_ALGS` (the shadow registers with writable bits in `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc`), only a register that has a writable bit at 0 in the staged image, and a pattern that sets such a bit, while every other shadow word is drawn as a read only; the drawn `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` function-group bits leave at least one writable bit at 0 in each. `reseed = 4` gives four access sequences per regression. The state is a seed-dependent cell: a regression runs both states with a probability of about 0.88 (1 - 2 x (1/2)^4), and a merge of regressions closes the 16 graded state-class-operation cells.

*Objective*: In PROD and in RMA_SIP the JTAG port reaches only the token block (three token arrays, `TOKEN_EOP`, three match registers). Every other JTAG read or write completes with an error response and changes nothing. No feature-control bit gates the port. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` (section Lifecycle State (LC_STATE) Effects); `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections Debug and Test Port Path Gating, row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path; Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_mmr.adoc` (section eFuse Token Registers Address Map); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`. The specification does not state the JTAG policy in the other states or before sense, whether `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` belongs to the token block, the response code of a denied access, or the arbitration of concurrent AXI and JTAG requests. The test draws only PROD and RMA_SIP, serializes the two ports apart from one logged pair of concurrent shadow reads, and grades a response that is not OKAY. The integrity-error state is a leg of `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`, which is blocked: it needs its injection point.

*Test Coverage*: Two states (PROD, RMA_SIP) by five target classes: shadow word (read and write), interface-control register (read and write), token word (read and write), `TOKEN_EOP` (write only) and match register (read only), which gives 16 state-class-operation cells; this test grades all 16. The token match through the JTAG port with every DBG_1 and DBG_2 feature bit closed, in both states. The target readback after each denied access and the OTP bit p at the end of the leaf. The open test `sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test` grades the shadow map and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` in TEST_DEV. This test grades, in both states, the response of every access, the token word readback and the match golden of the channels with a go, the unchanged target after each denied access, the unburned bit p at the end of the leaf, and the token match with every debug bit closed. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_jtag_policy_cg` (`cp_state` for PROD and RMA_SIP, `cp_class`, `cp_resp`, `x_state_class_op`, `cp_gate_independent_of_feat`; `cp_state`, `x_state_class_op` and `cp_gate_independent_of_feat` are seed-dependent, because one leaf draws one state). In `x_state_class_op` the bin is the DUT response: an OKAY for a denied class and an error for an allowed class are `illegal_bins` entries.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw from the seed the state, N, the order of the eight required (class, operation) accesses among the N, and for each access the operation, class, in-class address and pattern. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n> state=<name>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value. Draw the control bit c and the probe bit p: two different spare bits that read 0 in the image and whose field is unlocked. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Stage the OTP image through the preload: the `LC_STATE` encoding of the drawn state; `SIP_DIS` with every DBG_1 and DBG_2 bit set (an exception to the vector filter: the test grades no demotion, owner veto or RMA bypass); `SYS_DIS` with a drawn non-zero value that has at least one 0 bit; `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_DIGEST` as the digest of `TOKEN_T1` and `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_DIGEST` as the digest of `TOKEN_T2`, staged by the digest rule of this bucket; `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0, so that no SIP or chiplet Match moves the state shadow and `FEAT_CTRL` stays at the profile of the drawn state; every lock bit 0; the other fields by the image rules.
. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0. Cold reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Measure the sense time; the window W is a multiple of it (the multiple is a test parameter). Read `secure_tm_o`; it reads 0, so the shadow protection of secure test mode cannot hide an accepted write.
. Read `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor and at `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Both read the same value, and bits 47:0 (DBG_1 and DBG_2) read 0. If a debug bit reads 1, the leaf fails with `CHK-JTAG-TOKEN FAIL`.
. Set a status flag that a denied write could clear. Through the frontdoor, write `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` = 1, `efuse_addr` = 8192 (the first bit address past the 256 words of 32 bits) and `efuse_read_go` = 1. Wait the full window W with no early exit; `read_done` is not graded, because the register description does not state that an out-of-range read sets it. Read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`; `efuse_read_addr_error` reads 1, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Write `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` = 0.
. Control program. Through the frontdoor, write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` = the bit address of c and `efuse_program_go` = 1. Wait the full window W with no early exit. Read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`; `program_done` reads 1. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with all fields 0. Read the word of c through the direct read; bit c reads 1, and bit p reads 0 in the word of p.
. For each drawn access of a denied class (shadow word or interface-control register), in the drawn order: read the target through the frontdoor; issue the JTAG read or write; record the response code; read the target through the frontdoor again. A shadow-word write pattern sets a writable bit that reads 0 in the staged image, so an accepted write changes the readback. A write to `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` sets bit 10 (`efuse_read_addr_error_clear`). A write to `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` sets `program_enable`, `efuse_data` and `efuse_program_go` with `efuse_addr` = the bit address of p, so an accepted write burns p. A write to `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` sets `read_enable` and `efuse_read_go` with a drawn `efuse_addr` below 8192. A read pattern is the address alone. Error injection: the point is the JTAG master; the kind is a read or write outside the token block; the expected response is a response that is not OKAY and no change. The read data is not graded. After the last denied access of the leaf wait the window W, then read the word of p through the direct read. Bit p reads 0.
. Run the drawn token-block accesses in the drawn order, one at a time, each as the action of its kind in the four steps that follow. The kinds are interleaved as drawn and do not run as separate passes.
. Token-word write: issue the JTAG write of the drawn vector word to the drawn array and word index (index 0 holds `token[31:0]`); record the response; it is OKAY.
. Token-word read: issue the JTAG read of a word that the leaf has written; record the response and the data; the response is OKAY and the data is the word last written.
. `TOKEN_EOP` write: issue the JTAG write with one go bit (bit 0 for the SIP array, bit 8 for the chiplet array) after the eight words of that array are written; record the response; it is OKAY. Read the match register of that channel through the frontdoor before the go; the code differs from the golden of the go. Poll the JTAG read of that channel's match register until it reads the SHA-256 golden of the array content at the go: Match is 0x15, Mismatch is 0x2a. An Idle (0x00) read keeps the poll running; any other code, or a poll read that is not OKAY, fails the check.
. Match-register read: issue the JTAG read; record the response. For a channel with a go in the leaf, compare the data with the golden of its last go. For a channel with no go since power-on, log the data in `OBS-JTAG-MATCH-NOGO` and do not grade it. The SEC_DIS channel never has a go, so its match read is always a read with no go.
. Token match under closed debug bits. Unless the last SIP result is a Mismatch from a go of this leaf, first write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` and go bit 0 and poll `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` through the JTAG master until it reads 0x2a, so the 0x15 that follows cannot be a stale read. The SIP code has no reset value after power-on, so a leaf with no SIP go (its go is on the chiplet channel) also runs this step. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_T1` through the JTAG master to `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I[0]` to `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I[7]`, each answered OKAY. Write 0x1 to `TOKEN_EOP` through the JTAG master. Poll `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` through the JTAG master until it reads 0x15 (6'b010101). Read `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` through the frontdoor; it reads 0x15.
. Read `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor and at `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Bits 47:0 read 0 in both.
. End the match. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the SIP array through the JTAG master and write 0x1 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` through the frontdoor until it reads 0x2a. If the chiplet channel holds a Match, do the same on that channel with `TOKEN_OTHER`, go bit 8 and `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH`. The next leaf starts with no held match. Each end go is a control: the code reads 0x2a inside the bounded poll and every JTAG write answers OKAY, logged as `CTRL-JTAG-MATCH-END seen: seed=<n> state=<s> channel=<ch> code=0x2a reads=<k> non_okay=0`; a miss logs `CTRL-MISSING JTAG-MATCH-END` and fails the leaf. The drawn go result and these steps make each leaf show both results: after a Mismatch go, the Match of step 13; after a SIP Match go, the Mismatch that step 13 polls before `TOKEN_T1` (a missing 0x2a fails CHK-JTAG-TOKEN); after a chiplet Match go, the Mismatch of this step.
. Deny-write controls, after every graded access of the leaf (a shadow write is set-only and cannot be undone). If the leaf made a denied write to `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`: through the frontdoor, run the out-of-range read of step 5 again, read `efuse_read_addr_error` (it reads 1), write the same clear pattern and read the flag again (it reads 0). This runs first, so a lock bit that a shadow control sets cannot refuse it. Then, for each distinct denied `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` write: write 0, read the register, write the pattern, wait W, read `read_enable` and `efuse_addr` back (they equal the pattern), and write 0. Then, for each distinct denied shadow write: read the word, write the pattern through the frontdoor and read the word again; the pattern bit reads 0 before and 1 after.
. Concurrent read. Last, with no other access running, start a frontdoor read and a JTAG read of the shadow word of the bit p on the same `clk_i` edge. Wait for both. Record the clock of the AR handshake of each read (`s_axi_ar*`, `j_axi_ar*`) and require each to be no later than the completion clock of the other read (`CTRL-JTAG-CONCURRENT-OVERLAP`), so both requests were outstanding together. Grade the JTAG response and the frontdoor response and data (CHK-JTAG-CONCURRENT). Log the JTAG read data, the clock on which each completes and the commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (`OBS-EF-ARB-CONCURRENT`). The specification does not state the arbitration of the two masters or the data of a denied read.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-JTAG
|Each access answers as its class requires, in PROD and RMA_SIP alike. For the allowed classes the response is OKAY; a token word read returns the word last written, and a match read of a channel with a go in the leaf returns the SHA-256 golden result of the last go (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, sections Token Matching Mechanism and Token Submission). A match read of a channel with no go since power-on answers OKAY and logs its value without grading it, because the retained digest and its valid bit have no reset value. For the denied classes (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control) the response is not OKAY, the target reads the same at the frontdoor before and after, and the bit p reads 0 at the direct read at the end of the leaf. A shadow-word write changes a writable bit that reads 0, so an accepted write shows in the readback. A DUT that lets a write reach a shadow word or an interface-control register, answers OKAY for a denied access, burns the bit p, or blocks the token block fails. Anchor: the JTAG response code, the frontdoor readback of the target (a real readback) and the direct read of the word of p. Control: `secure_tm_o` reads 0 (`CTRL-JTAG-STM`); the control program of step 6 sets bit c at the same direct read with `program_done` 1 while p reads 0, so a burn can show (`CTRL-JTAG-PROGRAM`); the status flag of step 5 reads 1 before the denied write to the status register, so a taken clear shows, else `CHK-HOOK FAIL`; the deny-write controls of step 16 show that each denied status, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and shadow write pattern, written through the frontdoor, changes the readback (`CTRL-JTAG-DENY-WRITE`; a miss logs `CTRL-MISSING JTAG-DENY-WRITE` and fails the leaf); the allowed accesses answer OKAY in each state. No program command reaches the post-guard port `efuse_shim_command_req_o` after a denied write; its control is one program command at the tap for the program of step 6, and a leaf in which that command does not show fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: one line per graded access, `CHK-JTAG PASS seed=<n> state=<PROD\|RMA_SIP> op=<rd\|wr> tgt=<class> addr=0x<a> exp=<allow\|deny> resp=<code> data_ok=<1\|na> target_unchanged=<1\|na> ...`. A denied access prints `target_unchanged=1 before=0x<v> after=0x<v> rsvd=0x<v> wdata=0x<v\|na>`. An allowed access prints `target_unchanged=na`; a token word access adds `channel=<ch> index=<k>` (and `vector=<name>` for a write, `data=0x<d> last_wr=0x<w>` for a read); the go adds `channel=<ch> golden=0x<g> go_result=0x<g> pre_go=0x<v> polls=<k> codes=<last codes>`; a match read adds `channel=<ch>` and, after a go, `data=0x<v> golden=0x<g>`. A match read with no go also logs `OBS-JTAG-MATCH-NOGO seed=<n> state=<s> channel=<ch> data=0x<v>`. One line at the end of the leaf, `CHK-JTAG PASS seed=<n> state=<PROD\|RMA_SIP> tgt=otp_word_p p_writes=<k> p_bit_read=0 ctl_bit_read=1 ctl_cmd=1 prog_cmds_after_denied=0`, reports the direct reads and needs `p_writes` of at least 1. Control lines: `CTRL-JTAG-STM seen: seed=<n> secure_tm_o=0`; `CTRL-JTAG-PROGRAM seen: seed=<n> program_ctrl=0x<v> ctl_bit_read=1 p_bit_read=0`; `CTRL-JTAG-DENY-WRITE seen: seed=<n> state=<s> tgt=<shadow\|ifctl> reg=<name> addr=0x<a> wdata=0x<v> before=0x<v> after=0x<v>`; `CTRL-JTAG-MATCH-END seen: ...`.
|CHK-JTAG-TOKEN
|A JTAG write of `TOKEN_T1` into the SIP array and a go at `TOKEN_EOP` bit 0 give the SIP match 0x15 at a frontdoor read, while `FEAT_CTRL` has every DBG_1 and DBG_2 bit at 0 before and after the go (`hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc`, row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path: no feature-control bit gates the path; `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, section Token Matching Mechanism). A DUT that gates the token path on a feature-control bit shows a response that is not OKAY or a match value other than 0x15, and fails. The 0x15 follows a known 0x2a on the SIP channel (a graded Mismatch go of the leaf or the `TOKEN_OTHER` step), so a DUT whose SIP code reads 0x15 before the go and that drops the go fails. The leaf fails if `FEAT_CTRL` has an open debug bit, because the check could then not tell a gated port from an open one. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` and of `FEAT_CTRL`, and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Control: in the same leaf the allowed accesses of CHK-JTAG answer OKAY while the debug bits are closed. Evidence: `CHK-JTAG-TOKEN PASS seed=<n> state=<PROD\|RMA_SIP> feat=0x<64b> feat_before=0x<64b> dbg_open=0x0 sip_match=0x15 jtag_polls=<k> non_okay=0`.
|CHK-JTAG-CONCURRENT
|A frontdoor read and a JTAG read of the shadow word of p, started on the same `clk_i` edge: the JTAG read answers a response that is not OKAY, and the frontdoor read answers OKAY with the staged word, with the bit c left out when it shares the word (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control: in PROD and RMA_SIP a JTAG access to the shadow registers completes with an error response; `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc`, row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path). A DUT that lets the concurrent JTAG read through, or drops, errors or corrupts the frontdoor read under the concurrent request, fails. Anchor: both response codes and the frontdoor read data. Control: `CTRL-JTAG-CONCURRENT-OVERLAP seen: seed=<n> state=<s> word=<w> fd_ar_clk=<n> jtag_ar_clk=<n> fd_done_clk=<n> jtag_done_clk=<n>`; each AR handshake is no later than the completion of the other read, and a miss logs `CTRL-MISSING JTAG-CONCURRENT-OVERLAP` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-JTAG-CONCURRENT PASS seed=<n> state=<PROD\|RMA_SIP> word=<w> jtag_resp=<r> fd_resp=0 fd_rdata=0x<v> staged=0x<v> ignore=0x<m>`.
|OBS-EF-ARB-CONCURRENT
|A logged line, not a graded check: the JTAG read data, the clock on which each read of the concurrent pair completes and the command count at the post-guard tap. Evidence: `OBS-EF-ARB-CONCURRENT seed=<n> state=<s> word=<w> jtag_rdata=0x<v> fd_done_clk=<c> jtag_done_clk=<c> shim_cmds=<k>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The JTAG policy in TEST_DEV, PROD_END, RMA_CHIPLET, INVALID and before sense, the access to `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`, the arbitration of concurrent AXI and JTAG requests (it logs one pair of concurrent shadow reads), the read data of a denied read and the code of a denied response are not claimed (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). No SEC_DIS go is made, so the leaf needs no SEC_DIS digest. The integrity-error state is graded in `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`. The open test `sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test` keeps the TEST_DEV cells.

'''''

[[sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test]]
===== 6.29 `sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense, `+sep_edn_jtag_reset_waive`, 3 seeds; every leaf runs one class, B or C, which the seed draws with equal weight; tool: VCS only, because the reset behaviour and the unreset shadow values need X; testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`

*Source Module*: `sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor master, the CPU-side bench master of `lsu_stub_all_live` (approved). OTP image preload (approved). `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `security_disable_o`, `secure_tm_req_i` and `secure_tm_o` (approved boundary ports). `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` and the SEP reset net (approved read-only probes). IC_RESET TDR driver on the SEP slice of the override (approved). The fixed vectors `TOKEN_ZERO` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. The post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the class C statement of CHK-SDIS-HOLD) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Dimensions: the class, B or C with equal weight, the match offset k after reset release (class B, between the bounds k_lo and k_hi that the control sense and the match latency L of step 3 give), the staged image (which fields hold non-zero distinct words, at least four, with at least one below byte offset 0x200 and at least one at or above it, so that the staged loads span the sense), the disable-vector pair from the vector filter. Trap filters: one match per simulation segment; every class ends with a mismatch and a cold reset; `secure_tm_req_i` held at 0. `reseed = 3` gives three classes per regression. The class is a seed-dependent cell: a regression runs both classes with a probability of 0.75 (1 - 2 x (1/2)^3), and the cells that only one class fills (the during-sense bin of `cp_match_phase`, the two phase bins of `cp_stops_sense` and `cp_reset_values_hold`) close over a merge of regressions. The before-sense bin of `cp_match_phase` fills in both classes: class C from its held match, and class B from its cold reset under the retained match.

*Objective*: A SEC_DIS match made before sense keeps sense done low and every sensed shadow word at its reset value. A match made during sense keeps sense done low and the sensed shadow words unchanged. A cold reset under the match leaves every shadow word at its reset value with sensing off. After a mismatch hash and a cold reset, a full sense loads the staged image; the sense and the feature control of that recovery are logged controls. The sense stop, the OKAY map read, the feature override and the drop after a mismatch hash are logged controls here, because `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades them. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` (sections Token Activation, Fuse Sensing and the Shadow Map, Reset Persistence); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, the SLVERR of the map window until sense completes); `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` (sections Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures, Token Submission); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` (section Security Disable Bypass Mechanism); `hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc` (section Isolation and Reset Sequencing); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (reset values). `test_mode.adoc` and `security_disable.adoc` word the strap sample edge at the early capture point differently, so the strap is held low (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). `lc_state_o` after the cold reset under the match is graded as the INVALID encoding 0x0F (`CHK-LC-STATE-PORT`).

*Test Coverage*: Class B, a `TOKEN_ZERO` match that lands during sense while a staged non-zero shadow word still holds its reset value, then a release of the SEP reset through the IC_RESET TDR. Class C, a match held over a cold reset with the token arrays retained, so the match is present from reset release and before sense. In both classes: the sensed shadow words held over the window, a cold reset under the match, the reset value of every shadow field after it, and the recovery by a mismatch hash, a cold reset and a full sense, which is a logged control. Logged controls: sense done low and `security_disable_o` at 1 over the window, an OKAY shadow-map read, the sense-done time, the shadow words and the `FEAT_CTRL` value of the recovery sense, a control sense that measures the sense time, the SLVERR of a map read before sense done in that sense, and the staged non-zero fields reading non-zero there. `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades the match override of `FEAT_CTRL`, the drop after a mismatch, the stop of sensing, the OKAY read of the map while sensing is open, the full sense after a mismatch and a reset together with the `FEAT_CTRL` golden of the staged vector pair after it, and the match after sense. `sep_sec_dis_reset_reach_test` grades the reset override. The open owners keep those cells. Only the new cells are graded here: the class C shadow words at their reset values over the window, the class B unchanged sensed shadow words, and the reset value of every shadow word after a cold reset under the match. Functional coverage: Phase 1 group `sep_sec_dis_boot_cg` (`cp_hold`, `cp_release`, `cp_rehold`; `cp_map_resp` is filled by `sep_sec_dis_override_test`, because the hold read of this test targets the last shadow field and not `LC_STATE`), `sep_sec_dis_activation_cg` (`cp_match_phase`: the before-sense bin in both classes, class B from its cold reset under the retained match; the during-sense bin in class B only, seed-dependent) and `sep_sec_dis_robustness_cg` (`cp_stops_sense`, seed-dependent; `cp_reset_values_hold`, seed-dependent, filled in a class C leaf only). Both points fill only inside the hold bound that the leaf writes to `efuse_sdis_hold_bound`. The logged controls (the retained match after the cold reset, the OKAY map read, the recovery) credit no bin.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the class, the staged image and the disable-vector pair. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n> class=<B/C>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value, and `STAGED seed=<n> staged_nonzero_fields=<m>`. Run `CHK-BIND` first (the leaf makes SEC_DIS gos). Run the class sequence once: steps 4 to 9 for class B or steps 10 to 12 for class C, then steps 13 and 14. Stage a TEST_DEV image through the preload with non-zero distinct words in the drawn fields (`LC_STATE` excepted) and the drawn vector pair. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0 for the whole leaf. Count the staged non-zero fields as m. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Control sense. Cold reset. From reset release, sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and `efuse_shadow_probe_o` on every clock until sense done rises. While sense done is low, issue one frontdoor read of a shadow-map word; the response is SLVERR. If sense done is already high at the response, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. At sense done, record t_sense, the clocks from reset release to the rise of sense done. Record t_first and tl, the clocks of the first and the last change of a staged non-zero shadow word in the probe trace. Read every shadow register through the frontdoor; each staged non-zero field reads non-zero and equals its staged value, logged as `CTRL-SDIS-STAGED-NONZERO seen: seed=<n> fields=<m> t_sense=<c> t_first=<c> t_last=<c>` (a miss logs `CTRL-MISSING SDIS-STAGED-NONZERO` and fails the leaf). Set the window W to 3 times t_sense (the multiple 3 is a test parameter). Match latency. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[7]` through the frontdoor and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Record L, the clocks from the issue of the first token-word write to the rise of `security_disable_o`; if it does not rise, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` until it reads 0x2a and check that `security_disable_o` is 0. Cold reset and wait for sense done. In class C, read every shadow register again; each staged non-zero field reads its staged value, logged as `CTRL-SDIS-PRE-COLD-STAGED seen: seed=<n> fields=<m>` (a miss logs `CTRL-MISSING SDIS-PRE-COLD-STAGED` and fails the leaf).
. Class B only. Draw k from k_lo = max(1, t_first - L + 1) to k_hi = tl - L - 1 clocks, so that the match lands after the first and before the last load of a staged non-zero word, or take the k that step 6 sets. If k_lo is greater than k_hi, no try can land inside sense, and the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL reason=window`. Cold reset. Release it. After k clocks, write W to the FCOV sampler variable `efuse_sdis_hold_bound` (the outcome bound of `cp_stops_sense`), then write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[7]` through the frontdoor, then write 0x10000 (go bit 16) to `TOKEN_EOP`.
. Class B only. Wait for the rise of `security_disable_o`. If it does not rise, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. At the rise, read `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the probe. The try is accepted when sense done is low, at least one staged non-zero shadow word reads its staged value and at least one staged non-zero shadow word still reads its reset value. At an accepted try, save the probe sample S and the count n_loaded of staged non-zero words that read their staged value.
. Class B only. A try that is not accepted is retried. Set k to the midpoint of k_lo and k when sense done is high or no staged non-zero word reads its reset value; set k to the midpoint of k and k_hi when no staged non-zero word reads its staged value. Write 0 to `efuse_sdis_hold_bound`. If the rise came at or after sense done, the SEP Reset Controller releases `sep_cpu_reset_n` a few clocks after sense done, and in a no-CPU leaf every frontdoor access passes the CPU-stub LSU demux, which runs on that reset, so no frontdoor access spans the release: wait at most 64 clocks for the SEP reset net `dbg_sep_reset_n_o` to read 1 before the next frontdoor write. A net that stays low fails the leaf with `CHK-SDIS-HOLD FAIL seed=<n> class=B reason=try<t>_sep_reset_release attempt=<t> bound=64`. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[7]` and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` until it reads 0x2a and check that `security_disable_o` is 0. Cold reset and wait for sense done. Repeat steps 4 and 5, up to three tries in all; after the third try that is not accepted, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. Class B only. For W clocks from the rise, sample on every clock: sense done is low, `security_disable_o` is 1 and the probe equals S. Within the window, issue one frontdoor read of a shadow-map word; the response is OKAY. When the W clocks end, write 0 to `efuse_sdis_hold_bound`.
. Class B only. Read the SEP reset net; it reads held (0). Through the IC_RESET TDR driver, override `sep_reset_n` to release the SEP reset. Read the SEP reset net; it reads released. For W clocks, sample on every clock: sense done is low, `security_disable_o` is 1 and the probe equals S. Then return the TDR slice to the value of the harness reset state.
. Class B only. Cold reset with the token arrays and the match retained. Release it. Go to step 13.
. Class C only. After the control sense, write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to the SEC_DIS array through the frontdoor and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` through the frontdoor until it reads 0x15 (6'b010101). Wait for the rise of `security_disable_o`; if it does not rise, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. Class C only. Write W to `efuse_sdis_hold_bound`. Cold reset with the token arrays retained. Release it. Log `OBS-SDIS-FIRST-CYCLE seed=<n> rel_secdis=<b> rel_feat=0x<v> rel_sense_done=<b> rel_lc_port=0x<v> secdis_first_at=<k> secdis_first_feat=0x<v>`: on the first `clk_i` edge after the release and on the first edge with `security_disable_o` = 1 (k edges after the release, -1 when none within 64), the feature-control probe. No text states the feature-control value on those first cycles.
. Class C only. For W clocks from reset release, sample on every clock: sense done is low, `security_disable_o` is 1 and every probe bit of a field that declares a reset equals its RDL reset value (`0x0F` for the `LC_STATE` word), taken from the generated IP-XACT of `sep_efuse_map` as in CHK-SDIS-RESET-VALUES. Within the window, issue one frontdoor read of a shadow-map word; the response is OKAY. When the W clocks end, write 0 to `efuse_sdis_hold_bound`. Log `OBS-SDIS-CLASS-C seed=<n> window=<w> reads_in_window=<k> probe_reset_nonzero=0x<v> probe_unreset=0x<v>`, where k counts the read commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` inside the window, `probe_reset_nonzero` is the expected reset value of the compared bits and `probe_unreset` ORs the probe bits outside the compared set over the window (logged, not graded).
. Reset values under the match. While sense done is low, read every register of the eFuse map through the frontdoor. Each response is OKAY. In the same read, `lc_state_o` equals 0x0F with `security_disable_o` 1 (`CHK-LC-STATE-PORT`). Each field reads its reset value: 0x0, and `0x0F` for `LC_STATE`. Each of the m staged non-zero fields reads 0. The compared bits and the reset value of each word come from the generated IP-XACT of the eFuse map. Sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` at each response; it is low.
. Recovery. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the SEC_DIS array through the frontdoor and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` through the frontdoor until it reads 0x2a (6'b101010); `security_disable_o` is 0. Cold reset. Wait for sense done within 2 times t_sense (the multiple 2 is a test parameter). Read every shadow register and `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor and at `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. The shadow words equal the staged image, with `LC_STATE` as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}`. Log `INFO-SDIS-RECOVER LOG seed=<n> feat=0x<64b> ctl_mismatch=0x2a ctl_sense_done_cycles=<c> ctl_shadows_eq_staged=<0\|1>` and grade nothing: `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades the recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|The SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the SEC_DIS digest that the bench binds (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A wrong binding makes `TOKEN_ZERO` a mismatch, so the classes could not raise `security_disable_o` and a hold check would have no match to hold under. Anchor: the bound digest at the bench tap `sec_dis_digest_probe_o`, read by its simulator handle, against the golden digest. Control: the match-latency step of step 3 raises `security_disable_o`, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h> bound=0x<h> src=handle`.
|CHK-SDIS-HOLD
|Under the match, the sensed shadow words do not change over the window. Sense done low, `security_disable_o` at 1 and the OKAY frontdoor shadow-map read are logged controls inside the `PASS` line; `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades them (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and the Shadow Map). In class B the sensed shadow words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` equal their values at the rise of `security_disable_o` over the window and again after the TDR release, and sense done stays low after the TDR release. In class C the sensed shadow words equal their RDL reset values from reset release over the window, on every bit of a field that declares a reset (`0x0F` for the `LC_STATE` word; `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Reset Persistence). A DUT that senses under the match, loads a word after the match, or restarts sensing at the SEP reset release fails. The class B try is accepted only when at least one staged non-zero word is loaded and at least one is still at reset at the rise (step 5), so the probe holds a loaded word and an unloaded word that a DUT could change; the class B line carries both counts and requires each to be at least 1. Anchor: `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `security_disable_o` and `efuse_shadow_probe_o` on every clock, and the frontdoor response code. Controls: the control sense shows sense done and the staged non-zero shadow words inside the measured sense time (`ctl_sense_done_cycle`); the probe of the control sense at clock k differs from its probe at sense done, so the same words do change after the class B offset (`ctl_probe_changed=1`); a map read before sense done in the control sense answers SLVERR (`ctl_map_resp`); and in class B the SEP reset net reads held (0) before the TDR step and released after it (`sep_rst_pre=0`, `sep_rst_released=1`), so the release is an edge that the TDR made and the window after it tests a released reset. In class C no read command reaches the bank port `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from reset release over the window; its controls are the read commands that the control sense and the recovery sense show at the tap, and a leaf whose control sense shows no read command at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-SDIS-HOLD PASS seed=<n> class=<B\|C> t_sense=<c> window=<w> probe_unchanged=<1\|na> shadow_reset_over_window=<1\|na> ctl_sense_done_cycle=<c> ctl_probe_changed=1 ctl_map_resp=SLVERR sep_rst_released=<1\|na> sep_rst_pre=<0\|na> match_lat=<c> k=<c\|na> n_loaded=<n\|na> n_reset=<n\|na> ctl_sense_done=0 ctl_sdis_o=1 hold_map_resp=OKAY ctl_sense_cmds=<n\|na> held_clocks=<c> bad_probe_clocks=0 reads_in_window=<0\|na>`. `ctl_map_resp` is the map read before sense done in the control sense; `hold_map_resp` is the map read inside the window; `reads_in_window` counts the read commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` inside the class C window. Each class B try logs `INFO-SDIS-TRY seed=<n> attempt=<t> k=<c> k_lo=<c> k_hi=<c> rise_clk=<c> sense_done=<0\|1> n_loaded=<n> n_reset=<n> accepted=<0\|1>`.
|CHK-LC-STATE-PORT
|At the reset-value reads after the cold reset under the match, with sense done 0 and `security_disable_o` 1, `lc_state_o` equals the `LC_STATE` reset value 0x0F (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc:62-64`; `hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_efuse_map/sep_efuse_map.rdl:577`; `doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc:202`). Both classes. Evidence: `CHK-LC-STATE-PORT PASS: seed=<n> class=<B\|C> got_port=0x0f exp=0x0f shadow=0x0000000f sense_done=0 secdis=1`.
|OBS-SDIS-FIRST-CYCLE
|Class C only. A logged line, not a graded check: on the first `clk_i` edge after the release and on the first edge with `security_disable_o` = 1 (k edges after the release, -1 when none within 64), the feature-control probe. No text states the feature-control value on those first cycles. Evidence: `OBS-SDIS-FIRST-CYCLE seed=<n> rel_secdis=<b> rel_feat=0x<v> rel_sense_done=<b> rel_lc_port=0x<v> secdis_first_at=<k> secdis_first_feat=0x<v>`.
|CHK-SDIS-RESET-VALUES
|After a cold reset under the match, every shadow field reads its described reset value through the frontdoor (0x0, and `0x0F` for `LC_STATE`, the INVALID hold that `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` gives before sensing completes), and each response is OKAY. The compared bits and the reset value of each word come from the generated IP-XACT of the eFuse map (`hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/ipxact/sep.xml`): every bit of a field that declares a reset is compared, `rsvd` and `reserved` fields with access R and reset 0x0 included. Only a bit outside every field, or of a field with no declared reset, is masked and logged as `rsvd=0x<v>`; the eFuse map has no such bit. Sense done low at each response is a logged control that `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades; an unknown sense done fails the leaf (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Reset Persistence; `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc`, reset values). The staged non-zero fields read 0. In class B the cold reset follows an interrupted sense in which at least one staged non-zero word is loaded and at least one is not loaded (step 5, `n_loaded` in the evidence line), so a DUT that keeps the partial words has a loaded word to keep and fails. In class C the cold reset follows a full sense, so a DUT that keeps the sensed words fails; `CTRL-SDIS-PRE-COLD-STAGED` (step 3) reads the staged words after that sense. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of every register of the map. Control: the same fields read non-zero in the control sense of the leaf (step 3), so a zero reading is a cleared word and not an unwritten one. Evidence: `CHK-SDIS-RESET-VALUES PASS seed=<n> class=<B\|C> fields=<n> staged_nonzero=<m> n_loaded=<n\|na> all_reset_value=1 ctl_sense_done=0 rsvd=0x<v> lc_state=0x0000000f first_bad=none`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes, `CHK-LC-STATE-PORT` among the required ids. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The strap sample edge at the early capture point and the secure scan chain are not claimed (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The revision enable of SEC_DIS keeps its A0 value; the test does not set it. A token match with `secure_tm_req_i` at 1 is not made. The sense stop, the OKAY map read, the feature override, the drop after a mismatch, the sense-done time of the recovery sense, its shadow words and its `FEAT_CTRL` are logged controls here, and `sep_sec_dis_override_test` keeps their grade. The leaf makes SEC_DIS gos, so it runs `CHK-BIND` by the common rule of this bucket. The reset override is graded in `sep_sec_dis_reset_reach_test`. The match after sense is graded in `sep_sec_dis_override_test`. The SMC fuse controller is not driven.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test]]
===== 6.30 `sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense with the injected shim response, 3 seeds; every leaf runs the injected sense of leg A at one word class that the seed draws, with one cold reset; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor master, the CPU-side bench master of `lsu_stub_all_live`, with the response code of every access (approved). OTP image preload and the direct OTP word read through the frontdoor read interface (approved). `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the program interface outputs (busy, done, error) (approved). Added hooks, error injects that the test adds to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`: the bank-model sense status inject, `efuse_sense_status_inject_i` with `efuse_sense_status_word_i`, which sets the status bit on the sense response of one word; and the shim response hold `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i`, which withholds the shim response of a program request on the `sep` input `efuse_shim_command_resp_i` while it is high. Observation taps that the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list: the shim response taps `efuse_shim_resp_valid_o`, `efuse_shim_resp_status_o` and `efuse_shim_resp_data_o` at the `sep` input `efuse_shim_command_resp_i` (the sense responses of CHK-SENSE-STATUS), the sensing FSM taps `efuse_shadow_resp_valid_o` and `efuse_shadow_sense_done_o` (the response valid at the input and `fuse_sense_done_o` at the output of `efuse_shadow_regs`, the ports where the specification states the sense-done latency of CHK-SENSE-STATUS), and the post-guard fuse command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the hook control of the shim response hold in CHK-PROG-LIFE).

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Dimensions: the word class of w, one per leaf, with weight 2 for each edge class (the first allowed word 10, the last allowed word 254) and weight 1 for each other class (the word of the 32-bit field `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE` at offset 0x88, and a word drawn from 11 to 253 inside a 256-bit field), the drawn word of the last class, the three spare bits, the program timeout cycles T, the hold multiple m and, last, the control word of the last sense (one of the shadow words 0 to 9 and 255 whose staged value differs from its RDL reset value; with the fixed image these are 3, 6, 7, 8, 9 and 255). Trap filters: w in 10 to 254; the staged value of each w and of its w + 1 is non-zero; each spare bit reads 0 in the image and its field is unlocked, and the three bits are different; the timeout cell draws T so that m times T clocks fit the run budget, with m at least 2. `reseed = 3` samples other word classes, drawn words, spare bits, timeout cycles and hold multiples. The word class is a seed draw with no FCOV bin of its own (`cp_sense_status_word` has one bin for every word of 10 to 254, and CHK-SENSE-STATUS grades the drawn word in every leaf). All four classes run over a merge of about 4 regressions, because each of the two lighter classes takes 1 in 6 of the leaves and a regression has 3 leaves.

*Objective*: A shim response whose status bit is set during sensing still advances the sensing word counter and leaves that shadow word unwritten, so it reads its reset value, and sense done still rises. Every other word loads its staged value. A program request that waits for its shim response shows `program_busy` and not `program_done` while it waits with the program timeout disabled, and shows `program_done` with `program_status` 0 and no busy after the response. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, the paragraph on the sensing sequence: a strobe with its status bit set advances the counter and leaves that word unwritten); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (the reset value 0x0 of every field except `LC_STATE`); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (registers `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT`); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc` (section Fuse Command Protocol, the response status bit). The specification does not state what a program request reports when its timeout fires, and it states two readings for a status on the last word (255): that word is left unwritten, and the strobe at counter 255 stores the last word (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The timeout cell and the shadow word of a word-255 control sense are logged and not graded. A control sense on words 0 to 9 is graded by CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD, and the feature controls after every control sense by CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD-FEAT; Leg A does not draw words 0 to 9 and 255.

*Test Coverage*: Leg A, a sense response with its status bit set at one word w of 10 to 254, one drawn word class per leaf: the first allowed word, the last allowed word, a word of a 32-bit field or a word inside a 256-bit field; the word w reads its reset value, its neighbour w + 1 and every other word read the staged value, and sense done rises. Leg B, the program lifecycle with the program timeout disabled: a program with a normal response ends with done, status 0 and no busy; a program with a withheld response shows busy and not done, and shows done, status 0 and no busy after the release. A timeout cell with the timeout enabled and the response withheld, logged and not graded. `sep_efuse_sense_test` and `sep_efuse_image_test` sense an image with every response normal, `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test` drops a program write through the bank-model plusargs, and `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test` grades the read timeout. The open owners keep those cells. Only the new cells are graded here: the sense response with the status bit set and the busy and done lifecycle of a program request whose shim response is late. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_if_status_cg` (`cp_sense_status_word`, `x_prog_lifecycle` and `cp_sense_shape` with the bins `one_read_cmd` and `done_after_last_strobe`; all fill on every seed). The control sense and the first injected sense are graded with the owner window closed. A second, identical injected sense runs inside the owner window only when every check and control so far passed, and the window closes after its own CHK-SENSE-STATUS line. `cp_sense_shape` and `cp_sense_status_word` of that sense are held and sample only after that line passed. The held program of leg B runs inside the owner window; `x_prog_lifecycle` is held and samples only after CHK-PROG-LIFE and its hook control passed. The first FAIL closes the window for the rest of the leaf.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw from the seed the word class, the word of the drawn class, the three spare bits a, b and c, T, m and the control word. The class gives one word w and its neighbour w + 1. Print `INFO-SEED seed=<n>` and one `INFO-DRAW` line per drawn value. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Stage a TEST_DEV image through the preload with non-zero distinct words in every field of shadow words 10 to 254, every lock bit 0, the fixed filter-legal disable-vector pair of the image rules (no seed draw) and the other fields by the image rules. Each of a, b and c sits at a 0 bit of its staged spare word. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0.
. Leg A control sense. With both injects low, cold reset. From reset release, count the clocks until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises; the count is t_sense. Read the probe `efuse_shadow_probe_o`; every word equals its staged value, with `LC_STATE` as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}`. Read words w and w + 1 through the frontdoor; both read their staged value, which is non-zero. Record the commands at `efuse_shim_cmd_*_o` and the response strobes from the first `rst_ni`-high clock to the rise of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Leg A injected sense, at the drawn word class. Drive `efuse_sense_status_word_i` = w and `efuse_sense_status_inject_i` = 1. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` within a multiple of t_sense (the multiple is a test parameter); a timeout fails CHK-SENSE-STATUS.
. After sense done, read `efuse_shadow_probe_o` for all 256 words. Read words w and w + 1 through the frontdoor. Read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` through the frontdoor. Drive `efuse_sense_status_inject_i` = 0. Repeat the injected sense of the two steps above once more, read and grade as before (`sense=injected_binned`). The owner window is open for this sense only when no check or control so far failed, and it closes after the grade of this sense.
. Leg B control program. With both injects low, cold reset and wait for sense done. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` with `program_req_timeout_enable` = 0. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` = the bit address of a, `efuse_program_read_back` = 0 and `efuse_program_go` = 1. Poll `program_done` until it reads 1. Read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`. Read the word of a through the direct read; bit a reads 1.
. Leg B held program. Cold reset and wait for sense done; the program interface registers read their reset values. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` with `program_req_timeout_enable` = 0. Drive `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i` = 1. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` = the bit address of b and `efuse_program_go` = 1.
. Poll `efuse_program_busy_o` and `program_busy` until both read 1. Then, for a window of a multiple of t_sense (a test parameter), sample the program interface outputs on every clock and read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` through the frontdoor at least 4 times (a test parameter): `program_busy` is 1 and `program_done` is 0 at every sample and every read.
. Drive `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i` = 0. Poll `program_done` until it reads 1. Read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and the program interface outputs. Read the word of b through the direct read; bit b reads 1.
. Timeout cell. Cold reset and wait for sense done. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` with `program_req_timeout_enable` = 1 and `program_req_timeout_cycles` = T. Drive `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i` = 1. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` = the bit address of c and `efuse_program_go` = 1. After m times T clocks, read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and log `program_busy`, `program_done` and `program_status`. Drive `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i` = 0.
. Control-word sense. Clear `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`. Drive `efuse_sense_status_word_i` = the drawn control word and `efuse_sense_status_inject_i` = 1, cold reset, and wait, bounded, for sense done. Wait 20 clocks. For every drawn word, grade `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` against the profile row of the sensed `LC_STATE`, `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` shadows with the drawn word at its RDL reset value (CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD-FEAT). For a word of 0 to 9, grade the shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` against its RDL reset value and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` = 1 (CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD). For word 255, log the shadow word, the staged word, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` with `OBS-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD`. Drive `efuse_sense_status_inject_i` = 0. The step runs with the graded window closed and samples no bin.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-SENSE-STATUS
|The check runs on three senses: `sense=control`, `sense=injected` and `sense=injected_binned`. In each, from the first `rst_ni`-high clock to the `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rise, exactly one READ command (`FUSE_COMMAND_READ`, `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc`, Command Types) appears at `efuse_shim_cmd_*_o`, exactly 256 response strobes (the map size divided by 4) appear at `efuse_shim_resp_valid_o`, and at the sensing FSM ports (`efuse_shadow_resp_valid_o` at the response input and `efuse_shadow_sense_done_o` at `fuse_sense_done_o` of `efuse_shadow_regs`) 256 strobes appear and `fuse_sense_done_o` first reads 1 exactly two cycles after the cycle of the last strobe (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc:96`: one READ for all words; `:97-98`: one strobe per word; `:101-102` and the sensing timing diagram: the last word in cycle n+1 moves the FSM to ST_FINISHED in cycle n+2, and `fuse_sense_done_o` is high from cycle n+3). A DUT that issues one READ per word, no READ, a wrong strobe count, a done on the last strobe, a done one cycle early or any cycle late, or a done tied to 1 fails, and a dead command tap gives zero READ commands and fails. In the two injected senses, sense done rises within the bound, the sensed-shadow output `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and the frontdoor read of word w equal its RDL reset value (`word_reset`; 0x0 for every word of 10 to 254 today), every other word at the probe equals its staged value, and `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS.efuse_sense_done` reads 1 (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing; `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc`, reset values; `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`). The frontdoor read of word w + 1 equals its staged value. At the `sep` input `efuse_shim_command_resp_i` the injected sense has 256 responses, exactly one of them with the status bit set, at word w, and that response carries the staged data of word w, so the inject lands on the word under test and the DUT receives the data that it must discard. A DUT that stops the counter, stores the data of a status word, shifts the following words by one or hangs fails. Anchor: `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, the frontdoor readback, the shim response taps and the sensing FSM taps. Control: the control sense of the same leaf loads word w with its staged value, which is non-zero, so a zero reading comes from the status bit and not from an unwritten word; the probe equals the staged image and words w and w + 1 read their staged values (`CTRL-SENSE-STATUS seen: seed=<n> word=<w> ctl_word=0x<v> ctl_next=0x<v> staged=0x<v> probe_equal=256`). Evidence: `CHK-SENSE-STATUS PASS seed=<n> word=<w> word_class=<c> got_probe=0x0 got_read=0x0 staged=0x<v> next_read=0x<v> others_equal=1 others_bad=none sense_done=1 t_sense_inj=<c> status_sense_done=1 ctl_word=0x<v> resp_strobes=256 resp_status_words=<w> resp_w_status=1 resp_w_data=0x<v>`.
|CHK-PROG-LIFE
|With the program timeout disabled, `program_busy` reads 1 and `program_done` reads 0 over the window; after the release `program_done` reads 1, `program_status` reads 0, `program_busy` reads 0 and the direct read of the word of the programmed bit reads it set (`hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT`; `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc`, section Fuse Command Protocol). A DUT that completes without a response, loses the request or reports an error after a late response fails. Anchor: the program interface outputs on every clock, the frontdoor readback of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and the direct read of the programmed word. Control: the same program without a withheld response, in the same leaf from a cold reset, ends with done 1, status 0 and busy 0 and sets its bit at the direct read, so the done bit can read 1 and the direct read can show a burn; it logs `CTRL-PROG-LIFE seen: seed=<n> bit_addr=<a> ctl_done=1 ctl_pg=0x<v> ctl_bit_read=1 ctl_word=0x<w>`, and a miss logs `CTRL-MISSING PROG-LIFE` and fails the leaf. Hook control of the hold: the held program shows at least one program command with the address of b at the post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o`, and the control program completes in fewer clocks than the held span from the go to the end of the window, logged as `CTRL-HOOK seen: seed=<n> check=PROG-LIFE held_prog_cmds=<k> bit_addr=<a> ctl_clks=<c> held_span=<c>`; a miss fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-PROG-LIFE PASS seed=<n> bit_addr=<a> held_busy=1 held_done=0 rel_done=1 rel_status=0 rel_busy=0 bit_read=1 ctl_done=1 ctl_bit_read=1`.
|OBS-PROG-TIMEOUT
|A logged cell, not a graded check. The bits `program_busy`, `program_done` and `program_status` after the timeout window are logged as `OBS-PROG-TIMEOUT` and are not graded, because the specification does not state what a program request reports when its timeout fires. Evidence: `OBS-PROG-TIMEOUT LOG seed=<n> cycles=<c> held=<k>x busy=<0\|1> done=<0\|1> status=<0\|1> bit_addr=<a>`.
|CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD
|A sense whose response for one control word (drawn from shadow words 0 to 9 whose staged value differs from the RDL reset value) has its status bit set: that word reads its RDL reset value at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` (`LC_STATE` 0xF, every other word 0x0) and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` reads 1 within the bound (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing: a strobe with its status bit set advances the counter and leaves that word unwritten; `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc`, reset values). The staged value differs from the reset value, so a DUT that stores the data of the status word fails, and a DUT that stops the counter fails on sense done. Anchor: `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Evidence: `CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD PASS seed=<n> word=<w> shadow=0x<v> want=0x<v> staged=0x<v> sense_done=1 t_sense=<c>`.
|OBS-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD
|A logged line, not a graded check: the same sense when the drawn control word is 255. The line gives the shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, the staged word, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, the clocks to sense done (`none` if it does not rise inside the bound) and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. The sensing paragraph states both that a strobe with its status bit set leaves its word unwritten and that the strobe at counter 255 stores the last word. Evidence: `OBS-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD LOG seed=<n> word=255 shadow=0x<v> staged=0x<v> sense_done=<0\|1> t_sense=<c\|none> feat=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD-FEAT
|After the control-word sense, for every drawn word (255 included): `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` equals the profile row of the sensed `LC_STATE`, `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` shadows, with the drawn word at its RDL reset value and every other word at its staged value, and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` reads 1 (`hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Debug and Functional Feature Control and Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile: the enable is `~(SIP_DIS \| SYS_DIS)` per state row, and INVALID disables all features; an `LC_STATE` word at its reset value 0xF decodes as INVALID). Word 255 is no input of the profile. A DUT whose feature controls follow the dropped data, or keep a value from before the sense, fails: the drawn word differs from its reset value in the staged image. The probe must read with no X or Z bit, so an unknown probe cannot pass the all-zero INVALID row. Anchor: `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Evidence: `CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD-FEAT PASS seed=<n> word=<w> feat=0x<64b> exp=0x<64b> state=<name> row=<k> sense_done=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CHK-SENSE-STATUS, CHK-PROG-LIFE and CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD-FEAT PASS, and CHK-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD PASS when the drawn control word is 0 to 9. UVM_ERROR == 0. OBS-PROG-TIMEOUT and OBS-SENSE-STATUS-CTRLWORD (word 255) each log one line and are not counted in `checks=<k>`.

*Scope limit*: The test does not claim the report of a program request whose timeout fires, the shadow word of the last word (word 255) after a sense status (logged when drawn, not graded), the program with read back, or the response of a read request (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). Only the new cells are graded: the open owners `sep_efuse_sense_test`, `sep_efuse_image_test`, `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test` and `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test` keep the normal-response sense, the dropped program write and the read timeout.

'''''
